```
1→"""State mutation helpers for triage stage records."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.state_io import utc_now
     6→
     7→from ..stage_queue import cascade_clear_later_confirmations
     8→
     9→
    10→def resolve_reusable_report(
    11→    report: str | None,
    12→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    13→) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    14→    if report:
    15→        return report, False
    16→    if existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
    17→        return existing_stage["report"], True
    18→    return None, False
    19→
    20→
    21→def record_observe_stage(
    22→    stages: dict,
    23→    *,
    24→    report: str,
    25→    issue_count: int,
    26→    cited_ids: list[str],
    27→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    28→    is_reuse: bool,
    29→    assessments: list[dict] | None = None,
    30→) -> list[str]:
    31→    stages["observe"] = {
    32→        "stage": "observe",
    33→        "report": report,
    34→        "cited_ids": cited_ids,
    35→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    36→        "issue_count": issue_count,
    37→    }
    38→    if assessments is not None:
    39→        stages["observe"]["assessments"] = assessments
    40→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    41→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    42→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    43→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
    44→    if not is_reuse:
    45→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    46→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    47→    return cleared
    48→
    49→
    50→def record_organize_stage(
    51→    stages: dict,
    52→    *,
    53→    report: str,
    54→    issue_count: int,
    55→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    56→    is_reuse: bool,
    57→) -> list[str]:
    58→    stages["organize"] = {
    59→        "stage": "organize",
    60→        "report": report,
    61→        "cited_ids": [],
    62→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    63→        "issue_count": issue_count,
    64→    }
    65→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    66→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    67→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    68→    return cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "organize")
    69→
    70→
    71→def record_enrich_stage(
    72→    stages: dict,
    73→    *,
    74→    report: str,
    75→    shallow_count: int,
    76→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    77→    is_reuse: bool,
    78→) -> list[str]:
    79→    stages["enrich"] = {
    80→        "stage": "enrich",
    81→        "report": report,
    82→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    83→        "shallow_count": shallow_count,
    84→    }
    85→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    86→        stages["enrich"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    87→        stages["enrich"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    88→    return cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "enrich")
    89→
    90→
    91→def record_sense_check_stage(
    92→    stages: dict,
    93→    *,
    94→    report: str,
    95→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    96→    is_reuse: bool,
    97→) -> list[str]:
    98→    stages["sense-check"] = {
    99→        "stage": "sense-check",
   100→        "report": report,
   101→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   102→    }
   103→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   104→        stages["sense-check"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   105→        stages["sense-check"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   106→    return cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "sense-check")
   107→
   108→
   109→def record_confirm_existing_completion(
   110→    *,
   111→    stages: dict,
   112→    note: str,
   113→    issue_count: int,
   114→    confirmed_text: str,
   115→) -> None:
   116→    stages["organize"] = {
   117→        "stage": "organize",
   118→        "report": f"[confirmed-existing] {note}",
   119→        "cited_ids": [],
   120→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   121→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   122→        "confirmed_at": utc_now(),
   123→        "confirmed_text": confirmed_text,
   124→        "reused_existing_plan": True,
   125→        "completion_note": note,
   126→    }
   127→
   128→

   132→    "record_observe_stage",
   133→    "record_organize_stage",
   134→    "record_sense_check_stage",
```

```
1→"""Queue/stage mutation helpers for triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import purge_ids
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanModel
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_IDS, TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    12→
    13→from .plan_state_access import ensure_queue_order, ensure_skipped_map
    14→
    15→STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check"]
    16→
    17→
    18→def has_triage_in_queue(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    19→    """Return True when any triage stage IDs are currently queued."""
    20→    order = set(ensure_queue_order(plan))
    21→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    22→
    23→
    24→def clear_triage_stage_skips(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    25→    """Remove skipped markers for triage stages before reinjection."""
    26→    skipped = ensure_skipped_map(plan)
    27→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    28→        skipped.pop(sid, None)
    29→
    30→
    31→def inject_triage_stages(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    32→    """Inject the canonical triage stage IDs at the queue front."""
    33→    order = ensure_queue_order(plan)
    34→    clear_triage_stage_skips(plan)
    35→    remaining = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in TRIAGE_IDS]
    36→    order[:] = [*remaining, *TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS]
    37→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
    38→
    39→
    40→def purge_triage_stage(plan: PlanModel, stage_name: str) -> None:
    41→    """Remove one triage stage workflow item from the plan."""
    42→    purge_ids(plan, [f"triage::{stage_name}"])
    43→
    44→
    45→def cascade_clear_later_confirmations(
    46→    stages: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    47→    from_stage: str,
    48→) -> list[str]:
    49→    """Clear later-stage confirmations after mutating an earlier stage."""
    50→    try:
    51→        idx = STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    52→    except ValueError:
    53→        return []
    54→    cleared: list[str] = []
    55→    for later in STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    56→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    57→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    58→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    59→            cleared.append(later)
    60→    return cleared
    61→
    62→
    63→def print_cascade_clear_feedback(
    64→    cleared: list[str],
    65→    stages: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    66→) -> None:
    67→    """Render confirmation-clearing feedback after stage rewrites."""
    68→    if not cleared:
    69→        return
    70→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    71→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    72→        (
    73→            stage
    74→            for stage in STAGE_ORDER
    75→            if stage in stages and not stages[stage].get("confirmed_at")
    76→        ),
    77→        None,
    78→    )
    79→    if next_unconfirmed:
    80→        print(
    81→            colorize(
    82→                f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan triage --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
    83→                "dim",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→
    87→
    88→__all__ = [
    89→    "STAGE_ORDER",
    90→    "cascade_clear_later_confirmations",
    91→    "has_triage_in_queue",
    92→    "inject_triage_stages",
    93→    "print_cascade_clear_feedback",
    94→    "purge_triage_stage",
    95→]
    96→
```

```
1→"""Observe stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
     9→
    10→from ..stage_queue import (
    11→    has_triage_in_queue,
    12→    inject_triage_stages,
    13→    print_cascade_clear_feedback,
    14→)
    15→from ..lifecycle import TriageLifecycleDeps, ensure_triage_started
    16→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    17→from .flow_helpers import validate_stage_report_length
    18→from .records import record_observe_stage, resolve_reusable_report
    19→from .rendering import _print_observe_report_requirement
    20→
    21→
    22→def cmd_stage_observe(
    23→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    24→    *,
    25→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    26→    has_triage_in_queue_fn=has_triage_in_queue,
    27→    inject_triage_stages_fn=inject_triage_stages,
    28→) -> None:
    29→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes."""
    30→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    31→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    32→
    33→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    34→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    37→
    38→    if not has_triage_in_queue_fn(plan):
    39→        start_outcome = ensure_triage_started(
    40→            plan,
    41→            services=resolved_services,
    42→            state=state,
    43→            attestation=attestation,
    44→            start_message="  Planning mode auto-started (6 stages queued).",
    45→            deps=TriageLifecycleDeps(
    46→                has_triage_in_queue=has_triage_in_queue_fn,
    47→                inject_triage_stages=inject_triage_stages_fn,
    48→            ),
    49→        )
    50→        if start_outcome.status == "blocked":
    51→            return
    52→
    53→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    54→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    55→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
    56→
    57→    report, is_reuse = resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    58→    if not report:
    59→        _print_observe_report_requirement()
    60→        return
    61→
    62→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    63→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
    64→    if issue_count == 0:
    65→        cleared = record_observe_stage(
    66→            stages,
    67→            report=report,
    68→            issue_count=0,
    69→            cited_ids=[],
    70→            existing_stage=existing_stage,
    71→            is_reuse=is_reuse,
    72→        )
    73→        resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    74→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no issues to analyse).", "green"))
    75→        if is_reuse:
    76→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
    77→            if cleared:
    78→                print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
    79→        return
    80→
    81→    if not validate_stage_report_length(
    82→        report=report,
    83→        issue_count=issue_count,
    84→        guidance="  Describe themes, root causes, contradictions, and how issues relate.",
    85→    ):
    86→        return
    87→
    88→    from .evidence_parsing import (
    89→        format_evidence_failures,
    90→        parse_observe_evidence,
    91→        validate_observe_evidence,
    92→    )
    93→
    94→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
    95→    cited = resolved_services.extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
    96→    evidence = parse_observe_evidence(report, valid_ids)
    97→    evidence_failures = validate_observe_evidence(evidence, issue_count)
    98→    blocking = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if failure.blocking]
    99→    advisory = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if not failure.blocking]
   100→    if blocking:
   101→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(blocking, stage_label="observe"), "red"))
   102→        return
   103→    if advisory:
   104→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(advisory, stage_label="observe"), "yellow"))
   105→
   106→    assessments = [
   107→        {
   108→            "hash": entry.issue_hash,
   109→            "verdict": entry.verdict,
   110→            "verdict_reasoning": entry.verdict_reasoning,
   111→            "files_read": entry.files_read,
   112→            "recommendation": entry.recommendation,
   113→        }
   114→        for entry in evidence.entries
   115→    ]
   116→    cleared = record_observe_stage(
   117→        stages,
   118→        report=report,
   119→        issue_count=issue_count,
   120→        cited_ids=sorted(cited),
   121→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   122→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   123→        assessments=assessments,
   124→    )
   125→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   126→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   127→        plan,
   128→        "triage_observe",
   129→        actor="user",
   130→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "cited_ids": sorted(cited), "reuse": is_reuse},
   131→    )
   132→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   133→
   134→    print(colorize(f"  Observe stage recorded: {issue_count} issues analysed.", "green"))
   135→    if is_reuse:
   136→        print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   137→        if cleared:
   138→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    print(colorize("  Now confirm your analysis.", "yellow"))
   142→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe", "dim"))
   143→    print_user_message(
   144→        "Observe recorded. Before confirming — did the subagent"
   145→        " verify every issue with code reads? Check: are there"
   146→        " specific file/line citations in the report, or just"
   147→        " restated issue titles? Each issue needs a verdict:"
   148→        " genuine / false positive / exaggerated / not-worth-it. Don't confirm"
   149→        " until the analysis is backed by actual code evidence."
   150→    )
   151→
   152→_cmd_stage_observe = cmd_stage_observe
   153→
   154→
   155→__all__ = ["_cmd_stage_observe", "cmd_stage_observe"]
   156→
```

```
1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, NotRequired, Required, TypedDict
     6→

    28→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    29→    created_at: str

   100→    superseded_at: str

   109→    strict: float

   119→    issue_id: Required[str]

   127→    name: Required[str]

   133→class TriageStagePayload(TypedDict, total=False):
   134→    """Persisted payload for one triage stage checkpoint."""
   135→
   136→    stage: str
   137→    report: str
   138→    cited_ids: list[str]
   139→    timestamp: str
   140→    issue_count: int
   141→    recurring_dims: list[str]
   142→    confirmed_at: str
   143→    confirmed_text: str
   144→    # Structured reflect contract (populated only for reflect stage)
   145→    disposition_ledger: list[ReflectDisposition]

   147→
   148→
   149→class LastTriageSnapshot(TypedDict, total=False):
   150→    """Archived triage stage state captured when triage is completed."""
   151→
   152→    completed_at: str
   153→    stages: dict[str, TriageStagePayload]
   154→    strategy: str
   155→
   156→
   157→class EpicTriageMeta(TypedDict, total=False):
   158→    """Metadata persisted for the multi-stage triage flow."""
   159→
   160→    triaged_ids: list[str]
   161→    active_triage_issue_ids: list[str]
   162→    dismissed_ids: list[str]
   163→    undispositioned_issue_ids: list[str]
   164→    undispositioned_issue_count: int
   165→    issue_snapshot_hash: str
   166→    strategy_summary: str
   167→    trigger: str
   168→    version: int
   169→    last_run: str
   170→    last_completed_at: str
   171→    triage_stages: dict[str, TriageStagePayload]
   172→    stage_snapshot_hash: str
   173→    stage_refresh_required: bool
   174→    last_triage: LastTriageSnapshot
   175→    triage_defer_state: dict[str, Any]
   176→    triage_force_visible: bool
   177→
   178→
   179→class RefreshState(TypedDict, total=False):
   180→    """Metadata for the post-flight refresh pipeline."""
   181→
   182→    lifecycle_phase: str
   183→    postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count: int
   184→    pending_import_scores: dict[str, Any]
   185→
   186→
   187→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   188→    version: Required[int]
   189→    created: Required[str]
   190→    updated: Required[str]
   191→    queue_order: list[str]
   192→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   193→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   194→    active_cluster: str | None
   195→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   196→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   197→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   198→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
   199→    plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   200→    previous_plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   201→    refresh_state: RefreshState
   202→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
   203→    epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta
   204→    subjective_defer_meta: dict[str, Any]
   205→    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]
   206→    uncommitted_issues: list[str]
   207→    commit_tracking_branch: str | None
   208→    completed_clusters: NotRequired[list[dict[str, Any]]]  # legacy snapshot key
   209→
   210→
   211→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   212→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   213→    now = utc_now()
   214→    return {
   215→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   216→        "created": now,
   217→        "updated": now,
   218→        "queue_order": [],
   219→        "deferred": [],
   220→        "skipped": {},
   221→        "active_cluster": None,
   222→        "overrides": {},
   223→        "clusters": {},
   224→        "superseded": {},
   225→        "promoted_ids": [],
   226→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   227→        "refresh_state": {},
   228→        "execution_log": [],
   229→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   230→        "commit_log": [],
   231→        "uncommitted_issues": [],
   232→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   233→    }
   234→
   235→
   236→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   237→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   238→
   239→    Runtime contract is v8-only. Legacy payloads are upgraded in-place once.
   240→    """
   241→    defaults = empty_plan()
   242→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   243→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   244→    _upgrade_plan_to_v8(plan)
   245→    subjective_defer_meta = plan.get("subjective_defer_meta")
   246→    if isinstance(subjective_defer_meta, dict):
   247→        subjective_defer_meta.pop("force_visible_ids", None)
   248→    epic_triage_meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta")
   249→    if isinstance(epic_triage_meta, dict):
   250→        epic_triage_meta.pop("triage_force_visible", None)
   251→
   252→
   253→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   254→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   255→    return {
   256→        name: cluster
   257→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   258→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   259→    }
   260→
   261→
   262→def tracked_plan_ids(plan: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
   263→    """Collect all issue IDs the plan is actively tracking.
   264→
   265→    Includes queue_order, skipped, overrides, and cluster members/step refs.
   266→    Returns an empty set for None or non-dict plans.
   267→    """
   268→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   269→        return set()
   270→    tracked: set[str] = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   271→    tracked.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   272→    tracked.update(plan.get("overrides", {}).keys())
   273→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   274→        tracked.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   275→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", []):
   276→            if isinstance(step, dict):
   277→                tracked.update(step.get("issue_refs", []))
   278→    return tracked
   279→
   280→
   281→def planned_objective_ids(
   282→    all_objective_ids: set[str],
   283→    plan: dict[str, Any] | None,
   284→) -> set[str]:
   285→    """Return the subset of objective IDs the plan considers active work.
   286→
   287→    Pre-triage (plan tracks nothing): all objective IDs are treated as
   288→    planned work.
   289→
   290→    Once the plan tracks anything, only the intersection with live
   291→    objective IDs counts as planned. This lets stale queue_order entries
   292→    fail closed into postflight/backlog instead of broadening execution
   293→    back out to the entire objective backlog.
   294→    """
   295→    tracked = tracked_plan_ids(plan)
   296→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys()) if isinstance(plan, dict) else set()
   297→    active_tracked = {
   298→        issue_id
   299→        for issue_id in tracked - skipped_ids
   300→        if not any(issue_id.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   301→    }
   302→    if not active_tracked:
   303→        return set(all_objective_ids)
   304→    return all_objective_ids & active_tracked
   305→
   306→
   307→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   308→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   309→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   310→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   311→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   312→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   313→
   314→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   315→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   316→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   317→    if overlap:
   318→        raise ValueError(
   319→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   320→        )
   321→
   322→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   323→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   324→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   325→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] must be an object")
   326→        if "kind" not in entry:
   327→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] missing required key 'kind'")
   328→        kind = entry["kind"]
   329→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   330→            raise ValueError(
   331→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   332→            )
   333→
   334→
   335→__all__ = [
   336→    "ActionStep",
   337→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   338→    "EpicTriageMeta",
   339→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   340→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   341→    "Cluster",
   342→    "CommitRecord",
   343→    "ItemOverride",
   344→    "LastTriageSnapshot",
   345→    "PlanModel",
   346→    "PlanStartScores",
   347→    "RefreshState",
   348→    "SkipEntry",
   349→    "SupersededEntry",
   350→    "TriageStagePayload",
   351→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   352→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",

   354→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   355→    "planned_objective_ids",
   356→    "tracked_plan_ids",
   357→    "triage_clusters",
   358→    "validate_plan",
   359→]
   360→
```

```
1→"""Reflect stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.state_io import utc_now
     9→
    10→from ..display.dashboard import print_reflect_result
    11→from ..stage_queue import cascade_clear_later_confirmations, has_triage_in_queue
    12→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    13→from ..validation.core import (
    14→    ReflectDisposition,
    15→    _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions,
    16→    parse_reflect_dispositions,
    17→)
    18→from ..validation.reflect_accounting import validate_reflect_accounting
    19→from ..validation.stage_policy import auto_confirm_observe_if_attested
    20→from .flow_helpers import validate_stage_report_length
    21→from .records import resolve_reusable_report
    22→from .rendering import _print_reflect_report_requirement
    23→
    24→
    25→def _validate_reflect_submission(
    26→    *,
    27→    report: str,
    28→    plan: dict,
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    stages: dict,
    31→    attestation: str | None,
    32→    services: TriageServices,
    33→) -> tuple[object, int, dict, list[str], set[str], list[str], list[str], list[ReflectDisposition]] | None:
    34→    if "observe" not in stages:
    35→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
    36→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    37→        return None
    38→
    39→    triage_input = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    40→    if not auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
    41→        plan=plan,
    42→        stages=stages,
    43→        attestation=attestation,
    44→        triage_input=triage_input,
    45→        save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
    46→    ):
    47→        return None
    48→
    49→    issue_count = len(triage_input.open_issues)
    50→    if not validate_stage_report_length(
    51→        report=report,
    52→        issue_count=issue_count,
    53→        guidance="  Describe how current issues relate to previously completed work.",
    54→    ):
    55→        return None
    56→
    57→    recurring = services.detect_recurring_patterns(
    58→        triage_input.open_issues,
    59→        triage_input.resolved_issues,
    60→    )
    61→    recurring_dims = sorted(recurring.keys())
    62→    if not _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
    63→        report=report,
    64→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
    65→        recurring=recurring,
    66→    ):
    67→        return None
    68→
    69→    valid_ids = set(triage_input.open_issues.keys())
    70→    accounting_ok, cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = validate_reflect_accounting(
    71→        report=report,
    72→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
    73→    )
    74→    if not accounting_ok:
    75→        return None
    76→
    77→    from .evidence_parsing import (
    78→        format_evidence_failures,
    79→        validate_reflect_skip_evidence,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→    blocking_skips = [
    83→        failure
    84→        for failure in validate_reflect_skip_evidence(report)
    85→        if failure.blocking
    86→    ]
    87→    if blocking_skips:
    88→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(blocking_skips, stage_label="reflect"), "red"))
    89→        return None
    90→
    91→    # Parse structured disposition ledger from Coverage Ledger section
    92→    disposition_ledger = parse_reflect_dispositions(report, valid_ids)
    93→
    94→    return (
    95→        triage_input,
    96→        issue_count,
    97→        recurring,
    98→        recurring_dims,
    99→        cited_ids,
   100→        missing_ids,
   101→        duplicate_ids,
   102→        disposition_ledger,
   103→    )
   104→
   105→
   106→def _persist_reflect_stage(
   107→    *,
   108→    plan: dict,
   109→    meta: dict,
   110→    stages: dict,
   111→    report: str,
   112→    issue_count: int,
   113→    cited_ids: set[str],
   114→    missing_ids: list[str],
   115→    duplicate_ids: list[str],
   116→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   117→    disposition_ledger: list[ReflectDisposition],
   118→    existing_stage: dict | None,
   119→    is_reuse: bool,
   120→    services: TriageServices,
   121→) -> tuple[dict, list[str]]:
   122→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   123→    reflect_stage: dict = {
   124→        "stage": "reflect",
   125→        "report": report,
   126→        "cited_ids": sorted(cited_ids),
   127→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   128→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   129→        "missing_issue_ids": missing_ids,
   130→        "duplicate_issue_ids": duplicate_ids,
   131→        "recurring_dims": recurring_dims,
   132→    }
   133→    if disposition_ledger:
   134→        reflect_stage["disposition_ledger"] = [d.to_dict() for d in disposition_ledger]
   135→    stages["reflect"] = reflect_stage
   136→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   137→        reflect_stage["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   138→        reflect_stage["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   139→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "reflect")
   140→
   141→    services.save_plan(plan)
   142→    services.append_log_entry(
   143→        plan,
   144→        "triage_reflect",
   145→        actor="user",
   146→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "reuse": is_reuse, "recurring_dims": recurring_dims},
   147→    )
   148→    services.save_plan(plan)
   149→    return reflect_stage, cleared
   150→
   151→
   152→def _cmd_stage_reflect(
   153→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   154→    *,
   155→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   156→) -> None:
   157→    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current issues against completed work."""
   158→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   159→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   160→
   161→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   162→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   163→    state = runtime.state
   164→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   165→
   166→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   167→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to reflect on.", "yellow"))
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   171→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   172→    existing_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   173→
   174→    report, is_reuse = resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   175→    if not report:
   176→        _print_reflect_report_requirement()
   177→        return
   178→
   179→    submission = _validate_reflect_submission(
   180→        report=report,
   181→        plan=plan,
   182→        state=state,
   183→        stages=stages,
   184→        attestation=attestation,
   185→        services=resolved_services,
   186→    )
   187→    if submission is None:
   188→        return
   189→    (
   190→        triage_input, issue_count, recurring, recurring_dims,
   191→        cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids, disposition_ledger,
   192→    ) = submission
   193→    reflect_stage, cleared = _persist_reflect_stage(
   194→        plan=plan,
   195→        meta=meta,
   196→        stages=stages,
   197→        report=report,
   198→        issue_count=issue_count,
   199→        cited_ids=cited_ids,
   200→        missing_ids=missing_ids,
   201→        duplicate_ids=duplicate_ids,
   202→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   203→        disposition_ledger=disposition_ledger,
   204→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   205→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   206→        services=resolved_services,
   207→    )
   208→
   209→    print_reflect_result(
   210→        issue_count=issue_count,
   211→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   212→        recurring=recurring,
   213→        report=report,
   214→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   215→        cleared=cleared,
   216→        stages=stages,
   217→    )
   218→
   219→
   220→def cmd_stage_reflect(
   221→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   222→    *,
   223→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   224→) -> None:
   225→    """Public entrypoint for reflect stage recording."""
   226→    _cmd_stage_reflect(args, services=services)
   227→
   228→
   229→__all__ = ["_cmd_stage_reflect", "cmd_stage_reflect"]
   230→
```

```
1→"""Organize stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→
     9→from ..display.dashboard import print_organize_result
    10→from ..completion_flow import count_log_activity_since
    11→from ..review_coverage import open_review_ids_from_state
    12→from ..stage_queue import has_triage_in_queue
    13→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    14→from ..validation.stage_policy import (
    15→    ReflectAutoConfirmDeps,
    16→    auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize,
    17→)
    18→from ..validation.organize_policy import (
    19→    _clusters_enriched_or_error,
    20→    _manual_clusters_or_error,
    21→    _organize_report_or_error,
    22→    _unclustered_review_issues_or_error,
    23→    _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error,
    24→)
    25→from ..validation.core import _require_reflect_stage_for_organize
    26→from .records import record_organize_stage
    27→
    28→
    29→def _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
    30→    *,
    31→    plan: dict,
    32→    stages: dict,
    33→    manual_clusters: list[str],
    34→    open_review_ids: set[str],
    35→    is_reuse: bool,
    36→    attestation: str | None,
    37→) -> bool:
    38→    """Require enough cluster operations after reflect unless explicitly attested."""
    39→    if not open_review_ids:
    40→        return True
    41→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
    42→    if not reflect_ts or is_reuse:
    43→        return True
    44→    activity = count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
    45→    cluster_ops = sum(
    46→        activity.get(key, 0)
    47→        for key in ("cluster_create", "cluster_add", "cluster_update", "cluster_remove")
    48→    )
    49→    min_ops = max(3, len(manual_clusters))
    50→    if cluster_ops >= min_ops:
    51→        return True
    52→    if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 40:
    53→        print(
    54→            colorize(
    55→                f"  Note: only {cluster_ops} cluster op(s) logged (expected {min_ops}+). "
    56→                "Proceeding with attestation override.",
    57→                "yellow",
    58→            )
    59→        )
    60→        return True
    61→    print(
    62→        colorize(
    63→            f"  Cannot organize: only {cluster_ops} cluster operation(s) logged "
    64→            f"since reflect (need {min_ops}+).",
    65→            "red",
    66→        )
    67→    )
    68→    print(
    69→        colorize(
    70→            "  Cluster operations (create/add/update/remove) are logged automatically\n"
    71→            "  when you use the CLI. Did you create clusters, add issues, and enrich them?",
    72→            "dim",
    73→        )
    74→    )
    75→    print(
    76→        colorize(
    77→            '  Override: pass --attestation "reason why fewer ops are expected" (40+ chars).',
    78→            "dim",
    79→        )
    80→    )
    81→    return False
    82→
    83→
    84→def _validate_organize_submission(
    85→    *,
    86→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    87→    plan: dict,
    88→    state: dict,
    89→    stages: dict,
    90→    report: str | None,
    91→    attestation: str | None,
    92→    is_reuse: bool,
    93→    services: TriageServices,
    94→) -> tuple[list[str], str] | None:
    95→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    96→    triage_input = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    97→    if not auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
    98→        args=args,
    99→        plan=plan,
   100→        stages=stages,
   101→        attestation=attestation,
   102→        deps=ReflectAutoConfirmDeps(
   103→            triage_input=triage_input,
   104→            detect_recurring_patterns_fn=services.detect_recurring_patterns,
   105→            save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
   106→        ),
   107→    ):
   108→        return None
   109→
   110→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_or_error(plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids)
   111→    if manual_clusters is None:
   112→        return None
   113→    if not _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan):
   114→        return None
   115→    if not _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan, state):
   116→        return None
   117→    if not _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(
   118→        plan=plan, stages=stages,
   119→    ):
   120→        return None
   121→    if not _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
   122→        plan=plan,
   123→        stages=stages,
   124→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   125→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   126→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   127→        attestation=attestation,
   128→    ):
   129→        return None
   130→
   131→    normalized_report = _organize_report_or_error(report)
   132→    if normalized_report is None:
   133→        return None
   134→
   135→    from .evidence_parsing import (
   136→        format_evidence_failures,
   137→        validate_report_references_clusters,
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    cluster_ref_failures = validate_report_references_clusters(normalized_report, manual_clusters)
   141→    if cluster_ref_failures:
   142→        print(
   143→            colorize(
   144→                format_evidence_failures(cluster_ref_failures, stage_label="organize"),
   145→                "red",
   146→            )
   147→        )
   148→        return None
   149→    return manual_clusters, normalized_report
   150→
   151→
   152→def _persist_organize_stage(
   153→    *,
   154→    plan: dict,
   155→    meta: dict,
   156→    report: str,
   157→    open_review_ids: set[str],
   158→    existing_stage: dict | None,
   159→    is_reuse: bool,
   160→    manual_clusters: list[str],
   161→    services: TriageServices,
   162→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
   163→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   164→    cleared = record_organize_stage(
   165→        stages,
   166→        report=report,
   167→        issue_count=len(open_review_ids),
   168→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   169→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   170→    )
   171→    services.save_plan(plan)
   172→    services.append_log_entry(
   173→        plan,
   174→        "triage_organize",
   175→        actor="user",
   176→        detail={"cluster_count": len(manual_clusters), "reuse": is_reuse},
   177→    )
   178→    services.save_plan(plan)
   179→    return cleared, stages
   180→
   181→
   182→def _cmd_stage_organize(
   183→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   184→    *,
   185→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   186→) -> None:
   187→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment."""
   188→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   189→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   190→
   191→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   192→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   193→
   194→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   195→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
   196→        return
   197→
   198→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   199→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   200→    existing_stage = stages.get("organize")
   201→
   202→    is_reuse = False
   203→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   204→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   205→        is_reuse = True
   206→
   207→    if not _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages):
   208→        return
   209→
   210→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   211→    state = runtime.state
   212→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   213→
   214→    validated = _validate_organize_submission(
   215→        args=args,
   216→        plan=plan,
   217→        state=state,
   218→        stages=stages,
   219→        report=report,
   220→        attestation=attestation,
   221→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   222→        services=resolved_services,
   223→    )
   224→    if validated is None:
   225→        return
   226→    manual_clusters, normalized_report = validated
   227→    cleared, stages = _persist_organize_stage(
   228→        plan=plan,
   229→        meta=meta,
   230→        report=normalized_report,
   231→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   232→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   233→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   234→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   235→        services=resolved_services,
   236→    )
   237→
   238→    print_organize_result(
   239→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   240→        plan=plan,
   241→        report=normalized_report,
   242→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   243→        cleared=cleared,
   244→        stages=stages,
   245→    )
   246→
   247→
   248→def cmd_stage_organize(
   249→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   250→    *,
   251→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   252→) -> None:
   253→    """Public entrypoint for organize stage recording."""
   254→    _cmd_stage_organize(args, services=services)
   255→
   256→
   257→__all__ = [
   258→    "_cmd_stage_organize",
   259→    "cmd_stage_organize",
   260→]
   261→
```

```
1→"""Whole-plan triage API.
     2→
     3→Primary runtime triage orchestration is staged and lives in
     4→``app.commands.plan.triage``. The single-shot ``triage_epics`` runtime
     5→lives here; callers import from ``engine.plan_triage``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass
    13→from typing import Any
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.apply import (
    19→    TriageMutationResult,
    20→    apply_triage_to_plan,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.parsing import (
    23→    ISSUE_ID_RE,
    24→    extract_issue_citations,
    25→    parse_triage_result,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.prompt import (
    28→    _TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    29→    ContradictionNote,
    30→    DismissedIssue,
    31→    TriageInput,
    32→    TriageResult,
    33→    build_triage_prompt,
    34→    collect_triage_input,
    35→)
    36→
    37→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    38→
    39→
    40→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    41→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    42→    real_modes = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    43→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    44→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    45→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    46→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in real_modes:
    47→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    48→                if ts:
    49→                    return str(ts)
    50→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    51→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    52→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    53→    return None
    54→
    55→
    56→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    57→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    58→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    59→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    60→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open issues.
    61→
    62→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    63→    A dimension with both resolved and open issues signals a potential
    64→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    65→    """
    66→
    67→    def _dimension(finding: dict) -> str:
    68→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    69→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    70→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    71→        return ""
    72→
    73→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    74→    for fid, finding in open_issues.items():
    75→        dim = _dimension(finding)
    76→        if dim:
    77→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    78→
    79→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    80→    for fid, finding in resolved_issues.items():
    81→        dim = _dimension(finding)
    82→        if dim:
    83→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    84→
    85→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
    86→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
    87→        recurring[dim] = {
    88→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
    89→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
    90→        }
    91→    return recurring
    92→
    93→
    94→@dataclass
    95→class TriageDeps:
    96→    """Injectable dependencies for the triage engine."""
    97→
    98→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
    99→
   100→
   101→def triage_epics(
   102→    plan: PlanModel,
   103→    state: StateModel,
   104→    *,
   105→    deps: TriageDeps | None = None,
   106→    dry_run: bool = False,
   107→    trigger: str = "manual",
   108→) -> TriageMutationResult:
   109→    """Run legacy whole-plan triage: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
   110→
   111→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   112→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   113→
   114→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   115→    """
   116→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   117→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   118→
   119→    prompt = build_triage_prompt(si)
   120→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   121→
   122→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   123→        result = TriageMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   124→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_issues)} issues"
   125→        return result
   126→
   127→    try:
   128→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   129→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   130→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   131→        logger.error("Cluster triage LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   132→        result = TriageMutationResult()
   133→        result.strategy_summary = f"Triage failed: {exc}"
   134→        return result
   135→
   136→    triage = parse_triage_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
   137→    return apply_triage_to_plan(plan, state, triage, trigger=trigger)
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = [
   141→    "ContradictionNote",
   142→    "DismissedIssue",
   143→    "ISSUE_ID_RE",
   144→    "TriageDeps",
   145→    "TriageInput",
   146→    "TriageMutationResult",
   147→    "TriageResult",
   148→    "apply_triage_to_plan",
   149→    "build_triage_prompt",
   150→    "collect_triage_input",
   151→    "detect_recurring_patterns",
   152→    "extract_issue_citations",
   153→    "last_real_review_timestamp",
   154→    "parse_triage_result",
   155→    "triage_epics",
   156→]
   157→
```

```
1→"""Dismissal helpers for cluster triage plan mutations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from .prompt import TriageResult
     6→
     7→
     8→def _triaged_out_payload(
     9→    *,
    10→    issue_id: str,
    11→    reason: str,
    12→    note: str | None,
    13→    now: str,
    14→    scan_count: int,
    15→) -> dict:
    16→    return {
    17→        "issue_id": issue_id,
    18→        "kind": "triaged_out",
    19→        "reason": reason,
    20→        "note": note,
    21→        "attestation": None,
    22→        "created_at": now,
    23→        "review_after": None,
    24→        "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def dismiss_triage_issues(
    29→    *,
    30→    triage: TriageResult,
    31→    order: list[str],
    32→    skipped: dict,
    33→    now: str,
    34→    version: int,
    35→    scan_count: int,
    36→) -> tuple[list[str], int]:
    37→    """Move triage-dismissed issues out of queue and into skipped metadata."""
    38→    dismissed_ids: list[str] = []
    39→    dismiss_count = 0
    40→    for dismissed in triage.dismissed_issues:
    41→        issue_id = dismissed.issue_id
    42→        dismissed_ids.append(issue_id)
    43→        if issue_id in order:
    44→            order.remove(issue_id)
    45→        skipped[issue_id] = _triaged_out_payload(
    46→            issue_id=issue_id,
    47→            reason=dismissed.reason,
    48→            note=f"Dismissed by epic triage v{version}",
    49→            now=now,
    50→            scan_count=scan_count,
    51→        )
    52→        dismiss_count += 1
    53→
    54→    for epic_data in triage.clusters:
    55→        for issue_id in epic_data.get("dismissed", []):
    56→            if issue_id in dismissed_ids or issue_id not in order:
    57→                continue
    58→            order.remove(issue_id)
    59→            dismissed_ids.append(issue_id)
    60→            skipped[issue_id] = _triaged_out_payload(
    61→                issue_id=issue_id,
    62→                reason=f"Dismissed by epic triage v{version}",
    63→                note=None,
    64→                now=now,
    65→                scan_count=scan_count,
    66→            )
    67→            dismiss_count += 1
    68→
    69→    return dismissed_ids, dismiss_count
    70→
    71→
    72→__all__ = ["dismiss_triage_issues"]
    73→
```

```
1→"""Whole-plan triage API.
     2→
     3→Primary runtime triage orchestration is staged and lives in
     4→``app.commands.plan.triage``. The single-shot ``triage_epics`` runtime
     5→lives here; callers import from ``engine.plan_triage``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass
    13→from typing import Any
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.apply import (
    19→    TriageMutationResult,
    20→    apply_triage_to_plan,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.parsing import (
    23→    ISSUE_ID_RE,
    24→    extract_issue_citations,
    25→    parse_triage_result,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.prompt import (
    28→    _TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    29→    ContradictionNote,
    30→    DismissedIssue,
    31→    TriageInput,
    32→    TriageResult,
    33→    build_triage_prompt,
    34→    collect_triage_input,
    35→)
    36→
    37→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    38→
    39→
    40→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    41→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    42→    real_modes = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    43→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    44→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    45→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    46→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in real_modes:
    47→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    48→                if ts:
    49→                    return str(ts)
    50→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    51→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    52→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    53→    return None
    54→
    55→
    56→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    57→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    58→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    59→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    60→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open issues.
    61→
    62→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    63→    A dimension with both resolved and open issues signals a potential
    64→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    65→    """
    66→
    67→    def _dimension(finding: dict) -> str:
    68→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    69→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    70→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    71→        return ""
    72→
    73→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    74→    for fid, finding in open_issues.items():
    75→        dim = _dimension(finding)
    76→        if dim:
    77→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    78→
    79→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    80→    for fid, finding in resolved_issues.items():
    81→        dim = _dimension(finding)
    82→        if dim:
    83→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    84→
    85→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
    86→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
    87→        recurring[dim] = {
    88→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
    89→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
    90→        }
    91→    return recurring
    92→
    93→
    94→@dataclass
    95→class TriageDeps:
    96→    """Injectable dependencies for the triage engine."""
    97→
    98→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
    99→
   100→
   101→def triage_epics(
   102→    plan: PlanModel,
   103→    state: StateModel,
   104→    *,
   105→    deps: TriageDeps | None = None,
   106→    dry_run: bool = False,
   107→    trigger: str = "manual",
   108→) -> TriageMutationResult:
   109→    """Run legacy whole-plan triage: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
   110→
   111→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   112→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   113→
   114→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   115→    """
   116→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   117→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   118→
   119→    prompt = build_triage_prompt(si)
   120→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   121→
   122→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   123→        result = TriageMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   124→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_issues)} issues"
   125→        return result
   126→
   127→    try:
   128→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   129→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   130→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   131→        logger.error("Cluster triage LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   132→        result = TriageMutationResult()
   133→        result.strategy_summary = f"Triage failed: {exc}"
   134→        return result
   135→
   136→    triage = parse_triage_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
   137→    return apply_triage_to_plan(plan, state, triage, trigger=trigger)
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = [
   141→    "ContradictionNote",
   142→    "DismissedIssue",
   143→    "ISSUE_ID_RE",
   144→    "TriageDeps",
   145→    "TriageInput",
   146→    "TriageMutationResult",
   147→    "TriageResult",
   148→    "apply_triage_to_plan",
   149→    "build_triage_prompt",
   150→    "collect_triage_input",
   151→    "detect_recurring_patterns",
   152→    "extract_issue_citations",
   153→    "last_real_review_timestamp",
   154→    "parse_triage_result",
   155→    "triage_epics",
   156→]
   157→
```

```
1→"""Prompt/data contracts for whole-plan triage orchestration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     9→    Cluster,
    10→    PlanModel,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→    triage_clusters,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass
    18→class TriageInput:
    19→    """All data needed to produce/update triage clusters."""
    20→
    21→    open_issues: dict[str, dict]       # id -> issue (review + concerns)
    22→    mechanical_issues: dict[str, dict]  # id -> issue (non-review, for context)
    23→    existing_clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    24→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
    25→    new_since_last: set[str]             # issue IDs new since last triage
    26→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # issue IDs resolved since last
    27→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior triage
    28→    triage_version: int                  # next version number
    29→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]   # full issue objects for resolved IDs
    30→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last triage
    31→
    32→@dataclass
    33→class DismissedIssue:
    34→    """A issue the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
    35→
    36→    issue_id: str
    37→    reason: str
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class ContradictionNote:
    41→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
    42→
    43→    kept: str
    44→    dismissed: str
    45→    reason: str
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class TriageResult:
    49→    """Parsed and validated LLM triage output."""
    50→
    51→    strategy_summary: str
    52→    epics: list[dict]
    53→    dismissed_issues: list[DismissedIssue] = field(default_factory=list)
    54→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→    priority_rationale: str = ""
    56→
    57→    @property
    58→    def clusters(self) -> list[dict]:
    59→        """Canonical cluster view for triage results."""
    60→        return self.epics
    61→
    62→
    63→def _issue_dimension(issue: dict) -> str:
    64→    detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    65→    if isinstance(detail, dict):
    66→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    67→        if isinstance(dimension, str):
    68→            return dimension
    69→    return ""
    70→
    71→
    72→def _recurring_dimensions(
    73→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    74→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    75→) -> dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]]:
    76→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    77→    for issue_id, issue in open_issues.items():
    78→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
    79→        if dimension:
    80→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dimension, []).append(issue_id)
    81→
    82→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    83→    for issue_id, issue in resolved_issues.items():
    84→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
    85→        if dimension:
    86→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dimension, []).append(issue_id)
    87→
    88→    recurring: dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]] = {}
    89→    for dimension in sorted(set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim)):
    90→        recurring[dimension] = {
    91→            "open": sorted(open_by_dim[dimension]),
    92→            "resolved": sorted(resolved_by_dim[dimension]),
    93→        }
    94→    return recurring
    95→
    96→
    97→def _split_open_issue_buckets(
    98→    issues: dict[str, dict],
    99→) -> tuple[dict[str, dict], dict[str, dict]]:
   100→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   101→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   102→    for issue_id, issue in issues.items():
   103→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   104→            continue
   105→        if issue.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   106→            open_review[issue_id] = issue
   107→            continue
   108→        open_mechanical[issue_id] = issue
   109→    return open_review, open_mechanical
   110→
   111→
   112→def _recent_completed_clusters(meta: dict, plan: PlanModel) -> list[dict]:
   113→    last_completed = meta.get("last_completed_at", "")
   114→    all_completed: list[dict] = plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
   115→    if not last_completed:
   116→        return list(all_completed)
   117→    return [
   118→        cluster for cluster in all_completed
   119→        if cluster.get("completed_at", "") > last_completed
   120→    ]
   121→
   122→
   123→def collect_triage_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> TriageInput:
   124→    """Gather all data needed for the triage LLM prompt."""
   125→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   126→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   127→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   128→    epics = triage_clusters(plan)
   129→    open_review, open_mechanical = _split_open_issue_buckets(issues)
   130→
   131→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   132→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
   133→    new_since = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   134→    resolved_since = triaged_ids - current_review_ids
   135→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
   136→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   137→
   138→    # Resolved issue objects (for REFLECT stage)
   139→    resolved_issue_objs = {
   140→        fid: issues[fid] for fid in resolved_since if fid in issues
   141→    }
   142→
   143→    return TriageInput(
   144→        open_issues=open_review,
   145→        mechanical_issues=open_mechanical,
   146→        existing_clusters=dict(epics),
   147→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   148→        new_since_last=new_since,
   149→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   150→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   151→        triage_version=version,
   152→        resolved_issues=resolved_issue_objs,
   153→        completed_clusters=_recent_completed_clusters(meta, plan),
   154→    )
   155→
   156→_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   157→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Produce a coherent
   158→prioritized strategy for all open review issues.
   159→
   160→Your plan should:
   161→- Cluster issues by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   162→- Give each cluster a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   163→- Order clusters by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   164→- Dismiss issues that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   165→- Mark which clusters are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   166→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   167→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all issues coherently
   168→
   169→Available directions for clusters: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   170→
   171→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   172→- `desloppify plan queue` — view the execution queue in priority order
   173→- `desloppify backlog` — inspect broader open work outside the execution queue
   174→- `desloppify plan focus <name>` — focus the queue on one cluster
   175→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   176→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   177→- `desloppify plan resolve <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   178→- `desloppify plan reorder <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   179→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   180→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   181→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review issues
   182→
   183→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Issues not assigned to any cluster
   184→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed issues will be
   185→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   186→
   187→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   188→{
   189→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   190→  "clusters": [
   191→    {
   192→      "name": "slug-name",
   193→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   194→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   195→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   196→      "issue_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   197→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   198→      "agent_safe": true,
   199→      "dependency_order": 1,
   200→      "action_steps": [
   201→        {
   202→          "title": "Short imperative step title",
   203→          "detail": "Concrete implementation detail with file paths/locations",
   204→          "issue_refs": ["id1"]
   205→        }
   206→      ],
   207→      "status": "pending"
   208→    }
   209→  ],
   210→  "dismissed_issues": [
   211→    {"issue_id": "id", "reason": "why this issue doesn't make sense"}
   212→  ],
   213→  "contradiction_notes": [
   214→    {"kept": "issue_id", "dismissed": "issue_id", "reason": "why"}
   215→  ],
   216→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   217→}
   218→"""
   219→
   220→def _append_existing_clusters_section(parts: list[str], clusters: dict[str, Cluster]) -> None:
   221→    if not clusters:
   222→        return
   223→    parts.append("## Existing clusters")
   224→    for name, cluster in sorted(clusters.items()):
   225→        status = cluster.get("status", "pending")
   226→        thesis = cluster.get("thesis", "")
   227→        direction = cluster.get("direction", "")
   228→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   229→        parts.append(
   230→            f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   231→            f"\n  Issues: {', '.join(issue_ids[:10])}"
   232→            f"{'...' if len(issue_ids) > 10 else ''}"
   233→        )
   234→    parts.append("")
   235→
   236→
   237→def _append_changed_issue_section(
   238→    parts: list[str],
   239→    *,
   240→    title: str,
   241→    issue_ids: set[str],
   242→    issues: dict[str, dict] | None = None,
   243→) -> None:
   244→    if not issue_ids:
   245→        return
   246→    parts.append(f"## {title} ({len(issue_ids)})")
   247→    for issue_id in sorted(issue_ids):
   248→        if issues is None:
   249→            parts.append(f"- {issue_id}")
   250→            continue
   251→        issue = issues.get(issue_id, {})
   252→        parts.append(f"- {issue_id}: {issue.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   253→    parts.append("")
   254→
   255→
   256→def _append_resolved_issue_context(parts: list[str], resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
   257→    if not resolved_issues:
   258→        return
   259→    parts.append(
   260→        "## Resolved review issues available for recurrence context "
   261→        f"({len(resolved_issues)})"
   262→    )
   263→    resolved_ids = sorted(resolved_issues)
   264→    for issue_id in resolved_ids[:30]:
   265→        issue = resolved_issues.get(issue_id, {})
   266→        summary = str(issue.get("summary", "(no summary)"))
   267→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
   268→        dim_suffix = f" [{dimension}]" if dimension else ""
   269→        parts.append(f"- {issue_id}{dim_suffix}: {summary}")
   270→    if len(resolved_ids) > 30:
   271→        parts.append(f"- ... and {len(resolved_ids) - 30} more resolved issues")
   272→    parts.append("")
   273→
   274→
   275→def _append_completed_clusters_section(parts: list[str], completed_clusters: list[dict]) -> None:
   276→    if not completed_clusters:
   277→        return
   278→    parts.append(
   279→        "## Completed clusters since last triage "
   280→        f"({len(completed_clusters)})"
   281→    )
   282→    completed = sorted(
   283→        completed_clusters,
   284→        key=lambda cluster: str(cluster.get("completed_at", "")),
   285→        reverse=True,
   286→    )
   287→    for cluster in completed[:10]:
   288→        name = str(cluster.get("name", "(unnamed cluster)"))
   289→        thesis = str(cluster.get("thesis") or cluster.get("description") or "")
   290→        completed_at = str(cluster.get("completed_at", ""))
   291→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   292→        issue_count = len(issue_ids) if isinstance(issue_ids, list) else 0
   293→        parts.append(
   294→            f"- {name}: {thesis} (issues: {issue_count}, completed_at: {completed_at})"
   295→        )
   296→    if len(completed) > 10:
   297→        parts.append(f"- ... and {len(completed) - 10} more completed clusters")
   298→    parts.append("")
   299→
   300→
   301→def _append_recurring_dimensions_section(
   302→    parts: list[str],
   303→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
   304→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
   305→) -> None:
   306→    recurring = _recurring_dimensions(open_issues, resolved_issues)
   307→    if not recurring:
   308→        return
   309→    parts.append(
   310→        "## Potential recurring dimensions (resolved issues still have open peers)"
   311→    )
   312→    for dimension, bucket in recurring.items():
   313→        open_count = len(bucket["open"])
   314→        resolved_count = len(bucket["resolved"])
   315→        parts.append(
   316→            f"- {dimension}: {open_count} open / {resolved_count} recently resolved"
   317→        )
   318→    parts.append("")
   319→
   320→
   321→def _append_open_review_issues_section(parts: list[str], open_issues: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
   322→    parts.append(f"## All open review issues ({len(open_issues)})")
   323→    for issue_id, issue in sorted(open_issues.items()):
   324→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   325→        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   326→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   327→        confidence = issue.get("confidence", "medium")
   328→        file_path = issue.get("file", "")
   329→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
   330→        parts.append(f"- [{confidence}] {issue_id}")
   331→        parts.append(f"  File: {file_path}")
   332→        if dimension:
   333→            parts.append(f"  Dimension: {dimension}")
   334→        parts.append(f"  Summary: {summary}")
   335→        if suggestion:
   336→            parts.append(f"  Suggestion: {suggestion}")
   337→    parts.append("")
   338→
   339→
   340→def _append_dimension_scores_section(parts: list[str], dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   341→    if not dimension_scores:
   342→        return
   343→    parts.append("## Dimension scores (context)")
   344→    for name, data in sorted(dimension_scores.items()):
   345→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   346→            continue
   347→        score = data.get("score", "?")
   348→        strict = data.get("strict", score)
   349→        issues = data.get("failing", 0)
   350→        parts.append(f"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)")
   351→    parts.append("")
   352→
   353→
   354→def _append_previously_dismissed_section(parts: list[str], dismissed_ids: list[str]) -> None:
   355→    if not dismissed_ids:
   356→        return
   357→    parts.append(f"## Previously dismissed ({len(dismissed_ids)})")
   358→    parts.append("Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.")
   359→    for issue_id in dismissed_ids:
   360→        parts.append(f"- {issue_id}")
   361→    parts.append("")
   362→
   363→
   364→def build_triage_prompt(si: TriageInput) -> str:
   365→    """Build the user-facing prompt content with all issue data."""
   366→    parts: list[str] = []
   367→    _append_existing_clusters_section(parts, si.existing_clusters)
   368→    _append_changed_issue_section(
   369→        parts,
   370→        title="New issues since last triage",
   371→        issue_ids=si.new_since_last,
   372→        issues=si.open_issues,
   373→    )
   374→    _append_changed_issue_section(
   375→        parts,
   376→        title="Resolved since last triage",
   377→        issue_ids=si.resolved_since_last,
   378→    )
   379→    _append_resolved_issue_context(parts, si.resolved_issues)
   380→    _append_completed_clusters_section(parts, si.completed_clusters)
   381→    _append_recurring_dimensions_section(parts, si.open_issues, si.resolved_issues)
   382→    _append_open_review_issues_section(parts, si.open_issues)
   383→    _append_dimension_scores_section(parts, si.dimension_scores)
   384→    _append_previously_dismissed_section(parts, si.previously_dismissed)
   385→    return "\n".join(parts)
   386→
   387→__all__ = [
   388→    "_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT",
   389→    "ContradictionNote",
   390→    "DismissedIssue",
   391→    "TriageInput",
   392→    "TriageResult",
   393→    "build_triage_prompt",
   394→    "collect_triage_input",
   395→]
   396→
```

```
1→"""Prompt/data contracts for whole-plan triage orchestration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     9→    Cluster,
    10→    PlanModel,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→    triage_clusters,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass
    18→class TriageInput:
    19→    """All data needed to produce/update triage clusters."""
    20→
    21→    open_issues: dict[str, dict]       # id -> issue (review + concerns)
    22→    mechanical_issues: dict[str, dict]  # id -> issue (non-review, for context)
    23→    existing_clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    24→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
    25→    new_since_last: set[str]             # issue IDs new since last triage
    26→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # issue IDs resolved since last
    27→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior triage
    28→    triage_version: int                  # next version number
    29→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]   # full issue objects for resolved IDs
    30→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last triage
    31→
    32→@dataclass
    33→class DismissedIssue:
    34→    """A issue the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
    35→
    36→    issue_id: str
    37→    reason: str
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class ContradictionNote:
    41→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
    42→
    43→    kept: str
    44→    dismissed: str
    45→    reason: str
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class TriageResult:
    49→    """Parsed and validated LLM triage output."""
    50→
    51→    strategy_summary: str
    52→    epics: list[dict]
    53→    dismissed_issues: list[DismissedIssue] = field(default_factory=list)
    54→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→    priority_rationale: str = ""
    56→
    57→    @property
    58→    def clusters(self) -> list[dict]:
    59→        """Canonical cluster view for triage results."""
    60→        return self.epics
    61→
    62→
    63→def _issue_dimension(issue: dict) -> str:
    64→    detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    65→    if isinstance(detail, dict):
    66→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    67→        if isinstance(dimension, str):
    68→            return dimension
    69→    return ""
    70→
    71→
    72→def _recurring_dimensions(
    73→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    74→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    75→) -> dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]]:
    76→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    77→    for issue_id, issue in open_issues.items():
    78→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
    79→        if dimension:
    80→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dimension, []).append(issue_id)
    81→
    82→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    83→    for issue_id, issue in resolved_issues.items():
    84→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
    85→        if dimension:
    86→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dimension, []).append(issue_id)
    87→
    88→    recurring: dict[str, dict[str, list[str]]] = {}
    89→    for dimension in sorted(set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim)):
    90→        recurring[dimension] = {
    91→            "open": sorted(open_by_dim[dimension]),
    92→            "resolved": sorted(resolved_by_dim[dimension]),
    93→        }
    94→    return recurring
    95→
    96→
    97→def _split_open_issue_buckets(
    98→    issues: dict[str, dict],
    99→) -> tuple[dict[str, dict], dict[str, dict]]:
   100→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   101→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   102→    for issue_id, issue in issues.items():
   103→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   104→            continue
   105→        if issue.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   106→            open_review[issue_id] = issue
   107→            continue
   108→        open_mechanical[issue_id] = issue
   109→    return open_review, open_mechanical
   110→
   111→
   112→def _recent_completed_clusters(meta: dict, plan: PlanModel) -> list[dict]:
   113→    last_completed = meta.get("last_completed_at", "")
   114→    all_completed: list[dict] = plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
   115→    if not last_completed:
   116→        return list(all_completed)
   117→    return [
   118→        cluster for cluster in all_completed
   119→        if cluster.get("completed_at", "") > last_completed
   120→    ]
   121→
   122→
   123→def collect_triage_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> TriageInput:
   124→    """Gather all data needed for the triage LLM prompt."""
   125→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   126→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   127→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   128→    epics = triage_clusters(plan)
   129→    open_review, open_mechanical = _split_open_issue_buckets(issues)
   130→
   131→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   132→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
   133→    new_since = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   134→    resolved_since = triaged_ids - current_review_ids
   135→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
   136→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   137→
   138→    # Resolved issue objects (for REFLECT stage)
   139→    resolved_issue_objs = {
   140→        fid: issues[fid] for fid in resolved_since if fid in issues
   141→    }
   142→
   143→    return TriageInput(
   144→        open_issues=open_review,
   145→        mechanical_issues=open_mechanical,
   146→        existing_clusters=dict(epics),
   147→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   148→        new_since_last=new_since,
   149→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   150→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
```
