1→"""Observe stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
     9→
    10→from ..stage_queue import (
    11→    has_triage_in_queue,
    12→    inject_triage_stages,
    13→    print_cascade_clear_feedback,
    14→)
    15→from ..lifecycle import TriageLifecycleDeps, ensure_triage_started
    16→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    17→from .flow_helpers import validate_stage_report_length
    18→from .records import record_observe_stage, resolve_reusable_report
    19→from .rendering import _print_observe_report_requirement
    20→
    21→
    22→def cmd_stage_observe(
    23→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    24→    *,
    25→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    26→    has_triage_in_queue_fn=has_triage_in_queue,
    27→    inject_triage_stages_fn=inject_triage_stages,
    28→) -> None:
    29→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes."""
    30→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    31→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    32→
    33→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    34→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    37→
    38→    if not has_triage_in_queue_fn(plan):
    39→        start_outcome = ensure_triage_started(
    40→            plan,
    41→            services=resolved_services,
    42→            state=state,
    43→            attestation=attestation,
    44→            start_message="  Planning mode auto-started (6 stages queued).",
    45→            deps=TriageLifecycleDeps(
    46→                has_triage_in_queue=has_triage_in_queue_fn,
    47→                inject_triage_stages=inject_triage_stages_fn,
    48→            ),
    49→        )
    50→        if start_outcome.status == "blocked":
    51→            return
    52→
    53→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    54→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    55→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
    56→
    57→    report, is_reuse = resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    58→    if not report:
    59→        _print_observe_report_requirement()
    60→        return
    61→
    62→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    63→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
    64→    if issue_count == 0:
    65→        cleared = record_observe_stage(
    66→            stages,
    67→            report=report,
    68→            issue_count=0,
    69→            cited_ids=[],
    70→            existing_stage=existing_stage,
    71→            is_reuse=is_reuse,
    72→        )
    73→        resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    74→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no issues to analyse).", "green"))
    75→        if is_reuse:
    76→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
    77→            if cleared:
    78→                print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
    79→        return
    80→
    81→    if not validate_stage_report_length(
    82→        report=report,
    83→        issue_count=issue_count,
    84→        guidance="  Describe themes, root causes, contradictions, and how issues relate.",
    85→    ):
    86→        return
    87→
    88→    from .evidence_parsing import (
    89→        format_evidence_failures,
    90→        parse_observe_evidence,
    91→        validate_observe_evidence,
    92→    )
    93→
    94→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
    95→    cited = resolved_services.extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
    96→    evidence = parse_observe_evidence(report, valid_ids)
    97→    evidence_failures = validate_observe_evidence(evidence, issue_count)
    98→    blocking = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if failure.blocking]
    99→    advisory = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if not failure.blocking]
   100→    if blocking:
   101→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(blocking, stage_label="observe"), "red"))
   102→        return
   103→    if advisory:
   104→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(advisory, stage_label="observe"), "yellow"))
   105→
   106→    assessments = [
   107→        {
   108→            "hash": entry.issue_hash,
   109→            "verdict": entry.verdict,
   110→            "verdict_reasoning": entry.verdict_reasoning,
   111→            "files_read": entry.files_read,
   112→            "recommendation": entry.recommendation,
   113→        }
   114→        for entry in evidence.entries
   115→    ]
   116→    cleared = record_observe_stage(
   117→        stages,
   118→        report=report,
   119→        issue_count=issue_count,
   120→        cited_ids=sorted(cited),
   121→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   122→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   123→        assessments=assessments,
   124→    )
   125→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   126→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   127→        plan,
   128→        "triage_observe",
   129→        actor="user",
   130→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "cited_ids": sorted(cited), "reuse": is_reuse},
   131→    )
   132→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   133→
   134→    print(colorize(f"  Observe stage recorded: {issue_count} issues analysed.", "green"))
   135→    if is_reuse:
   136→        print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   137→        if cleared:
   138→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    print(colorize("  Now confirm your analysis.", "yellow"))
   142→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe", "dim"))
   143→    print_user_message(
   144→        "Observe recorded. Before confirming — did the subagent"
   145→        " verify every issue with code reads? Check: are there"
   146→        " specific file/line citations in the report, or just"
   147→        " restated issue titles? Each issue needs a verdict:"
   148→        " genuine / false positive / exaggerated / not-worth-it. Don't confirm"
   149→        " until the analysis is backed by actual code evidence."
   150→    )
   151→
   152→_cmd_stage_observe = cmd_stage_observe
   153→
   154→
   155→__all__ = ["_cmd_stage_observe", "cmd_stage_observe"]
   156→

1→"""Organize stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→
     9→from ..display.dashboard import print_organize_result
    10→from ..completion_flow import count_log_activity_since
    11→from ..review_coverage import open_review_ids_from_state
    12→from ..stage_queue import has_triage_in_queue
    13→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    14→from ..validation.stage_policy import (
    15→    ReflectAutoConfirmDeps,
    16→    auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize,
    17→)
    18→from ..validation.organize_policy import (
    19→    _clusters_enriched_or_error,
    20→    _manual_clusters_or_error,
    21→    _organize_report_or_error,
    22→    _unclustered_review_issues_or_error,
    23→    _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error,
    24→)
    25→from ..validation.core import _require_reflect_stage_for_organize
    26→from .records import record_organize_stage
    27→
    28→
    29→def _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
    30→    *,
    31→    plan: dict,
    32→    stages: dict,
    33→    manual_clusters: list[str],
    34→    open_review_ids: set[str],
    35→    is_reuse: bool,
    36→    attestation: str | None,
    37→) -> bool:
    38→    """Require enough cluster operations after reflect unless explicitly attested."""
    39→    if not open_review_ids:
    40→        return True
    41→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
    42→    if not reflect_ts or is_reuse:
    43→        return True
    44→    activity = count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
    45→    cluster_ops = sum(
    46→        activity.get(key, 0)
    47→        for key in ("cluster_create", "cluster_add", "cluster_update", "cluster_remove")
    48→    )
    49→    min_ops = max(3, len(manual_clusters))
    50→    if cluster_ops >= min_ops:
    51→        return True
    52→    if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 40:
    53→        print(
    54→            colorize(
    55→                f"  Note: only {cluster_ops} cluster op(s) logged (expected {min_ops}+). "
    56→                "Proceeding with attestation override.",
    57→                "yellow",
    58→            )
    59→        )
    60→        return True
    61→    print(
    62→        colorize(
    63→            f"  Cannot organize: only {cluster_ops} cluster operation(s) logged "
    64→            f"since reflect (need {min_ops}+).",
    65→            "red",
    66→        )
    67→    )
    68→    print(
    69→        colorize(
    70→            "  Cluster operations (create/add/update/remove) are logged automatically\n"
    71→            "  when you use the CLI. Did you create clusters, add issues, and enrich them?",
    72→            "dim",
    73→        )
    74→    )
    75→    print(
    76→        colorize(
    77→            '  Override: pass --attestation "reason why fewer ops are expected" (40+ chars).',
    78→            "dim",
    79→        )
    80→    )
    81→    return False
    82→
    83→
    84→def _validate_organize_submission(
    85→    *,
    86→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    87→    plan: dict,
    88→    state: dict,
    89→    stages: dict,
    90→    report: str | None,
    91→    attestation: str | None,
    92→    is_reuse: bool,
    93→    services: TriageServices,
    94→) -> tuple[list[str], str] | None:
    95→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    96→    triage_input = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    97→    if not auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
    98→        args=args,
    99→        plan=plan,
   100→        stages=stages,
   101→        attestation=attestation,
   102→        deps=ReflectAutoConfirmDeps(
   103→            triage_input=triage_input,
   104→            detect_recurring_patterns_fn=services.detect_recurring_patterns,
   105→            save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
   106→        ),
   107→    ):
   108→        return None
   109→
   110→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_or_error(plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids)
   111→    if manual_clusters is None:
   112→        return None
   113→    if not _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan):
   114→        return None
   115→    if not _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan, state):
   116→        return None
   117→    if not _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(
   118→        plan=plan, stages=stages,
   119→    ):
   120→        return None
   121→    if not _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
   122→        plan=plan,
   123→        stages=stages,
   124→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   125→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   126→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   127→        attestation=attestation,
   128→    ):
   129→        return None
   130→
   131→    normalized_report = _organize_report_or_error(report)
   132→    if normalized_report is None:
   133→        return None
   134→
   135→    from .evidence_parsing import (
   136→        format_evidence_failures,
   137→        validate_report_references_clusters,
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    cluster_ref_failures = validate_report_references_clusters(normalized_report, manual_clusters)
   141→    if cluster_ref_failures:
   142→        print(
   143→            colorize(
   144→                format_evidence_failures(cluster_ref_failures, stage_label="organize"),
   145→                "red",
   146→            )
   147→        )
   148→        return None
   149→    return manual_clusters, normalized_report
   150→
   151→
   152→def _persist_organize_stage(
   153→    *,
   154→    plan: dict,
   155→    meta: dict,
   156→    report: str,
   157→    open_review_ids: set[str],
   158→    existing_stage: dict | None,
   159→    is_reuse: bool,
   160→    manual_clusters: list[str],
   161→    services: TriageServices,
   162→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
   163→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   164→    cleared = record_organize_stage(
   165→        stages,
   166→        report=report,
   167→        issue_count=len(open_review_ids),
   168→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   169→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   170→    )
   171→    services.save_plan(plan)
   172→    services.append_log_entry(
   173→        plan,
   174→        "triage_organize",
   175→        actor="user",
   176→        detail={"cluster_count": len(manual_clusters), "reuse": is_reuse},
   177→    )
   178→    services.save_plan(plan)
   179→    return cleared, stages
   180→
   181→
   182→def _cmd_stage_organize(
   183→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   184→    *,
   185→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   186→) -> None:
   187→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment."""
   188→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   189→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   190→
   191→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   192→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   193→
   194→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   195→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
   196→        return
   197→
   198→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   199→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   200→    existing_stage = stages.get("organize")
   201→
   202→    is_reuse = False
   203→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   204→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   205→        is_reuse = True
   206→
   207→    if not _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages):
   208→        return
   209→
   210→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   211→    state = runtime.state
   212→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   213→
   214→    validated = _validate_organize_submission(
   215→        args=args,
   216→        plan=plan,
   217→        state=state,
   218→        stages=stages,
   219→        report=report,
   220→        attestation=attestation,
   221→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   222→        services=resolved_services,
   223→    )
   224→    if validated is None:
   225→        return
   226→    manual_clusters, normalized_report = validated
   227→    cleared, stages = _persist_organize_stage(
   228→        plan=plan,
   229→        meta=meta,
   230→        report=normalized_report,
   231→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   232→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   233→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   234→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   235→        services=resolved_services,
   236→    )
   237→
   238→    print_organize_result(
   239→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   240→        plan=plan,
   241→        report=normalized_report,
   242→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   243→        cleared=cleared,
   244→        stages=stages,
   245→    )
   246→
   247→
   248→def cmd_stage_organize(
   249→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   250→    *,
   251→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   252→) -> None:
   253→    """Public entrypoint for organize stage recording."""
   254→    _cmd_stage_organize(args, services=services)
   255→
   256→
   257→__all__ = [
   258→    "_cmd_stage_organize",
   259→    "cmd_stage_organize",
   260→]
   261→

    43→def _print_completion_coverage_warning(*, organized: int, total: int) -> None:
    44→    """Print warnings for incomplete cluster coverage at completion time."""
    45→    if total <= 0:
    46→        return

    49→        print(colorize(f"  {total} issues are waiting.", "dim"))
    50→        return
    51→    if organized < total:
    52→        remaining = total - organized
    53→        print(
    54→            colorize(

    56→                "yellow",
    57→            )
    58→        )

    83→    if missing:
    84→        print(

    87→                "yellow",

    89→        )
    90→        for issue_id in missing[:5]:

    92→        if len(missing) > 5:
    93→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(missing) - 5} more", "dim"))
    94→    return missing

   153→def _cmd_triage_complete(

   172→    review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   173→
   174→    # Organize gate
   175→    if not _require_organize_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages):
   176→        return
   177→    if not _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(

   179→        attestation=attestation, save_plan_fn=resolved_services.save_plan,
   180→    ):
   181→        return
   182→
   183→    # Enrich gate — compute underspecified steps once, share across require + confirm
   184→    underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
   185→    if not _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(

   198→    if not _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(

   211→        return

   213→    organized, total, _clusters = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
   214→    if total > 0 and organized == 0:
   215→        _print_completion_coverage_warning(organized=organized, total=total)
   216→        return

   218→

   222→    if not _completion_strategy_valid(strategy):

   224→
   225→    # Show summary
   226→    _print_complete_summary(plan, stages)
   227→
   228→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
   229→
   230→    _print_completion_jump_back_guidance()

   251→def _cmd_confirm_existing(
   252→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   253→    *,

   256→    """Fast-track: confirm existing plan structure is still valid."""

   258→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)

1→"""Shared triage lifecycle helpers used by command and runner entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from typing import Literal
     8→
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanModel
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    11→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    12→    TriageStartDecision,
    13→    decide_triage_start,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel
    17→
    18→from .review_coverage import ensure_active_triage_issue_ids
    19→from .services import TriageServices
    20→from .stage_queue import has_triage_in_queue, inject_triage_stages
    21→
    22→TriageStartStatus = Literal["started", "blocked", "already_active"]
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class TriageStartOutcome:
    27→    """Lifecycle result for triage-start attempts."""
    28→
    29→    status: TriageStartStatus
    30→    reason: str
    31→
    32→
    33→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    34→class TriageLifecycleDeps:
    35→    """Dependency seams for triage lifecycle starts (for tests/callers)."""
    36→
    37→    has_triage_in_queue: Callable[[PlanModel], bool] = has_triage_in_queue
    38→    inject_triage_stages: Callable[[PlanModel], None] = inject_triage_stages
    39→    decide_triage_start: Callable[..., TriageStartDecision] = decide_triage_start
    40→    colorize: Callable[[str, str], str] = colorize
    41→
    42→
    43→def _print_triage_start_block(reason: str, *, deps: TriageLifecycleDeps) -> None:
    44→    """Render reason-specific guard guidance for triage auto-start attempts."""
    45→    if reason == "unfinished_triage_stage_records":
    46→        print(
    47→            deps.colorize(
    48→                "  Cannot start triage while previous stage reports are still unconfirmed.",
    49→                "red",
    50→            )
    51→        )
    52→        print(
    53→            deps.colorize(
    54→                "  Confirm or complete the current triage stages first, or pass --attestation "
    55→                "(30+ chars) to override explicitly.",
    56→                "dim",
    57→            )
    58→        )
    59→        return
    60→
    61→    print(deps.colorize("  Cannot start triage while objective backlog is still open.", "red"))
    62→    print(
    63→        deps.colorize(
    64→            "  Finish current objective work first, or pass --attestation "
    65→            "(30+ chars) to override this guard explicitly.",
    66→            "dim",
    67→        )
    68→    )
    69→
    70→
    71→def ensure_triage_started(
    72→    plan: PlanModel,
    73→    *,
    74→    services: TriageServices,
    75→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    76→    attestation: str | None = None,
    77→    log_action: str | None = None,
    78→    log_actor: str = "system",
    79→    log_detail: Mapping[str, object] | None = None,
    80→    start_message: str | None = None,
    81→    start_message_style: str = "cyan",
    82→    deps: TriageLifecycleDeps | None = None,
    83→) -> TriageStartOutcome:
    84→    """Ensure triage stages are injected or return a blocked/already-active outcome."""
    85→    resolved_deps = deps or TriageLifecycleDeps()
    86→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    87→
    88→    if resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    89→        if state is not None:
    90→            ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    91→            services.save_plan(plan)
    92→        meta.pop("triage_start_blocked", None)
    93→        return TriageStartOutcome(status="already_active", reason="already_active")
    94→
    95→    decision = resolved_deps.decide_triage_start(
    96→        plan,
    97→        state,
    98→        explicit_start=True,
    99→        attested_override=bool(attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 30),
   100→    )
   101→    if decision.action == "defer":
   102→        meta["triage_start_blocked"] = decision.reason
   103→        services.save_plan(plan)
   104→        _print_triage_start_block(decision.reason, deps=resolved_deps)
   105→        return TriageStartOutcome(status="blocked", reason=decision.reason)
   106→
   107→    resolved_deps.inject_triage_stages(plan)
   108→    meta.pop("triage_start_blocked", None)
   109→    meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   110→    if state is not None:
   111→        ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   112→
   113→    if log_action:
   114→        detail = dict(log_detail or {})
   115→        detail.setdefault("injected_stage_ids", list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS))
   116→        services.append_log_entry(
   117→            plan,
   118→            log_action,
   119→            actor=log_actor,
   120→            detail=detail,
   121→        )
   122→
   123→    services.save_plan(plan)
   124→    if start_message:
   125→        print(
   126→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   127→                start_message,
   128→                start_message_style,
   129→            )
   130→        )
   131→    return TriageStartOutcome(status="started", reason=decision.reason)
   132→
   133→
   134→__all__ = [
   135→    "TriageLifecycleDeps",
   136→    "TriageStartOutcome",
   137→    "ensure_triage_started",
   138→]
   139→

1→"""Triage sync — inject/prune triage stage IDs based on review issue changes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
     7→    TRIAGE_IDS,
     8→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
     9→    QueueSyncResult,
    10→    confirmed_triage_stage_names,
    11→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix,
    12→    recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→
    18→from .defer_policy import (
    19→    DeferEscalationOptions,
    20→    DeferUpdateOptions,
    21→    should_escalate_defer_state,
    22→    update_defer_state,
    23→)
    24→from .triage_start_policy import decide_triage_start
    25→
    26→[REDACTED]
    27→_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD = "deferred_review_ids"
    28→[REDACTED]
    29→
    30→
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→# Helpers
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
    36→    state: StateModel,
    37→    meta: dict,
    38→) -> set[str]:
    39→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
    40→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    41→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids
    42→
    43→
    44→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
    45→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
    46→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    47→        while sid in order:
    48→            order.remove(sid)
    49→
    50→
    51→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
    52→    order: list[str],
    53→    confirmed: set[str],
    54→    *,
    55→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    56→) -> list[str]:
    57→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
    58→
    59→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
    60→    Returns list of injected stage IDs.
    61→    """
    62→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
    63→    existing = set(order)
    64→    injected: list[str] = []
    65→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
    66→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
    67→            if skipped is not None:
    68→                skipped.pop(sid, None)
    69→            order.append(sid)
    70→            injected.append(sid)
    71→            existing.add(sid)
    72→    return injected
    73→
    74→
    75→def _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta: dict) -> None:
    76→    """Clear defer metadata once triage is no longer deferred."""
    77→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
    78→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _store_triage_meta(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
    82→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
    83→
    84→
    85→def _inject_triage_result(
    86→    order: list[str],
    87→    confirmed: set[str],
    88→    *,
    89→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    90→    normalize: bool = False,
    91→) -> QueueSyncResult:
    92→    injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, confirmed, skipped=skipped)
    93→    if not injected:
    94→        return QueueSyncResult()
    95→    if normalize:
    96→        normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
    97→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=injected)
    98→
    99→
   100→def _mark_triage_deferred(
   101→    plan: PlanModel,
   102→    meta: dict,
   103→    *,
   104→    defer_state: dict,
   105→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   106→    meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   107→    meta["triage_recommended"] = True
   108→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
   109→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   110→    return QueueSyncResult(deferred=True)
   111→
   112→
   113→def _mark_triage_escalated(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   114→    meta[_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY] = True
   115→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   116→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   117→
   118→
   119→def _mark_triage_ready(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   120→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)

    10→def resolve_reusable_report(
    11→    report: str | None,
    12→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    13→) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    14→    if report:
    15→        return report, False
    16→    if existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
    17→        return existing_stage["report"], True
    18→    return None, False
    19→
    20→
    21→def record_observe_stage(
    22→    stages: dict,
    23→    *,
    24→    report: str,
    25→    issue_count: int,
    26→    cited_ids: list[str],
    27→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    28→    is_reuse: bool,
    29→    assessments: list[dict] | None = None,
    30→) -> list[str]:
    31→    stages["observe"] = {
    32→        "stage": "observe",
    33→        "report": report,
    34→        "cited_ids": cited_ids,
    35→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    36→        "issue_count": issue_count,
    37→    }
    38→    if assessments is not None:
    39→        stages["observe"]["assessments"] = assessments
    40→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    41→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    42→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    43→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
    44→    if not is_reuse:
    45→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    46→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    47→    return cleared
    48→
    49→
    50→def record_organize_stage(
    51→    stages: dict,
    52→    *,
    53→    report: str,
    54→    issue_count: int,
    55→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    56→    is_reuse: bool,
    57→) -> list[str]:
    58→    stages["organize"] = {
    59→        "stage": "organize",
    60→        "report": report,
    61→        "cited_ids": [],
    62→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    63→        "issue_count": issue_count,
    64→    }
    65→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    66→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    67→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    68→    return cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "organize")

1→"""Structured evidence parsing and validation for triage stage submissions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→
     9→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10→# Shared constants
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→
    13→VERDICT_KEYWORDS = frozenset({
    14→    "genuine",
    15→    "false positive",
    16→    "false-positive",
    17→    "exaggerated",
    18→    "over-engineering",
    19→    "over engineering",
    20→    "not-worth-it",
    21→    "not worth it",
    22→})
    23→
    24→# Permissive path regex — detects whether *any* file path was mentioned.
    25→# NOT used to validate existence (that's enrich-only in validation.enrich_checks).
    26→_EVIDENCE_PATH_RE = re.compile(r"[\w.@-]+/[\w./@-]+\.\w+")
    27→
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→# Data classes
    30→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31→
    32→@dataclass
    33→class EvidenceFailure:
    34→    """A single evidence validation failure."""
    35→
    36→    code: str
    37→    message: str
    38→    blocking: bool = True
    39→
    40→
    41→@dataclass
    42→class ObserveAssessment:
    43→    """A parsed per-issue assessment from an observe report (template format)."""
    44→
    45→    issue_hash: str
    46→    verdict: str  # normalised keyword
    47→    verdict_reasoning: str = ""
    48→    files_read: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    49→    recommendation: str = ""
    50→
    51→
    52→@dataclass
    53→class ObserveEvidence:
    54→    """Parsed observe-report evidence."""
    55→
    56→    entries: list[ObserveAssessment] = field(default_factory=list)
    57→    unparsed_citation_count: int = 0  # hashes cited without a verdict
    58→    has_parseable_ids: bool = True  # False if valid_ids had no hex-hash IDs
    59→
    60→
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→# OBSERVE evidence parsing — structured template format
    63→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    64→
    65→def _normalise_verdict(raw: str) -> str | None:
    66→    """Return a normalised verdict keyword, or None if not recognised."""
    67→    lower = raw.lower().strip()
    68→    for kw in VERDICT_KEYWORDS:
    69→        if kw in lower:
    70→            # Normalise to canonical form
    71→            canonical = kw.replace("-", " ")
    72→            return canonical
    73→    return None
    74→
    75→
    76→def _build_short_map(valid_ids: set[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    77→    """Build short-hash → full-id map from valid issue IDs."""
    78→    short_map: dict[str, str] = {}
    79→    for fid in valid_ids:
    80→        short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
    81→        if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{6,}", short):
    82→            short_map[short] = fid
    83→    return short_map
    84→
    85→
    86→def _is_valid_hash(raw_hash: str, short_map: dict[str, str], valid_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    87→    """Check if a hash is known in either the short map or valid IDs."""
    88→    return raw_hash in short_map or raw_hash in valid_ids
    89→
    90→
    91→# --- YAML-like template parser ---
    92→
    93→# Matches lines like:  - hash: abc12345  or  hash: abc12345
    94→_YAML_HASH_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*-?\s*hash\s*:\s*([0-9a-f]{6,})", re.IGNORECASE)
    95→# Matches lines like:  verdict: genuine  or  verdict: false-positive
    96→_YAML_VERDICT_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*verdict\s*:\s*(.+)", re.IGNORECASE)
    97→# Matches lines like:  verdict_reasoning: some reason
    98→_YAML_REASONING_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*verdict_reasoning\s*:\s*(.+)", re.IGNORECASE)
    99→# Matches lines like:  files_read: [src/foo.ts, src/bar.ts]  or  files_read: src/foo.ts
   100→_YAML_FILES_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*files_read\s*:\s*(.+)", re.IGNORECASE)
   101→# Matches lines like:  recommendation: do something
   102→_YAML_RECOMMENDATION_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*recommendation\s*:\s*(.+)", re.IGNORECASE)
   103→
   104→def _parse_yaml_files_field(raw: str) -> list[str]:
   105→    """Parse the files_read field — supports [a, b] list or single path."""
   106→    raw = raw.strip()
   107→    # Strip surrounding brackets
   108→    if raw.startswith("[") and raw.endswith("]"):
   109→        raw = raw[1:-1]
   110→    # Split on commas
   111→    paths = [p.strip().strip("'\"") for p in raw.split(",")]
   112→    return [p for p in paths if p]
   113→
   114→
   115→def _flush_yaml_entry(
   116→    current: dict,
   117→    short_map: dict[str, str],
   118→    valid_ids: set[str],
   119→) -> ObserveAssessment | None:
   120→    """Convert a collected YAML-like entry dict into an ObserveAssessment, or None if invalid."""
   121→    raw_hash = current.get("hash", "").lower()
   122→    if not raw_hash or not _is_valid_hash(raw_hash, short_map, valid_ids):
   123→        return None
   124→    raw_verdict = current.get("verdict", "")
   125→    verdict = _normalise_verdict(raw_verdict)
   126→    if not verdict:
   127→        return None
   128→    return ObserveAssessment(
   129→        issue_hash=raw_hash,
   130→        verdict=verdict,
   131→        verdict_reasoning=current.get("verdict_reasoning", ""),
   132→        files_read=current.get("files_read", []),
   133→        recommendation=current.get("recommendation", ""),
   134→    )
   135→
   136→
   137→def _parse_yaml_template(
   138→    report: str,
   139→    short_map: dict[str, str],
   140→    valid_ids: set[str],
   141→) -> list[ObserveAssessment]:
   142→    """Parse YAML-like template entries from report text."""
   143→    entries: list[ObserveAssessment] = []
   144→    current: dict | None = None
   145→
   146→    for line in report.splitlines():
   147→        m_hash = _YAML_HASH_RE.match(line)
   148→        if m_hash:
   149→            # Flush previous entry
   150→            if current is not None:

    52→
    53→
    54→def _update_existing_epic_cluster(
    55→    existing: Cluster,
    56→    epic_data: dict,
    57→    *,
    58→    now: str,

    60→) -> None:
    61→    existing["thesis"] = epic_data["thesis"]
    62→    existing["direction"] = epic_data["direction"]
    63→    existing["root_cause"] = epic_data.get("root_cause", "")
    64→    existing["issue_ids"] = epic_data["issue_ids"]

    69→    existing["updated_at"] = now

    71→    existing["description"] = epic_data["thesis"]
    72→    # Don't overwrite in_progress status from agent
    73→    if existing.get("status") != "in_progress":
    74→        existing["status"] = epic_data.get("status", "pending")
    75→
    76→
    77→def _create_epic_cluster(
    78→    *,
    79→    epic_name: str,
    80→    epic_data: dict,

    83→) -> Cluster:

    87→        "issue_ids": epic_data["issue_ids"],
    88→        "auto": True,
    89→        "cluster_key": f"epic::{epic_name}",
    90→        "action": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",

   108→
   109→def _upsert_triage_clusters(
   110→    *,
   111→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster],
   112→    triage: TriageResult,
   113→    now: str,
   114→    version: int,
   115→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   116→    created = 0
   117→    updated = 0
   118→    for epic_data in sorted(triage.clusters, key=_epic_sort_key):
   119→        raw_name = epic_data["name"]
   120→        epic_name = _normalized_epic_name(raw_name)
   121→        existing = clusters.get(epic_name)
   122→        if existing and existing.get("thesis"):
   123→            _update_existing_epic_cluster(existing, epic_data, now=now, version=version)
   124→            updated += 1
   125→            continue
   126→        clusters[epic_name] = _create_epic_cluster(
   127→            epic_name=epic_name,
   128→            epic_data=epic_data,
   129→            now=now,
   130→            version=version,
   131→        )
   132→        created += 1
   133→    return created, updated
   134→
   135→

   140→    dismissed_ids: list[str],
   141→) -> None:
   142→    epic_issue_ids: set[str] = set()
   143→    epic_ordered_ids: list[str] = []
   144→    dismissed_set = set(dismissed_ids)
   145→    for epic_data in sorted(triage.clusters, key=_epic_sort_key):
   146→        for fid in epic_data["issue_ids"]:
   147→            if fid in epic_issue_ids or fid in dismissed_set:
   148→                continue
   149→            epic_issue_ids.add(fid)
   150→            epic_ordered_ids.append(fid)

    11→def _cluster_or_raise(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> Cluster:

    14→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
    15→    return cluster
    16→
    17→
    18→def _upsert_cluster_override(
    19→    plan: PlanModel,
    20→    issue_id: str,
    21→    *,
    22→    cluster_name: str,
    23→    timestamp: str,
    24→) -> None:
    25→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
    26→    if issue_id not in overrides:
    27→        overrides[issue_id] = {"issue_id": issue_id, "created_at": timestamp}
    28→    overrides[issue_id]["cluster"] = cluster_name
    29→    overrides[issue_id]["updated_at"] = timestamp
    30→
    31→
    32→def _clear_cluster_override(
    33→    plan: PlanModel,

    35→    *,
    36→    cluster_name: str,
    37→    timestamp: str,
    38→) -> None:
    39→    override = plan["overrides"].get(issue_id)
    40→    if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
    41→        override["cluster"] = None
    42→        override["updated_at"] = timestamp

    46→    if not target.get("description") and source.get("description"):
    47→        target["description"] = source["description"]
    48→    if not target.get("action_steps") and source.get("action_steps"):

    81→        "action": action,

    83→    }

    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults

    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class SkipOptions:
    17→    """Optional fields used when skipping issue IDs."""
    18→
    19→    kind: str = "temporary"
    20→    reason: str | None = None
    21→    note: str | None = None
    22→    attestation: str | None = None
    23→    review_after: int | None = None
    24→    scan_count: int = 0
    25→
    26→
    27→def _skip_options(
    28→    *,
    29→    options: SkipOptions | None,
    30→    legacy_kwargs: dict[str, object],
    31→) -> SkipOptions:
    32→    """Resolve skip options from a config object or legacy kwargs."""
    33→    allowed_keys = {
    34→        "kind",
    35→        "reason",
    36→        "note",
    37→        "attestation",
    38→        "review_after",
    39→        "scan_count",
    40→    }
    41→    unknown = sorted(set(legacy_kwargs) - allowed_keys)
    42→    if unknown:
    43→        joined = ", ".join(unknown)
    44→        raise TypeError(f"Unexpected keyword argument(s): {joined}")
    45→    if options is not None and legacy_kwargs:
    46→        raise TypeError("Pass either options=... or legacy keyword args, not both")
    47→    if options is not None:
    48→        return options
    49→    kind = legacy_kwargs.get("kind")
    50→    reason = legacy_kwargs.get("reason")
    51→    note = legacy_kwargs.get("note")
    52→    attestation = legacy_kwargs.get("attestation")
    53→    review_after = legacy_kwargs.get("review_after")
    54→    scan_count = legacy_kwargs.get("scan_count")
    55→    return SkipOptions(
    56→        kind=str(kind) if kind is not None else "temporary",
    57→        reason=None if reason is None else str(reason),
    58→        note=None if note is None else str(note),
    59→        attestation=None if attestation is None else str(attestation),
    60→        review_after=int(review_after) if isinstance(review_after, int) else None,
    61→        scan_count=int(scan_count) if isinstance(scan_count, int) else 0,
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def skip_items(
    66→    plan: PlanModel,
    67→    issue_ids: list[str],
    68→    *,
    69→    options: SkipOptions | None = None,
    70→    **legacy_kwargs,
    71→) -> int:
    72→    """Move issue IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
    73→    skip_options = _skip_options(
    74→        options=options,
    75→        legacy_kwargs=legacy_kwargs,
    76→    )
    77→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    78→    now = utc_now()
    79→    count = 0
    80→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    81→    skip_set = set(issue_ids)
    82→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, skip_set)
    83→    for fid in issue_ids:
    84→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
    85→        skipped[fid] = {
    86→            "issue_id": fid,
    87→            "kind": skip_options.kind,
    88→            "reason": skip_options.reason,
    89→            "note": skip_options.note,
    90→            "attestation": skip_options.attestation,
    91→            "created_at": now,
    92→            "review_after": skip_options.review_after,
    93→            "skipped_at_scan": skip_options.scan_count,
    94→        }
    95→        count += 1
    96→    clear_focus_if_cluster_empty(plan)
    97→    return count
    98→

1→"""Canonical queue mutation helpers for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import add_promoted_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     7→
     8→
     9→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str) -> None:
    10→    """Remove an issue ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    11→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    12→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    13→    if issue_id in order:
    14→        order.remove(issue_id)
    15→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _resolve_position(
    19→    order: list[str],
    20→    position: str,
    21→    target: str | None = None,
    22→    offset: int | None = None,
    23→    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    24→) -> int:
    25→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    26→
    27→    ``issue_ids`` are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    28→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    29→    """
    30→    moving = set(issue_ids or [])
    31→
    32→    if position == "top":
    33→        return 0
    34→    if position == "bottom":
    35→        return len(order)
    36→
    37→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    38→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    39→
    40→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    41→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    42→            order,
    43→            moving,
    44→            position=position,
    45→            offset=offset,
    46→            issue_ids=issue_ids,
    47→        )
    48→
    49→    return len(order)
    50→
    51→
    52→def _resolve_relative_position(
    53→    order: list[str],
    54→    moving: set[str],
    55→    *,
    56→    position: str,
    57→    target: str,
    58→) -> int:
    59→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    60→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    61→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
    62→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
    63→
    64→
    65→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
    66→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
    67→        if item_id == target:
    68→            return i
    69→    return None
    70→
    71→
    72→def _resolve_offset_position(
    73→    order: list[str],
    74→    moving: set[str],
    75→    *,
    76→    position: str,
    77→    offset: int,
    78→    issue_ids: list[str] | None,
    79→) -> int:
    80→    if not issue_ids:
    81→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
    82→    first_id = issue_ids[0]
    83→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    84→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
    85→    if current_idx is None:
    86→        if position == "up":
    87→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    88→        return len(clean_order)
    89→    if position == "up":
    90→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    91→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    92→
    93→
    94→def move_items(
    95→    plan: PlanModel,
    96→    issue_ids: list[str],
    97→    position: str,
    98→    target: str | None = None,
    99→    offset: int | None = None,
   100→) -> int:

     3→from __future__ import annotations

     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (

    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel

    18→from .defer_policy import (
    19→    DeferEscalationOptions,

    22→    update_defer_state,
    23→)

    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
    36→    state: StateModel,
    37→    meta: dict,
    38→) -> set[str]:
    39→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
    40→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    41→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids
    42→
    43→
    44→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
    45→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
    46→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    47→        while sid in order:
    48→            order.remove(sid)
    49→
    50→
    51→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
    52→    order: list[str],
    53→    confirmed: set[str],
    54→    *,
    55→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    56→) -> list[str]:
    57→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
    58→
    59→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
    60→    Returns list of injected stage IDs.
    61→    """
    62→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
    63→    existing = set(order)
    64→    injected: list[str] = []
    65→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
    66→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
    67→            if skipped is not None:
    68→                skipped.pop(sid, None)
    69→            order.append(sid)
    70→            injected.append(sid)
    71→            existing.add(sid)
    72→    return injected
    73→

    34→def _required_issue_hashes(triage_input: TriageInput) -> list[str]:
    35→    """Return sorted short hashes for open review issues."""
    36→    return sorted(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in triage_input.open_issues)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _compact_issue_summary(triage_input: TriageInput) -> str:
    40→    """Return a compact issue summary for later triage stages."""
    41→    by_dim: Counter[str] = Counter()
    42→    for issue in triage_input.open_issues.values():
    43→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    44→        by_dim[str(detail.get("dimension", "unknown"))] += 1
    45→    dims = ", ".join(f"{name} ({count})" for name, count in sorted(by_dim.items()))
    46→    parts = [
    47→        "## Issue Summary",
    48→        f"Open review issues: {len(triage_input.open_issues)}",
    49→    ]
    50→    if dims:
    51→        parts.append(f"Open dimensions: {dims}")
    52→    if triage_input.new_since_last:
    53→        parts.append(f"New since last triage: {len(triage_input.new_since_last)}")
    54→    if triage_input.resolved_since_last:
    55→        parts.append(f"Resolved since last triage: {len(triage_input.resolved_since_last)}")
    56→
    57→    # Cluster summary if clusters exist
    58→    existing = triage_input.existing_clusters or {}
    59→    if existing:
    60→        parts.append("")
    61→        parts.append("## Clusters")
    62→        for cname, cdata in existing.items():
    63→            desc = cdata.get("description") or ""
    64→            count = len(cdata.get("issue_ids", []))
    65→            desc_str = f" ({desc})" if desc else ""
    66→            parts.append(f"- {cname}: {count} issues{desc_str}")
    67→
    68→    return "\n".join(parts)
    69→
    70→
    71→_INVESTIGATION_COMMANDS = """\
    72→
    73→## Available Investigation Commands
    74→
    75→To see full detail for a specific issue:
    76→  desloppify show <issue-id-or-hash-pattern> --no-budget
    77→To see all open review issues with suggestions:
    78→  desloppify show review --status open --no-budget
    79→To see a cluster's steps, members, and suggestions:
    80→  desloppify plan cluster show <name>
    81→To list all clusters:
    82→  desloppify plan cluster list --verbose"""
    83→
    84→
    85→def _issue_context_for_stage(
    86→    stage: str,
    87→    triage_input: TriageInput,
    88→    mode: PromptMode = "self_record",
    89→) -> str:
    90→    """Return the amount of issue context appropriate for a stage."""
    91→    if stage in {"observe", "reflect"}:
    92→        parts = ["## Issue Data\n\n" + build_triage_prompt(triage_input)]
    93→        if stage == "reflect":
    94→            short_ids = _required_issue_hashes(triage_input)
    95→            parts.append(
    96→                "## Required Issue Hashes\n"
    97→                f"Total open review issues: {len(short_ids)}\n"
    98→                "Every one of these hashes must appear exactly once in your cluster/skip blueprint.\n"
    99→                "Do not repeat hashes outside that blueprint.\n"
   100→                + ", ".join(short_ids)
   101→            )
   102→            parts.append(
   103→                "## Coverage Ledger Template\n"
   104→                "Your final report MUST contain a `## Coverage Ledger` section with one line per issue.\n"
   105→                "Allowed forms:\n"
   106→                '- `- abcd1234 -> cluster "cluster-name"`\n'
   107→                '- `- abcd1234 -> skip "specific-reason-tag"`\n'
   108→                "Do not mention hashes outside the `## Coverage Ledger` section.\n"
   109→                + "\n".join(f"- {short_id} -> TODO" for short_id in short_ids)
   110→            )
   111→        return "\n\n".join(parts)
   112→    summary = _compact_issue_summary(triage_input)
   113→    if mode == "self_record":
   114→        summary += _INVESTIGATION_COMMANDS
   115→    return summary
   116→
   117→
   118→def _format_assessments_table(assessments: list[dict]) -> str:
   119→    """Format structured observe assessments as a readable table for downstream stages."""
   120→    if not assessments:
   121→        return ""
   122→    lines = ["### Structured Observe Assessments\n"]
   123→    lines.append("| Hash | Verdict | Recommendation |")
   124→    lines.append("|------|---------|----------------|")
   125→    for a in assessments:
   126→        h = a.get("hash", "?")
   127→        v = a.get("verdict", "?")
   128→        r = a.get("recommendation", "")
   129→        # Truncate recommendation for table readability
   130→        if len(r) > 80:
   131→            r = r[:77] + "..."
   132→        lines.append(f"| {h} | {v} | {r} |")
   133→    return "\n".join(lines)
   134→
   135→
   136→def _format_assessment_file_evidence(assessments: list[dict]) -> str:
   137→    """Format file-level observe evidence for clustering-heavy downstream stages."""
   138→    if not assessments:
   139→        return ""
   140→    lines = ["### Observe File Evidence\n"]
   141→    for assessment in assessments:
   142→        issue_hash = str(assessment.get("hash", "?"))
   143→        files_read = [
   144→            str(path).strip()
   145→            for path in assessment.get("files_read", [])
   146→            if str(path).strip()
   147→        ]
   148→        verdict = str(assessment.get("verdict", "?")).strip() or "?"
   149→        reasoning = str(assessment.get("verdict_reasoning", "")).strip()
   150→        recommendation = str(assessment.get("recommendation", "")).strip()

    58→def load_prior_reports_from_plan(plan: Mapping[str, Any], stages: list[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    59→    """Seed prior stage reports from the current live triage state."""
    60→    triage_stages = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {})
    61→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
    62→    for stage in stages:

    40→class PlanLoadStatus:
    41→    """Resolved plan load result with degraded-mode signaling."""

    48→def get_plan_file() -> Path:

    52→
    53→def _default_plan_file() -> Path:

    55→
    56→    If tests monkeypatch ``PLAN_FILE`` in this module, use the patched value.
    57→    """
    58→    if PLAN_FILE != _INITIAL_PLAN_FILE:
    59→        return Path(PLAN_FILE)
    60→    return get_plan_file()
    61→
    62→
    63→@contextmanager
    64→def plan_lock(path: Path | None = None) -> Iterator[None]:
    65→    """Acquire exclusive lock on plan file for read-modify-write safety."""
    66→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
    67→    lock_path = plan_path.with_suffix(".lock")
    68→    lock_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    69→    fd = os.open(str(lock_path), os.O_CREAT | os.O_WRONLY)
    70→    try:
    71→        if sys.platform == "win32":
    72→            import msvcrt
    73→
    74→            msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_LOCK, 1)
    75→        else:
    76→            import fcntl
    77→
    78→            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
    79→        yield
    80→    finally:
    81→        if sys.platform == "win32":
    82→            import msvcrt
    83→
    84→            msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
    85→        else:
    86→            import fcntl
    87→
    88→            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    89→        os.close(fd)
    90→
    91→
    92→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    93→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    94→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
    95→    if not plan_path.exists():
    96→        return empty_plan()
    97→
    98→    try:
    99→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
   100→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:

    20→def build_work_queue(
    21→    state: StateModel,
    22→    *,
    23→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    24→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    25→    """Build the active canonical work queue for the current invocation."""
    26→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    27→        state,
    28→        options=options,
    29→        visibility=QueueVisibility.ALL,
    30→    )
    31→
    32→
    33→def _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    34→    state: StateModel,
    35→    *,
    36→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    37→    visibility: str = QueueVisibility.ALL,
    38→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    39→    """Build a ranked work queue from canonical queue partitions.
    40→
    41→    Open-status queue views are resolved from the canonical queue snapshot so
    42→    lifecycle phase, execution visibility, and backlog visibility all come
    43→    from one source of truth. Non-open status views remain issue-list queries.
    44→    """
    45→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    46→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = resolve_queue_inputs(opts, state)
    47→    items = select_queue_items(
    48→        state,
    49→        opts=opts,
    50→        plan=plan,
    51→        scan_path=scan_path,
    52→        status=status,
    53→        threshold=threshold,
    54→        visibility=visibility,
    55→    )
    56→    return finalize_queue(items, state=state, plan=plan, opts=opts)
    57→

    64→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    72→def _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    73→    """Extract the triage stages dict from meta or a raw stages dict."""
    74→    if not isinstance(meta_or_stages, dict):
    75→        return {}
    76→    if "triage_stages" in meta_or_stages:
    77→        raw = meta_or_stages.get("triage_stages")
    78→        return raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    79→    candidate_names = {str(name) for name in meta_or_stages.keys()}
    80→    if candidate_names and candidate_names.issubset(_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES):
    81→        return meta_or_stages
    82→    return {}
    83→
    84→
    85→def confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    86→    """Return triage stage names with an explicit ``confirmed_at`` marker."""
    87→    return {
    88→        str(name)
    89→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    90→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get("confirmed_at")
    91→    }
    92→
    93→

    96→    return {
    97→        str(name)
    98→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    99→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload and not payload.get("confirmed_at")
   100→    }

     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.shared.cluster_membership import cluster_issue_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import open_review_ids
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import Cluster, PlanModel
     9→
    10→from .plan_state_access import (
    11→    ensure_cluster_map,
    12→    ensure_queue_order,
    13→    ensure_skipped_map,
    14→    ensure_triage_meta,
    15→    normalized_issue_id_list,
    16→)
    17→
    18→[REDACTED]
    19→[REDACTED]

    21→
    22→
    23→def open_review_ids_from_state(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    24→    """Return open review IDs from the current state snapshot."""
    25→    return open_review_ids(state)
    26→
    27→
    28→def has_open_review_issues(state: StateModel | dict | None) -> bool:
    29→    """Return True when any open review issues exist."""
    30→    return bool(open_review_ids_from_state(state or {}))
    31→
    32→
    33→def plan_review_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
    34→    """Return review/concerns IDs currently represented in queue_order."""
    35→    return [
    36→        fid
    37→        for fid in ensure_queue_order(plan)
    38→        if not fid.startswith("triage::")
    39→        and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
    40→        and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
    41→    ]
    42→
    43→
    44→def coverage_open_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    45→    """Return the frozen or live open review IDs covered by this triage run."""
    46→    active_ids = normalized_issue_id_list(
    47→        ensure_triage_meta(plan).get(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY)
    48→    )
    49→    if active_ids:
    50→        return set(active_ids)
    51→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    52→    review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    53→    if not has_completed_scan and not review_ids:
    54→        return set(plan_review_ids(plan))
    55→    return review_ids
    56→
    57→
    58→def active_triage_issue_ids(
    59→    plan: PlanModel,
    60→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    61→) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return the frozen review issue set for the current triage run."""
    63→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
    64→    active_ids = normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY))
    65→    if active_ids:
    66→        return set(active_ids)
    67→    if state is None:
    68→        return set()
    69→    return coverage_open_ids(plan, state)
    70→
    71→
    72→def live_active_triage_issue_ids(
    73→    plan: PlanModel,
    74→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    75→) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return frozen triage IDs that are still open review issues in state."""
    77→    frozen_ids = active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    78→    if state is None or not frozen_ids:
    79→        return frozen_ids
    80→    return frozen_ids & open_review_ids(state)
    81→
    82→
    83→def ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    84→    """Freeze the current triage issue set for validation across stage reruns."""
    85→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
    86→    active_ids = sorted(coverage_open_ids(plan, state))
    87→    meta[_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY] = active_ids
    88→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
    89→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
    90→    return active_ids
    91→
    92→
    93→def clear_active_triage_issue_tracking(meta: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    94→    """Clear frozen triage coverage metadata after successful completion."""
    95→    meta.pop(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY, None)
    96→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
    97→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
    98→
    99→
   100→def undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(
   101→    plan: PlanModel,
   102→    state: StateModel | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Return frozen triage issues still lacking cluster/skip/dismiss coverage."""
   105→    target_ids = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   106→    if not target_ids:
   107→        return []
   108→    covered_ids: set[str] = set()
   109→    for cluster in ensure_cluster_map(plan).values():
   110→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   111→            continue
   112→        covered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   113→    covered_ids.update(
   114→        issue_id for issue_id in ensure_skipped_map(plan) if isinstance(issue_id, str)
   115→    )
   116→    covered_ids.update(
   117→        normalized_issue_id_list(ensure_triage_meta(plan).get("dismissed_ids"))
   118→    )
   119→    return sorted(issue_id for issue_id in target_ids if issue_id not in covered_ids)
   120→
   121→
   122→def sync_undispositioned_triage_meta(
   123→    plan: PlanModel,
   124→    state: StateModel | None = None,
   125→) -> list[str]:
   126→    """Persist the current undispositioned triage issue set for recovery UX."""
   127→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
   128→    missing = undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   129→    if missing:
   130→        meta[_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY] = missing
   131→        meta[_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY] = len(missing)
   132→    else:
   133→        meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
   134→        meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
   135→    return missing
   136→
   137→
   138→def triage_coverage(
   139→    plan: PlanModel,
   140→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   141→) -> tuple[int, int, dict[str, Cluster]]:
   142→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage."""
   143→    clusters = ensure_cluster_map(plan)
   144→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
   145→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   146→        all_cluster_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   147→    review_ids = list(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else plan_review_ids(plan)
   148→    organized = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
   149→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   150→

   156→_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   157→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Produce a coherent
   158→prioritized strategy for all open review issues.
   159→
   160→Your plan should:
   161→- Cluster issues by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   162→- Give each cluster a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   163→- Order clusters by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   164→- Dismiss issues that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   165→- Mark which clusters are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   166→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   167→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all issues coherently
   168→
   169→Available directions for clusters: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   170→
   171→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   172→- `desloppify plan queue` — view the execution queue in priority order
   173→- `desloppify backlog` — inspect broader open work outside the execution queue
   174→- `desloppify plan focus <name>` — focus the queue on one cluster
   175→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   176→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   177→- `desloppify plan resolve <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   178→- `desloppify plan reorder <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   179→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   180→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   181→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review issues
   182→
   183→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Issues not assigned to any cluster
   184→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed issues will be
   185→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   186→
   187→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   188→{
   189→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   190→  "clusters": [
   191→    {
   192→      "name": "slug-name",
   193→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   194→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   195→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   196→      "issue_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   197→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   198→      "agent_safe": true,
   199→      "dependency_order": 1,
   200→      "action_steps": [
   201→        {
   202→          "title": "Short imperative step title",
   203→          "detail": "Concrete implementation detail with file paths/locations",
   204→          "issue_refs": ["id1"]
   205→        }
   206→      ],
   207→      "status": "pending"
   208→    }
   209→  ],
   210→  "dismissed_issues": [
   211→    {"issue_id": "id", "reason": "why this issue doesn't make sense"}
   212→  ],
   213→  "contradiction_notes": [
   214→    {"kept": "issue_id", "dismissed": "issue_id", "reason": "why"}
   215→  ],
   216→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   217→}
   218→"""
   219→
   220→def _append_existing_clusters_section(parts: list[str], clusters: dict[str, Cluster]) -> None:
   221→    if not clusters:
   222→        return
   223→    parts.append("## Existing clusters")
   224→    for name, cluster in sorted(clusters.items()):
   225→        status = cluster.get("status", "pending")
   226→        thesis = cluster.get("thesis", "")
   227→        direction = cluster.get("direction", "")
   228→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   229→        parts.append(
   230→            f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   231→            f"\n  Issues: {', '.join(issue_ids[:10])}"
   232→            f"{'...' if len(issue_ids) > 10 else ''}"
   233→        )
   234→    parts.append("")
   235→
   236→
   237→def _append_changed_issue_section(
   238→    parts: list[str],
   239→    *,
   240→    title: str,
   241→    issue_ids: set[str],
   242→    issues: dict[str, dict] | None = None,
   243→) -> None:
   244→    if not issue_ids:
   245→        return
   246→    parts.append(f"## {title} ({len(issue_ids)})")
   247→    for issue_id in sorted(issue_ids):
   248→        if issues is None:
   249→            parts.append(f"- {issue_id}")
   250→            continue
   251→        issue = issues.get(issue_id, {})
   252→        parts.append(f"- {issue_id}: {issue.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   253→    parts.append("")
   254→
   255→
   256→def _append_resolved_issue_context(parts: list[str], resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]) -> None:
   257→    if not resolved_issues:
   258→        return
   259→    parts.append(
   260→        "## Resolved review issues available for recurrence context "
   261→        f"({len(resolved_issues)})"
   262→    )
   263→    resolved_ids = sorted(resolved_issues)
   264→    for issue_id in resolved_ids[:30]:
   265→        issue = resolved_issues.get(issue_id, {})
   266→        summary = str(issue.get("summary", "(no summary)"))
   267→        dimension = _issue_dimension(issue)
   268→        dim_suffix = f" [{dimension}]" if dimension else ""
   269→        parts.append(f"- {issue_id}{dim_suffix}: {summary}")
   270→    if len(resolved_ids) > 30:
   271→        parts.append(f"- ... and {len(resolved_ids) - 30} more resolved issues")
   272→    parts.append("")
   273→
   274→
   275→def _append_completed_clusters_section(parts: list[str], completed_clusters: list[dict]) -> None:
   276→    if not completed_clusters:
   277→        return
   278→    parts.append(
   279→        "## Completed clusters since last triage "
   280→        f"({len(completed_clusters)})"
   281→    )
   282→    completed = sorted(
   283→        completed_clusters,
   284→        key=lambda cluster: str(cluster.get("completed_at", "")),
   285→        reverse=True,
   286→    )
   287→    for cluster in completed[:10]:
   288→        name = str(cluster.get("name", "(unnamed cluster)"))
   289→        thesis = str(cluster.get("thesis") or cluster.get("description") or "")
   290→        completed_at = str(cluster.get("completed_at", ""))
   291→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   292→        issue_count = len(issue_ids) if isinstance(issue_ids, list) else 0
   293→        parts.append(
   294→            f"- {name}: {thesis} (issues: {issue_count}, completed_at: {completed_at})"
   295→        )
   296→    if len(completed) > 10:
   297→        parts.append(f"- ... and {len(completed) - 10} more completed clusters")
   298→    parts.append("")
   299→

    26→def _preserve_integrity_target(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
    27→    """Extract the current subjective integrity target so recompute doesn't erase it."""
    28→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity")
    29→    if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    30→        return None
    31→    raw = integrity.get("target_score")
    32→    if raw is None:
    33→        return None
    34→    try:
    35→        return max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw)))
    36→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    37→        return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    41→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    42→
    43→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    44→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    45→    """
    46→    if is_numeric(value):
    47→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    48→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    49→        raw = value.get("score")
    50→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    51→            return None
    52→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    53→    return None
    54→
    55→
    56→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    57→    state: StateModel,
    58→    *,
    59→    status: str,
    60→    resolved_issues: list[dict],
    61→    now: str,
    62→) -> None:
    63→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review issues are resolved.
    64→
    65→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    66→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    67→    for a re-review.
    68→    """
    69→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    70→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    71→        return
    72→
    73→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    74→    for issue in resolved_issues:
    75→        if issue.get("detector") != "review":
    76→            continue
    77→        dimension = str(issue.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    78→        if dimension:
    79→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    80→
    81→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    82→        if dimension not in assessments:
    83→            continue
    84→
    85→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    86→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    87→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    88→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_issue_{status}"
    89→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    90→        else:
    91→            assessments[dimension] = {
    92→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    93→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    94→                "refresh_reason": f"review_issue_{status}",
    95→                "stale_since": now,
    96→            }
    97→
    98→

   100→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"

253→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   254→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   255→    return {
   256→        name: cluster
   257→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   258→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   259→    }
   260→
   261→
   262→def tracked_plan_ids(plan: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
   263→    """Collect all issue IDs the plan is actively tracking.
   264→
   265→    Includes queue_order, skipped, overrides, and cluster members/step refs.
   266→    Returns an empty set for None or non-dict plans.
   267→    """
   268→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   269→        return set()
   270→    tracked: set[str] = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   271→    tracked.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   272→    tracked.update(plan.get("overrides", {}).keys())
   273→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   274→        tracked.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   275→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", []):
   276→            if isinstance(step, dict):
   277→                tracked.update(step.get("issue_refs", []))
   278→    return tracked
   279→
   280→
   281→def planned_objective_ids(
   282→    all_objective_ids: set[str],
   283→    plan: dict[str, Any] | None,
   284→) -> set[str]:
   285→    """Return the subset of objective IDs the plan considers active work.
   286→
   287→    Pre-triage (plan tracks nothing): all objective IDs are treated as
   288→    planned work.
   289→
   290→    Once the plan tracks anything, only the intersection with live
   291→    objective IDs counts as planned. This lets stale queue_order entries
   292→    fail closed into postflight/backlog instead of broadening execution
   293→    back out to the entire objective backlog.
   294→    """
   295→    tracked = tracked_plan_ids(plan)
   296→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys()) if isinstance(plan, dict) else set()
   297→    active_tracked = {
   298→        issue_id
   299→        for issue_id in tracked - skipped_ids
   300→        if not any(issue_id.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   301→    }
   302→    if not active_tracked:
   303→        return set(all_objective_ids)
   304→    return all_objective_ids & active_tracked
   305→
   306→
   307→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   308→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   309→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   310→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   311→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   312→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   313→
   314→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   315→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   316→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   317→    if overlap:
   318→        raise ValueError(
   319→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   320→        )
   321→
   322→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   323→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   324→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   325→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] must be an object")
   326→        if "kind" not in entry:
   327→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] missing required key 'kind'")
   328→        kind = entry["kind"]
   329→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   330→            raise ValueError(
   331→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   332→            )

155→def apply_completion(
   156→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   157→    plan: PlanModel,
   158→    strategy: str,
   159→    *,
   160→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
