   175→    )
   176→    existing_strategy = _normalize_summary_text(

   178→    )
   179→    meta, effective_strategy_summary = _sync_completion_meta(

   184→        completion_note=completion_note,

   203→
   204→def count_log_activity_since(plan: PlanModel, since: str) -> dict[str, int]:
   205→    """Count execution-log activity by action since a timestamp string."""
   206→    counts: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   207→    for raw_entry in ensure_execution_log(plan):
   208→        if "timestamp" not in raw_entry or "action" not in raw_entry:
   209→            continue
   210→        timestamp = raw_entry["timestamp"]
   211→        action = raw_entry["action"]
   212→        if not isinstance(timestamp, str) or not isinstance(action, str):
   213→            continue
   214→        if timestamp >= since:
   215→            counts[action] += 1
   216→    return dict(counts)
   217→
   218→
   219→__all__ = [
   220→    "apply_completion",
   221→    "count_log_activity_since",
   222→]
   223→

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding. Let me compile my findings into a detailed report.

## Comprehensive Triage State Architecture Report

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's the complete topology of the triage state architecture:

---

### 1. **Where Triage Stage State Lives**

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/records.py`

Triage stages are recorded in `plan.json` at `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"]`, a dict keyed by stage name:

```
{
  "observe": {...},
  "reflect": {...},
  "organize": {...},
  "enrich": {...},
  "sense-check": {...}
}
```

Each stage record (TypedDict `TriageStagePayload` in schema) contains:
- `stage`: stage name
- `report`: full text narrative from the agent
- `cited_ids`: list of issue IDs mentioned in report
- `timestamp`: UTC ISO timestamp
- `issue_count`: number of issues analyzed
- `confirmed_at`: ISO timestamp (set when user confirms, cleared by cascading)
- `confirmed_text`: user's confirmation rationale (cleared by cascading)

**Stage-specific fields**:
- **observe**: adds `assessments` (list of verdict dicts: hash, verdict, verdict_reasoning, files_read, recommendation)
- **reflect**: adds `missing_issue_ids`, `duplicate_issue_ids`, `recurring_dims`, `disposition_ledger` (structured ReflectDisposition objects)
- **organize**: adds nothing special (just base fields)
- **enrich**: adds `shallow_count` 
- **sense-check**: adds nothing special

**Critical behavior**: When any stage is recorded, `cascade_clear_later_confirmations()` (in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stage_queue.py`) clears `confirmed_at` and `confirmed_text` from all downstream stages. STAGE_ORDER is: `["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check"]`

---

### 2. **Where Observe Assessments Are Stored**

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/observe.py` (lines 106-124)

Observe assessments are parsed from structured evidence in the report and persisted as:
```python
stages["observe"]["assessments"] = [
    {
        "hash": entry.issue_hash,
        "verdict": entry.verdict,  # e.g., "genuine", "false_positive", "exaggerated", "not-worth-it"
        "verdict_reasoning": entry.verdict_reasoning,
        "files_read": entry.files_read,  # list of file paths examined
        "recommendation": entry.recommendation,
    }
    for entry in evidence.entries
]
```

These assessments are parsed by `parse_observe_evidence()` and validated by `validate_observe_evidence()` (both in `evidence_parsing.py`). They form a structured verdict per issue that downstream stages (especially reflect) can reference.

---

### 3. **Where Cluster State Lives**

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema/__init__.py` (Cluster TypedDict, lines 50-73)

Clusters live in `plan["clusters"]`, keyed by cluster name. Two types:
1. **Manual clusters**: user-created (names don't start with "auto/" or "epic/")
2. **Epic/triage clusters**: auto-generated by triage (names start with "epic/")

**Cluster TypedDict fields**:
- `name`: Required, cluster identifier
- `description`: long-form thesis/goal
- `issue_ids`: list of review issue IDs in this cluster
- `created_at`, `updated_at`: ISO timestamps
- `auto`: bool (True for auto-generated)
- `cluster_key`: deterministic grouping key for regeneration
- `action`: primary resolution command/guidance
- `user_modified`: True when user manually edits membership
- `action_steps`: list of ActionStep (title, detail, issue_refs list, done bool)
- `priority`: int priority order
- `status`: "pending", "in_progress", "completed"
- **Epic fields** (for triage-generated clusters):
  - `thesis`: one-liner imperative
  - `direction`: "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce", "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline"
  - `root_cause`: why this cluster exists
  - `supersedes`: list of earlier cluster names it replaces
  - `dismissed`: list of issue IDs this cluster dismisses
  - `agent_safe`: bool (can be executed mechanically)
  - `dependency_order`: int for execution ordering
  - `triage_version`: int version number
  - `source_clusters`: list of prior cluster names (for tracking lineage)

**How created/mutated**: 
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations/cluster.py` provides: `create_cluster()`, `add_to_cluster()`, `remove_from_cluster()`, etc.
- Triage engine in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/apply.py` applies LLM output to create/update epic clusters atomically.

**How read**:
- `triage_clusters(plan)` (schema/__init__.py line 253): filters clusters to only "epic/*" prefixed ones
- Work queue and plan views access via `plan["clusters"]`

---

### 4. **Where the Work Queue Lives**

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

The work queue is NOT persisted as state. It's computed on-demand by `build_work_queue(state, options)`, which:
1. Reads `state` and optionally `plan` (from options)
2. Selects items using `select_queue_items()` based on filters
3. Ranks/groups items via `group_queue_items()`
4. Returns `WorkQueueResult` (TypedDict with `items`, `total`, `grouped`, `new_ids`)

**Key models** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/models.py`):
- `QueueBuildOptions`: frozen dataclass with count, explain, scan_path, scope, status, chronic, include_subjective, etc.
- `WorkQueueResult`: typed dict with items list, total count, grouped dict, new_ids set
- `WorkQueueItem`: from types.py (represents one work item)

**Relation to clusters**:
- Queue items can be individual review issues, synthetic triage/workflow stages, or clusters
- Cluster issues are "expanded" or "collapsed" depending on view mode (`collapse_clusters()`)
- Queue respects `plan["queue_order"]` (the canonical ordering list)

---

### 5. **How Review Issues Relate to Plan State**

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/prompt.py` (TriageInput dataclass, lines 18-31)

Review issues come from **state**, not the plan:

```python
@dataclass
class TriageInput:
    open_issues: dict[str, dict]           # id -> issue (review + concerns ONLY)
    mechanical_issues: dict[str, dict]     # id -> issue (non-review, context)
    existing_clusters: dict[str, Cluster]  # from plan["clusters"]
    new_since_last: set[str]               # current_review_ids - triaged_ids
    resolved_since_last: set[str]          # triaged_ids - current_review_ids
    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]       # full issue objects for resolved IDs
    completed_clusters: list[dict]         # clusters completed since last triage
```

**How collected** (`collect_triage_input()`, lines 123-154):
1. Pull `issues` from `state["issues"]` dict
2. Split into "open_review" (detector="review"/"concerns") and "open_mechanical" (other detectors)
3. Freeze the active set in `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["active_triage_issue_ids"]` (for validation across stage reruns)
4. Compare against `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triaged_ids"]` to find new/resolved
5. Collect resolved issue objects for recurrence context

**Issue mapping**:
- Issue ID format: `"review::abc123def456"` or `"concerns::xyz789"`
- Issues have `detail.dimension` (detector dimension name) for recurring pattern detection
- Issues have `status` ("open", "resolved"), `detector` (review/concerns/metric/etc), `summary`, `file`, `line`, etc.

**How status changes**:
- Issues stay in `state["issues"]` until next scan resolves them
- Triage creates clusters in `plan["clusters"]` that reference these issues via issue_ids
- When cluster is completed, it moves to `plan["completed_clusters"]` (snapshots old cluster for context)
- Issues are NOT modified by triage; triage only groups them into plan clusters

---

### 6. **The Triage Lifecycle**

**Files**: 
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/lifecycle.py`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/triage.py` (reconciliation)

**Lifecycle sequence**:
1. **Triage start** (`ensure_triage_started()`):
   - Checks if triage is already queued via `has_triage_in_queue(plan)`
   - Calls `decide_triage_start(plan, state, explicit_start=True)` to check guards (no unfinished stages, objective work clear)
   - Injects 6 stage IDs to `plan["queue_order"]`: `triage::observe`, `triage::reflect`, `triage::organize`, `triage::enrich`, `triage::sense-check`, `triage::commit`
   - Freezes active triage issue IDs in `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["active_triage_issue_ids"]`

2. **Per-stage recording** (observe, reflect, organize, enrich, sense-check):
   - User submits report via CLI flag `--report "..."`
   - Stage validates and parses report (e.g., reflect parses Coverage Ledger)
   - Calls `record_[stage]_stage()` to persist to `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"][stage_name]`
   - Cascades clear confirmations on downstream stages
   - Returns cleared stages to inform user

3. **Confirmation flow**:
   - User runs `desloppify plan triage --confirm <stage_name>` to mark `confirmed_at` and `confirmed_text`
   - Can skip confirmation with `--attestation` (30+ char override)
   - Next stage cannot proceed until prior stage is recorded AND confirmed (or attested)

4. **Triage completion** (`triage::commit` stage):
   - Archives `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"]` to `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["last_triage"]`
   - Clears `active_triage_issue_ids` via `clear_active_triage_tracking()`
   - Updates `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triaged_ids"]` with all reviewed issue IDs
   - Removes triage stage IDs from queue

---

### 7. **How Stages Pass Data to Each Other**

**Files**:
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_prompts.py` (prompt builders)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/orchestrator_codex_pipeline_context.py` (context models)

**Data flow**:

**Observe → Reflect**:
- Reflect reads `stages["observe"]["report"]` and `stages["observe"]["assessments"]` from live plan
- Prompt builder includes formatted assessments table (hash | verdict | recommendation) via `_format_assessments_table()`
- Also includes file evidence via `_format_assessment_file_evidence()`
- Reflect must cite every open issue hash exactly once in a "## Coverage Ledger" section

**Reflect → Organize**:
- Organize reads `stages["reflect"]["report"]` 
- Parses `stages["reflect"]["disposition_ledger"]` (structured ReflectDisposition objects with issue_id, decision, target)
- Validates cluster count/enrichment against reflect's ledger
- Must reference all manual clusters created since reflect in the organize report

**Organize → Enrich**:
- Enrich reads `stages["organize"]["report"]` and cluster list
- Validates action_steps exist and are enriched (have detail, effort, issue_refs)
- Validates steps reference only live triage issues

**Enrich → Sense-check**:
- Sense-check reads `stages["enrich"]["report"]`
- Re-validates step quality (no vague detail, all paths exist, all steps have effort)
- Final sanity check before completion

**Prompt builders** assemble context via `StageRunContext`:
```python
StageRunContext(
    stage=stage_name,
    plan=plan,
    triage_input=triage_input,  # TriageInput with issues, clusters, etc.
    prior_reports={stage_name: report_text},  # all prior stage reports
    ...
)
```

The prior_reports dict is loaded from `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"][stage_name]["report"]` via `load_prior_reports_from_plan()`.

---

### 8. **Complete State Topology Diagram**

```
plan.json
├── queue_order: [issue_ids, triage::observe, triage::reflect, ...]  ← work queue seed
├── skipped: {issue_id: SkipEntry}
├── overrides: {issue_id: ItemOverride}
├── clusters: {name: Cluster}
│   ├── "manual-cluster-1": {issue_ids: [...], action_steps: [...], ...}
│   └── "epic/auto-cluster-1": {thesis, direction, dismissed, agent_safe, ...}
├── epic_triage_meta: {
│   ├── triaged_ids: [all reviewed issue IDs from past triage runs]
│   ├── active_triage_issue_ids: [frozen set for current triage run]
│   ├── dismissed_ids: [issues dismissed by prior triage]
│   ├── version: int (increments per triage run)
│   ├── last_completed_at: timestamp (for filtering recent completed clusters)
│   ├── triage_stages: {
│   │   ├── "observe": {
│   │   │   ├── report: "full narrative text...",
│   │   │   ├── assessments: [{hash, verdict, files_read, ...}],
│   │   │   ├── cited_ids: [review::abc123, ...],
│   │   │   ├── confirmed_at: timestamp (when user confirmed),
│   │   │   └── confirmed_text: "user's rationale"
│   │   ├── "reflect": {
│   │   │   ├── report: "coverage ledger + narrative...",
│   │   │   ├── disposition_ledger: [{issue_id, decision, target}, ...],
│   │   │   ├── recurring_dims: [dimension_name, ...],
│   │   │   └── confirmed_at: timestamp
│   │   ├── "organize": {
│   │   │   ├── report: "cluster enrichment validation...",
│   │   │   └── confirmed_at: timestamp
│   │   ├── "enrich": {
│   │   │   ├── report: "step enrichment results...",
│   │   │   └── confirmed_at: timestamp
│   │   └── "sense-check": {
│   │       ├── report: "final sanity check results...",
│   │       └── confirmed_at: timestamp
│   ├── last_triage: {
│   │   ├── completed_at: timestamp (when full triage finished),
│   │   ├── stages: {...archived triage_stages...},
│   │   └── strategy: "high-level summary"
│   └── triage_stages (persisted before completion)
└── completed_clusters: [{name, thesis, issue_ids, completed_at}, ...]

state.json
├── issues: {
│   ├── "review::abc123def456": {
│   │   ├── detector: "review",
│   │   ├── status: "open",
│   │   ├── summary: "...",
│   │   ├── detail: {
│   │   │   ├── dimension: "dimension-name",
│   │   │   └── ...other metric-specific fields
│   │   ├── file: "path/to/file.py",
│   │   └── line: 123
│   └── "concerns::xyz789...": {...}
├── dimension_scores: {dimension: score, ...}  ← for context
└── ...other state fields
```

---

### 9. **Critical Connectors & Flow**

**Issue → Cluster membership**:
- Issues originate in `state["issues"]`
- Reflect stage's disposition_ledger maps each issue to a target cluster
- Organize validates that manual clusters have been created for all targets
- Enrich enriches clusters' action_steps with implementation detail
- Plan tracks via `cluster["issue_ids"]` which is a list of issue IDs

**Stage→Stage data passing**:
1. Read prior report text from `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"][stage]["report"]`
2. For reflect: parse disposition_ledger (structured) + recurring dimensions
3. For organize: validate cluster count/enrichment matches reflect ledger
4. Load full `TriageInput` (open_issues, existing_clusters, resolved_issues) for context
5. Prompt builders inject both unstructured (prior reports) and structured (TriageInput) data

**Queue maintenance**:
- `plan["queue_order"]` is the canonical ordered list of work
- Triage stages appear as synthetic IDs `triage::stage-name`
- Work queue is computed from queue_order + cluster memberships + skipped entries
- When triage completes, stage IDs are removed from queue_order

---

## Comprehensive Report: plan.json and Review Issue State Architecture

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's the complete data flow and structural relationship between plan.json, review issue state, and the triage system:

---

### 1. plan.json Schema Structure

**File Location**: `.desloppify/plan.json`
**Schema Definition**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema/__init__.py`
**Current Version**: v8 (defined by `PLAN_VERSION = 8`)

**Top-Level Keys** in `PlanModel` TypedDict:
- `version: int` — Plan schema version
- `created: str` — ISO timestamp when plan created
- `updated: str` — Last update timestamp
- `queue_order: list[str]` — Prioritized issue/stage IDs to work on
- `deferred: list[str]` — Legacy (kept empty for compat)
- `skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]` — Issues explicitly skipped with metadata
- `active_cluster: str | None` — Currently focused cluster name
- `overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]` — User manual tweaks to issues
- `clusters: dict[str, Cluster]` — Named work groupings
- `superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]` — Remapped/dismissed issues
- `promoted_ids: list[str]` — IDs user explicitly repositioned
- `plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores` — Score snapshot when triage started
- `previous_plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores` — Prior cycle's scores
- `refresh_state: RefreshState` — Postflight lifecycle phase metadata
- `execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]` — Timestamped action audit
- `epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta` — **Central triage state machine** (see below)
- `subjective_defer_meta: dict[str, Any]` — Deferred review tracking
- `commit_log: list[CommitRecord]` — Git commit tracking
- `uncommitted_issues: list[str]` — Issues mentioned in commits
- `commit_tracking_branch: str | None` — Git branch context

---

### 2. epic_triage_meta: The Triage State Machine

**Type**: `EpicTriageMeta` (from schema/__init__.py)

This is the **nucleus of the staged triage workflow**. Keys:

```python
{
    "triaged_ids": list[str]           # Review issue IDs processed in this triage cycle
    "active_triage_issue_ids": list[str]  # Frozen set of review IDs for current triage run
    "dismissed_ids": list[str]         # Issues dismissed/triaged_out in this cycle
    "undispositioned_issue_ids": list[str]  # Issues still lacking cluster/skip placement
    "undispositioned_issue_count": int  # Count of ^
    "issue_snapshot_hash": str         # Integrity hash of review issues at triage start
    "strategy_summary": str            # User/LLM's meta-plan narrative (2-4 sentences)
    "trigger": str                     # "manual" | "confirm_existing"
    "version": int                     # Incrementing triage cycle number
    "last_run": str                    # ISO timestamp of last triage run
    "last_completed_at": str           # When triage fully completed
    "triage_stages": dict[str, TriageStagePayload]  # **Stage checkpoint data** (see below)
    "stage_snapshot_hash": str         # Hash of triage_stages for staleness detection
    "stage_refresh_required": bool     # Flag: stages need re-execution
    "last_triage": LastTriageSnapshot  # Archived prior triage with completed_at, stages, strategy
    "triage_defer_state": dict[str, Any]  # Deferral scheduling metadata
}
```

**TriageStagePayload** (each stage has one):
```python
{
    "stage": str                       # "observe" | "reflect" | "organize" | "enrich" | "sense-check" | "commit"
    "report": str                      # Agent's narrative analysis text
    "cited_ids": list[str]            # Issue IDs explicitly cited in report (from regex parsing)
    "timestamp": str                   # When stage was recorded
    "issue_count": int                 # Number of issues analyzed
    "recurring_dims": list[str]        # Dimensions with both open+resolved issues
    "confirmed_at": str                # ISO timestamp when user confirmed stage (optional)
    "confirmed_text": str              # User's confirmation narrative (optional)
    
    # OBSERVE stage only:
    "assessments": list[dict]          # [{hash, verdict, verdict_reasoning, files_read, recommendation}, ...]
    
    # REFLECT stage only:
    "disposition_ledger": list[ReflectDisposition]  # [{issue_id, decision, target}, ...]
    "cluster_blueprint": list[ReflectClusterBlueprint]  # [{name, description, priority_order, depends_on}, ...]
}
```

---

### 3. Review Issue State Structure

**File Location**: `.desloppify/state-{lang}.json`
**Schema Definition**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/schema_types_issues.py`

**Issue TypedDict** (central entity):
```python
{
    "id": str                          # Full issue ID (e.g., "review::abc123def456")
    "detector": str                    # "review" | "concerns" | "structural" | ... (26+ types)
    "file": str                        # Relative path
    "tier": int                        # 1=auto-fix, 2=quick-fix, 3=judgment, 4=major-refactor
    "confidence": str                  # "high" | "medium" | "low"
    "summary": str                     # Issue title
    "detail": dict[str, Any]           # Detector-specific fields:
                                       #   review: {holistic?, dimension?, suggestion?, evidence?, ...}
                                       #   concerns: {reasoning?, ...}
    "status": Status                   # "open" | "fixed" | "wontfix" | "false_positive" | "auto_resolved"
    "note": str | None                 # User annotation
    "first_seen": str                  # ISO timestamp
    "last_seen": str                   # ISO timestamp
    "resolved_at": str | None          # ISO when marked resolved (ONLY set when status != "open")
    "reopen_count": int                # Reopenings after prior fixes
    
    # Suppression (ignore patterns)
    "suppressed": bool (optional)      # True if matched an ignore pattern
    "suppressed_at": str | None        # When suppressed
    "suppression_pattern": str | None  # Pattern that suppressed it
    
    # Resolution attestation
    "resolution_attestation": dict[str, str | bool | None]  # {pattern, attestation, command, ...}
    
    "lang": str (optional)             # Language (auto-detected on load)
    "zone": str (optional)             # "production" | "test" | "config" | ... (policy-based)
}
```

**Key Status Transitions**:
- **"open"** → Issue first discovered, waiting for action
- **"open" → "wontfix"** — User skips with `--permanent` (requires attestation)
- **"open" → "false_positive"** — User resolves with reason + attestation
- **"open" → "fixed"** — User marks done or agent auto-resolves
- **"fixed" → "open"** — Reopen if same issue resurfaces in next scan
- **"fixed" → "auto_resolved"** — System marks fixed if no longer in scan output

**No Status Change in plan.json**: Issues remain "open" in state throughout triage. The **plan.json** controls disposition via:
- `clusters[name]["issue_ids"]` — Issues assigned to this cluster (actively being worked)
- `skipped[id]` — Issues marked temporary/permanent/false_positive
- `epic_triage_meta["dismissed_ids"]` — Issues rejected by triage ("triaged_out")

---

### 4. Skip/Dismiss Mechanism

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations/skip.py`

**SkipEntry** schema (one per skipped issue in `plan["skipped"]`):
```python
{
    "issue_id": str                   # Required
    "kind": str                       # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive" | "triaged_out"
    "reason": str | None              # Why it's skipped
    "note": str | None                # Additional context
    "attestation": str | None         # Required for permanent/false_positive (required 40+ chars)
    "created_at": str                 # ISO when skipped
    "review_after": int | None        # For temporary: re-surface after N scans
    "skipped_at_scan": int            # state.scan_count when skipped
}
```

**Skip Flow**:
1. User runs: `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."`
2. `skip_items()` in operations/skip.py:
   - Removes issue from `queue_order` list
   - Removes from clusters if present
   - Creates SkipEntry in `plan["skipped"][issue_id]`
   - **Does NOT change issue.status in state** — remains "open"
3. Plan persisted to disk via `plan_lock()` atomic write + `.json.bak` backup

**Organize Stage Dismiss**:
- When triage organizes and dismisses an issue, `dismiss_triage_issues()` (dismiss.py) creates a `SkipEntry` with `kind="triaged_out"` and moves it to `plan["skipped"]`
- These are **not removed from state** — state still tracks them as open
- **Issue status changes only when user explicitly resolves**: `plan resolve <id> --attest "..."` → sets `issue.status="fixed"` in state

---

### 5. collect_triage_input: Data Collection

**Function**: `collect_triage_input(plan, state)` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/prompt.py`

**Returns** `TriageInput` dataclass:
```python
{
    "open_issues": dict[str, dict]       # state["issues"] filtered: detector in ("review", "concerns"), status="open"
    "mechanical_issues": dict[str, dict]  # Non-review open issues (for context)
    "existing_clusters": dict[str, Cluster]  # plan["clusters"] filtered: name.startswith("epic/")
    "dimension_scores": dict[str, Any]   # state["dimension_scores"] (health scores per dimension)
    "new_since_last": set[str]           # open_review_ids - prior_triaged_ids
    "resolved_since_last": set[str]      # prior_triaged_ids - open_review_ids
    "previously_dismissed": list[str]    # plan["epic_triage_meta"]["dismissed_ids"]
    "triage_version": int                # meta["version"] + 1
    "resolved_issues": dict[str, dict]   # Full issue dicts for resolved IDs (for recurrence context)
    "completed_clusters": list[dict]     # Clusters completed since last triage
}
```

**Data Flow**:
1. Splits state issues into review (detector="review"|"concerns", status="open") vs mechanical
2. Compares current open_review_ids against `epic_triage_meta["triaged_ids"]` to find new/resolved
3. Pulls `last_completed_at` from meta to filter `completed_clusters`

---

### 6. Observe Assessments → Organize

**Observe Stage** (`app/commands/plan/triage/stages/observe.py`):

1. Agent submits report with per-issue verdicts (YAML template format):
   ```yaml
   - hash: abc12345
     verdict: genuine
     verdict_reasoning: "found actual issue in code"
     files_read: [src/foo.ts, src/bar.ts]
     recommendation: "refactor that function"
   ```

2. `parse_observe_evidence()` in evidence_parsing.py:
   - Extracts issue hash → `ObserveAssessment` dataclass
   - Normalizes verdict: "genuine" | "false positive" | "exaggerated" | "not-worth-it"
   - Validates against valid_ids (set of open review issues)

3. Assessments stored in plan: `triage_stages["observe"]["assessments"]` (list of dicts):
   ```python
   [
     {
       "hash": "abc12345",
       "verdict": "genuine",
       "verdict_reasoning": "...",
       "files_read": ["src/foo.ts"],
       "recommendation": "..."
     },
     ...
   ]
   ```

4. **No state mutation yet** — assessments live only in plan.json

**Organize Stage** (`app/commands/plan/triage/stages/organize.py`):

- **Reads assessments from plan.json** (indirectly: the report text is displayed, but structured assessments aren't directly consumed)
- Validates:
  - All manual clusters exist and have issues
  - All clusters enriched with action_steps
  - No unclustered review issues remain
  - Cluster operations logged since reflect stage (validates manual work)
- **Records stage**: `triage_stages["organize"]` with report text
- **Does NOT immediately resolve issues** — clustering is purely in plan

---

### 7. Confirmation Flow

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/completion.py`

**Stage Hierarchy** (each must be confirmed before next):
1. **Observe** — Agent analyses issues (creates assessments)
2. **Reflect** → Auto-confirmed from organize if no changes since last reflect
3. **Organize** → User enriches clusters manually
4. **Enrich** — User adds action_steps to clusters
5. **Sense-Check** — Final validation before completion
6. **Complete** — Finalize and remove triage stage IDs from queue

**Confirmation Mechanism**:

Each stage record has optional fields:
- `confirmed_at: str` — ISO timestamp when user confirmed
- `confirmed_text: str` — User's approval statement

When stage is recorded (e.g., `record_organize_stage()`):
- New submission: clears `confirmed_at`/`confirmed_text`
- Reuse (existing report): preserves prior confirmation
- **`cascade_clear_later_confirmations()`**: if a stage is re-run, clears confirmations of all later stages

**apply_completion()** final action:
1. Freezes coverage: `triaged_ids` ← current open_review_ids in clusters + skipped
2. Archives stages: `last_triage` ← snapshot of all stages + metadata
3. Clears `triage_stages: {}` (wipes in-progress stage data)
4. Purges triage IDs from queue: removes `triage::*` and `workflow::*` from `queue_order`
5. Saves plan atomically

**NO automatic status updates in state** — confirmation locks the plan structure, but review issues remain "open" in state until user explicitly resolves them.

---

```
STATE (.desloppify/state-{lang}.json)
  └─ issues:
       └─ review::abc123
             status: "open"
             detail: {dimension: "modularity", suggestion: "..."}

PLAN (.desloppify/plan.json)

  ├─ clusters:
  │    epic/modularity:

       triage_stages:

           assessments: [{hash: abc123, verdict: genuine, ...}]
           confirmed_at: "2026-03-12T..."
         organize:
           report: "Cluster plan confirmed..."
           timestamp: "2026-03-12T..."
       last_triage:
         completed_at: "2026-03-11T..."
         stages: {observe: {...}, ...}
```

### 10. Status Lifecycle Summary

**In state.json**:
- Issue lives in `state["issues"][id]` with `status` field
- Only status changes: "open" ↔ "fixed"/"wontfix"/"false_positive"/"auto_resolved"
- **During triage planning**: status STAYS "open" (no change)

**In plan.json**:
- **Triage decides disposition** (not status):
  - Cluster assignment: `clusters[name]["issue_ids"]` ← issue is "under active work"
  - Skip: `skipped[id]` with kind + attestation ← issue is "dismissed"
  - Dismissed: `epic_triage_meta["dismissed_ids"]` ← issue is "triaged_out"
- **Confirmation locks structure**: stages transition from pending→confirmed
- **Completion clears triage**: stages archived to `last_triage`, queue normalized

**When user executes plan**:
- `desloppify plan resolve <id> --attest "..."` → sets `state["issues"][id]["status"]="fixed"` + `resolved_at` timestamp
- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --attest "..."` → sets `plan["skipped"][id]` (state unchanged)

    34→    if "observe" not in stages:
    35→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
    36→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    37→        return None
    38→
    39→    triage_input = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    40→    if not auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
    41→        plan=plan,
    42→        stages=stages,
    43→        attestation=attestation,
    44→        triage_input=triage_input,
    45→        save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
    46→    ):
    47→        return None
    48→
    49→    issue_count = len(triage_input.open_issues)
    50→    if not validate_stage_report_length(
    51→        report=report,
    52→        issue_count=issue_count,
    53→        guidance="  Describe how current issues relate to previously completed work.",
    54→    ):
    55→        return None
    56→
    57→    recurring = services.detect_recurring_patterns(
    58→        triage_input.open_issues,
    59→        triage_input.resolved_issues,
    60→    )
    61→    recurring_dims = sorted(recurring.keys())
    62→    if not _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
    63→        report=report,
    64→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
    65→        recurring=recurring,
    66→    ):
    67→        return None
    68→
    69→    valid_ids = set(triage_input.open_issues.keys())
    70→    accounting_ok, cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = validate_reflect_accounting(
    71→        report=report,
    72→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
    73→    )
    74→    if not accounting_ok:
    75→        return None
    76→
    77→    from .evidence_parsing import (
    78→        format_evidence_failures,
    79→        validate_reflect_skip_evidence,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→    blocking_skips = [
    83→        failure
    84→        for failure in validate_reflect_skip_evidence(report)
    85→        if failure.blocking
    86→    ]
    87→    if blocking_skips:
    88→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(blocking_skips, stage_label="reflect"), "red"))
    89→        return None
    90→
    91→    # Parse structured disposition ledger from Coverage Ledger section
    92→    disposition_ledger = parse_reflect_dispositions(report, valid_ids)
    93→
    94→    return (
    95→        triage_input,
    96→        issue_count,
    97→        recurring,
    98→        recurring_dims,
    99→        cited_ids,
   100→        missing_ids,
   101→        duplicate_ids,
   102→        disposition_ledger,
   103→    )
   104→
   105→
   106→def _persist_reflect_stage(
   107→    *,
   108→    plan: dict,
   109→    meta: dict,
   110→    stages: dict,
   111→    report: str,
   112→    issue_count: int,
   113→    cited_ids: set[str],
   114→    missing_ids: list[str],
   115→    duplicate_ids: list[str],
   116→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   117→    disposition_ledger: list[ReflectDisposition],
   118→    existing_stage: dict | None,
   119→    is_reuse: bool,
   120→    services: TriageServices,
   121→) -> tuple[dict, list[str]]:
   122→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   123→    reflect_stage: dict = {
   124→        "stage": "reflect",
   125→        "report": report,
   126→        "cited_ids": sorted(cited_ids),
   127→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   128→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   129→        "missing_issue_ids": missing_ids,
   130→        "duplicate_issue_ids": duplicate_ids,
   131→        "recurring_dims": recurring_dims,
   132→    }
   133→    if disposition_ledger:
   134→        reflect_stage["disposition_ledger"] = [d.to_dict() for d in disposition_ledger]
   135→    stages["reflect"] = reflect_stage
   136→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   137→        reflect_stage["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   138→        reflect_stage["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   139→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "reflect")
   140→
   141→    services.save_plan(plan)
   142→    services.append_log_entry(
   143→        plan,
   144→        "triage_reflect",
   145→        actor="user",
   146→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "reuse": is_reuse, "recurring_dims": recurring_dims},
   147→    )
   148→    services.save_plan(plan)
   149→    return reflect_stage, cleared
   150→
   151→
   152→def _cmd_stage_reflect(
   153→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   154→    *,
   155→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   156→) -> None:
   157→    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current issues against completed work."""
   158→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   159→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   160→
   161→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   162→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   163→    state = runtime.state
   164→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   165→
   166→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   167→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to reflect on.", "yellow"))
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   171→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   172→    existing_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   173→
   174→    report, is_reuse = resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   175→    if not report:
   176→        _print_reflect_report_requirement()
   177→        return
   178→
   179→    submission = _validate_reflect_submission(
   180→        report=report,
   181→        plan=plan,
   182→        state=state,
   183→        stages=stages,
   184→        attestation=attestation,
   185→        services=resolved_services,
   186→    )
   187→    if submission is None:
   188→        return
   189→    (
   190→        triage_input, issue_count, recurring, recurring_dims,
   191→        cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids, disposition_ledger,
   192→    ) = submission
   193→    reflect_stage, cleared = _persist_reflect_stage(
   194→        plan=plan,
   195→        meta=meta,
   196→        stages=stages,
   197→        report=report,
   198→        issue_count=issue_count,
   199→        cited_ids=cited_ids,
   200→        missing_ids=missing_ids,
   201→        duplicate_ids=duplicate_ids,
   202→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   203→        disposition_ledger=disposition_ledger,
   204→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   205→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   206→        services=resolved_services,
   207→    )
   208→
   209→    print_reflect_result(
   210→        issue_count=issue_count,
   211→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   212→        recurring=recurring,
   213→        report=report,
   214→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   215→        cleared=cleared,
   216→        stages=stages,
   217→    )
   218→
   219→
   220→def cmd_stage_reflect(
   221→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   222→    *,
   223→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   224→) -> None:
   225→    """Public entrypoint for reflect stage recording."""
   226→    _cmd_stage_reflect(args, services=services)
   227→
   228→
   229→__all__ = ["_cmd_stage_reflect", "cmd_stage_reflect"]
   230→

    29→def _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
    30→    *,
    31→    plan: dict,

    33→    manual_clusters: list[str],

     2→

     4→

     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root

    33→
    34→def _required_issue_hashes(triage_input: TriageInput) -> list[str]:
    35→    """Return sorted short hashes for open review issues."""
    36→    return sorted(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in triage_input.open_issues)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _compact_issue_summary(triage_input: TriageInput) -> str:
    40→    """Return a compact issue summary for later triage stages."""
    41→    by_dim: Counter[str] = Counter()
    42→    for issue in triage_input.open_issues.values():
    43→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    44→        by_dim[str(detail.get("dimension", "unknown"))] += 1
    45→    dims = ", ".join(f"{name} ({count})" for name, count in sorted(by_dim.items()))
    46→    parts = [
    47→        "## Issue Summary",
    48→        f"Open review issues: {len(triage_input.open_issues)}",
    49→    ]
    50→    if dims:
    51→        parts.append(f"Open dimensions: {dims}")
    52→    if triage_input.new_since_last:
    53→        parts.append(f"New since last triage: {len(triage_input.new_since_last)}")
    54→    if triage_input.resolved_since_last:
    55→        parts.append(f"Resolved since last triage: {len(triage_input.resolved_since_last)}")
    56→
    57→    # Cluster summary if clusters exist
    58→    existing = triage_input.existing_clusters or {}
    59→    if existing:
    60→        parts.append("")
    61→        parts.append("## Clusters")
    62→        for cname, cdata in existing.items():
    63→            desc = cdata.get("description") or ""
    64→            count = len(cdata.get("issue_ids", []))
    65→            desc_str = f" ({desc})" if desc else ""
    66→            parts.append(f"- {cname}: {count} issues{desc_str}")
    67→
    68→    return "\n".join(parts)
    69→
    70→
    71→_INVESTIGATION_COMMANDS = """\
    72→
    73→## Available Investigation Commands
    74→
    75→To see full detail for a specific issue:
    76→  desloppify show <issue-id-or-hash-pattern> --no-budget
    77→To see all open review issues with suggestions:
    78→  desloppify show review --status open --no-budget
    79→To see a cluster's steps, members, and suggestions:
    80→  desloppify plan cluster show <name>
    81→To list all clusters:
    82→  desloppify plan cluster list --verbose"""
    83→
    84→
    85→def _issue_context_for_stage(
    86→    stage: str,
    87→    triage_input: TriageInput,
    88→    mode: PromptMode = "self_record",
    89→) -> str:
    90→    """Return the amount of issue context appropriate for a stage."""
    91→    if stage in {"observe", "reflect"}:
    92→        parts = ["## Issue Data\n\n" + build_triage_prompt(triage_input)]
    93→        if stage == "reflect":
    94→            short_ids = _required_issue_hashes(triage_input)
    95→            parts.append(
    96→                "## Required Issue Hashes\n"
    97→                f"Total open review issues: {len(short_ids)}\n"
    98→                "Every one of these hashes must appear exactly once in your cluster/skip blueprint.\n"
    99→                "Do not repeat hashes outside that blueprint.\n"
   100→                + ", ".join(short_ids)
   101→            )
   102→            parts.append(
   103→                "## Coverage Ledger Template\n"
   104→                "Your final report MUST contain a `## Coverage Ledger` section with one line per issue.\n"
   105→                "Allowed forms:\n"
   106→                '- `- abcd1234 -> cluster "cluster-name"`\n'
   107→                '- `- abcd1234 -> skip "specific-reason-tag"`\n'
   108→                "Do not mention hashes outside the `## Coverage Ledger` section.\n"
   109→                + "\n".join(f"- {short_id} -> TODO" for short_id in short_ids)
   110→            )
   111→        return "\n\n".join(parts)
   112→    summary = _compact_issue_summary(triage_input)
   113→    if mode == "self_record":
   114→        summary += _INVESTIGATION_COMMANDS
   115→    return summary
   116→
   117→
   118→def _format_assessments_table(assessments: list[dict]) -> str:
   119→    """Format structured observe assessments as a readable table for downstream stages."""
   120→    if not assessments:
   121→        return ""
   122→    lines = ["### Structured Observe Assessments\n"]
   123→    lines.append("| Hash | Verdict | Recommendation |")
   124→    lines.append("|------|---------|----------------|")
   125→    for a in assessments:
   126→        h = a.get("hash", "?")
   127→        v = a.get("verdict", "?")
   128→        r = a.get("recommendation", "")
   129→        # Truncate recommendation for table readability
   130→        if len(r) > 80:
   131→            r = r[:77] + "..."
   132→        lines.append(f"| {h} | {v} | {r} |")
   133→    return "\n".join(lines)
   134→
   135→
   136→def _format_assessment_file_evidence(assessments: list[dict]) -> str:
   137→    """Format file-level observe evidence for clustering-heavy downstream stages."""
   138→    if not assessments:
   139→        return ""
   140→    lines = ["### Observe File Evidence\n"]
   141→    for assessment in assessments:
   142→        issue_hash = str(assessment.get("hash", "?"))
   143→        files_read = [
   144→            str(path).strip()
   145→            for path in assessment.get("files_read", [])
   146→            if str(path).strip()
   147→        ]
   148→        verdict = str(assessment.get("verdict", "?")).strip() or "?"
   149→        reasoning = str(assessment.get("verdict_reasoning", "")).strip()
   150→        recommendation = str(assessment.get("recommendation", "")).strip()
   151→        lines.append(f"- {issue_hash}: {verdict}")
   152→        if files_read:
   153→            lines.append(f"  files_read: {', '.join(files_read)}")
   154→        if reasoning:
   155→            lines.append(f"  verdict_reasoning: {reasoning}")
   156→        if recommendation:
   157→            lines.append(f"  recommendation: {recommendation}")
   158→    return "\n".join(lines)
   159→
   160→
   161→def _relevant_prior_reports(
   162→    stage: str,
   163→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   164→    stages_data: dict | None = None,
   165→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   166→    """Return the stage reports that matter for the current stage."""
   167→    wanted = {
   168→        "reflect": ("observe",),
   169→        "organize": ("reflect",),
   170→        "enrich": ("organize",),
   171→        "sense-check": ("organize", "enrich"),
   172→    }.get(stage, tuple(prior_reports))
   173→    result = [(name, prior_reports[name]) for name in wanted if name in prior_reports]
   174→
   175→    # Append structured observe assessments for stages that need verdict data
   176→    if stage in {"reflect", "organize", "sense-check"} and stages_data:
   177→        observe_data = stages_data.get("observe", {})
   178→        assessments = observe_data.get("assessments", [])
   179→        if assessments:
   180→            table = _format_assessments_table(assessments)
   181→            if table:
   182→                result.append(("observe-assessments", table))
   183→        if stage == "organize":
   184→            evidence = _format_assessment_file_evidence(assessments)
   185→            if evidence:
   186→                result.append(("observe-evidence", evidence))
   187→
   188→    return result
   189→
   190→
   191→def build_stage_prompt(
   192→    stage: str,
   193→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   194→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   195→    *,
   196→    repo_root: Path,
   197→    mode: PromptMode = "self_record",
   198→    cli_command: str = "desloppify",
   199→    stages_data: dict | None = None,
   200→) -> str:
   201→    """Build a complete subagent prompt for a triage stage."""
   202→    parts: list[str] = []
   203→
   204→    # Preamble
   205→    parts.append(
   206→        triage_prompt_preamble(mode).format(
   207→            stage=stage.upper(),
   208→            repo_root=repo_root,
   209→            cli_command=cli_command,
   210→        )
   211→    )
   212→
   213→    # Prior stage reports
   214→    relevant_prior_reports = _relevant_prior_reports(stage, prior_reports, stages_data)
   215→    if relevant_prior_reports:
   216→        parts.append("## Prior Stage Reports\n")
   217→        for prior_stage, report in relevant_prior_reports:
   218→            parts.append(f"### {prior_stage.upper()} Report\n{report}\n")
   219→
   220→    # Issue data / summary
   221→    parts.append(_issue_context_for_stage(stage, triage_input, mode))
   222→
   223→    # Stage-specific instructions
   224→    instruction_fn = _STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS.get(stage)
   225→    if instruction_fn:
   226→        parts.append(instruction_fn(mode))
   227→
   228→    # CLI reference
   229→    if mode == "self_record":
   230→        parts.append(render_cli_reference(cli_command))
   231→
   232→    # Validation requirements
   233→    parts.append(_validation_requirements(stage))
   234→
   235→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   236→
   237→
   238→def cmd_stage_prompt(
   239→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   240→    *,
   241→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   242→) -> None:
   243→    """Print the current prompt for a triage stage, built from live plan data."""
   244→    stage = args.stage_prompt
   245→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   246→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   247→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   248→    state = runtime.state
   249→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   250→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   251→
   252→    # Extract real prior reports from plan.json
   253→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   254→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   255→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
   256→    for prior_stage in _STAGES:
   257→        if prior_stage == stage:
   258→            break
   259→        report = stages.get(prior_stage, {}).get("report", "")
   260→        if report:
   261→            prior_reports[prior_stage] = report
   262→
   263→    prompt = build_stage_prompt(stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root, stages_data=stages)
   264→    print(prompt)
   265→
   266→
   267→__all__ = [
   268→    "build_observe_batch_prompt",
   269→    "build_sense_check_content_prompt",
   270→    "build_sense_check_structure_prompt",
   271→    "build_stage_prompt",
   272→    "cmd_stage_prompt",
   273→    "_observe_batch_instructions",
   274→    "_validation_requirements",
   275→]
   276→

1→"""Review-coverage helpers for triage planning."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.shared.cluster_membership import cluster_issue_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import open_review_ids
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import Cluster, PlanModel
     9→
    10→from .plan_state_access import (
    11→    ensure_cluster_map,
    12→    ensure_queue_order,
    13→    ensure_skipped_map,
    14→    ensure_triage_meta,

    22→
    23→def open_review_ids_from_state(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    24→    """Return open review IDs from the current state snapshot."""
    25→    return open_review_ids(state)
    26→
    27→
    28→def has_open_review_issues(state: StateModel | dict | None) -> bool:
    29→    """Return True when any open review issues exist."""
    30→    return bool(open_review_ids_from_state(state or {}))
    31→
    32→
    33→def plan_review_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
    34→    """Return review/concerns IDs currently represented in queue_order."""
    35→    return [
    36→        fid
    37→        for fid in ensure_queue_order(plan)
    38→        if not fid.startswith("triage::")
    39→        and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
    40→        and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
    41→    ]
    42→
    43→
    44→def coverage_open_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    45→    """Return the frozen or live open review IDs covered by this triage run."""
    46→    active_ids = normalized_issue_id_list(
    47→        ensure_triage_meta(plan).get(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY)
    48→    )
    49→    if active_ids:
    50→        return set(active_ids)
    51→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    52→    review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    53→    if not has_completed_scan and not review_ids:
    54→        return set(plan_review_ids(plan))
    55→    return review_ids
    56→
    57→
    58→def active_triage_issue_ids(
    59→    plan: PlanModel,
    60→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    61→) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return the frozen review issue set for the current triage run."""
    63→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
    64→    active_ids = normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY))
    65→    if active_ids:
    66→        return set(active_ids)
    67→    if state is None:
    68→        return set()
    69→    return coverage_open_ids(plan, state)
    70→
    71→
    72→def live_active_triage_issue_ids(
    73→    plan: PlanModel,
    74→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    75→) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return frozen triage IDs that are still open review issues in state."""
    77→    frozen_ids = active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    78→    if state is None or not frozen_ids:
    79→        return frozen_ids
    80→    return frozen_ids & open_review_ids(state)
    81→
    82→
    83→def ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    84→    """Freeze the current triage issue set for validation across stage reruns."""
    85→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
    86→    active_ids = sorted(coverage_open_ids(plan, state))
    87→    meta[_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY] = active_ids
    88→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
    89→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
    90→    return active_ids
    91→
    92→
    93→def clear_active_triage_issue_tracking(meta: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    94→    """Clear frozen triage coverage metadata after successful completion."""
    95→    meta.pop(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY, None)
    96→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
    97→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
    98→
    99→
   100→def undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(
   101→    plan: PlanModel,
   102→    state: StateModel | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Return frozen triage issues still lacking cluster/skip/dismiss coverage."""
   105→    target_ids = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   106→    if not target_ids:
   107→        return []
   108→    covered_ids: set[str] = set()
   109→    for cluster in ensure_cluster_map(plan).values():
   110→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   111→            continue
   112→        covered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   113→    covered_ids.update(
   114→        issue_id for issue_id in ensure_skipped_map(plan) if isinstance(issue_id, str)
   115→    )
   116→    covered_ids.update(
   117→        normalized_issue_id_list(ensure_triage_meta(plan).get("dismissed_ids"))
   118→    )
   119→    return sorted(issue_id for issue_id in target_ids if issue_id not in covered_ids)
   120→
   121→
   122→def sync_undispositioned_triage_meta(
   123→    plan: PlanModel,
   124→    state: StateModel | None = None,
   125→) -> list[str]:
   126→    """Persist the current undispositioned triage issue set for recovery UX."""
   127→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
   128→    missing = undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   129→    if missing:
   130→        meta[_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY] = missing
   131→        meta[_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY] = len(missing)
   132→    else:
   133→        meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
   134→        meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
   135→    return missing
   136→
   137→
   138→def triage_coverage(
   139→    plan: PlanModel,
   140→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   141→) -> tuple[int, int, dict[str, Cluster]]:
   142→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage."""
   143→    clusters = ensure_cluster_map(plan)
   144→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
   145→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   146→        all_cluster_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   147→    review_ids = list(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else plan_review_ids(plan)
   148→    organized = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
   149→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   150→
   151→
   152→def manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
   153→    """Return manual clusters that currently own at least one issue."""
   154→    return [
   155→        name
   156→        for name, cluster in ensure_cluster_map(plan).items()
   157→        if cluster_issue_ids(cluster) and not cluster.get("auto")
   158→    ]
   159→
   160→
   161→def find_cluster_for(fid: str, clusters: dict[str, Cluster]) -> str | None:
   162→    """Return the owning cluster name for an issue ID, if any."""
   163→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   164→        if fid in cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   165→            return name
   166→    return None
   167→
   168→
   169→__all__ = [
   170→    "active_triage_issue_ids",

   172→    "cluster_issue_ids",
   173→    "coverage_open_ids",
   174→    "ensure_active_triage_issue_ids",
   175→    "find_cluster_for",
   176→    "has_open_review_issues",
   177→    "live_active_triage_issue_ids",

   181→    "sync_undispositioned_triage_meta",
   182→    "triage_coverage",

   142→def _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
   143→    *,
   144→    report: str,
   145→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   146→    recurring: RecurringDimensionBuckets,
   147→) -> bool:
   148→    if not recurring_dims:
   149→        return True
   150→    report_lower = report.lower()
   151→    mentioned = [dim for dim in recurring_dims if dim.lower() in report_lower]
   152→    if mentioned:
   153→        return True
   154→    print(colorize("  Recurring patterns detected but not addressed in report:", "red"))
   155→    for dim in recurring_dims:
   156→        info = recurring[dim]
   157→        print(
   158→            colorize(
   159→                f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, "
   160→                f"{len(info['open'])} still open — potential loop",
   161→                "yellow",
   162→            )
   163→        )
   164→    print(colorize("  Your report must mention at least one recurring dimension name.", "dim"))
   165→    return False

1→"""Organize-stage validation helpers and reflect-ledger reconciliation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass

     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→
    10→from ..review_coverage import cluster_issue_ids, manual_clusters_with_issues
    11→from ..stages.helpers import unclustered_review_issues, unenriched_clusters
    12→from .reflect_accounting import ReflectDisposition
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class ActualDisposition:
    17→    """What actually happened to an issue in plan state."""
    18→
    19→    kind: Literal["clustered", "skipped", "unplaced"]
    20→    cluster_name: str = ""
    21→
    22→    def describe(self, intended: ReflectDisposition | None = None) -> str:
    23→        """Human-readable description for error messages."""
    24→        if self.kind == "skipped":
    25→            return "permanently skipped"
    26→        if self.kind == "clustered":
    27→            if intended and intended.decision == "cluster" and self.cluster_name != intended.target:
    28→                return f'in cluster "{self.cluster_name}" (expected "{intended.target}")'
    29→            return f'clustered in "{self.cluster_name}"'
    30→        if self.kind == "unplaced":
    31→            if intended and intended.decision == "cluster":
    32→                return "not in any cluster"
    33→            return "not skipped, not clustered"
    34→        return "unknown state"
    35→
    36→
    37→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    38→class LedgerMismatch:
    39→    """One issue where plan state diverges from the reflect disposition."""
    40→
    41→    intended: ReflectDisposition
    42→    actual: ActualDisposition
    43→
    44→    @property
    45→    def issue_id(self) -> str:
    46→        return self.intended.issue_id
    47→
    48→    @property
    49→    def expected_decision(self) -> str:
    50→        return self.intended.decision
    51→
    52→    @property
    53→    def expected_target(self) -> str:
    54→        return self.intended.target
    55→
    56→    @property
    57→    def actual_state(self) -> str:
    58→        return self.actual.describe(self.intended)
    59→
    60→
    61→def _manual_clusters_or_error(
    62→    plan: dict,
    63→    *,
    64→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    65→) -> list[str] | None:
    66→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    67→    if manual_clusters:
    68→        return manual_clusters
    69→    if open_review_ids is not None and not open_review_ids:
    70→        return []
    71→    any_clusters = [
    72→        name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
    73→        if cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    74→    ]
    75→    if any_clusters:
    76→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: only auto-clusters exist.", "red"))
    77→        print(colorize("  Create manual clusters that group issues by root cause:", "dim"))
    78→    else:
    79→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
    80→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
    81→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>", "dim"))
    82→    return None
    83→
    84→
    85→def _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan: dict) -> bool:
    86→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
    87→    if not gaps:
    88→        return True
    89→    print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment.", "red"))
    90→    for name, missing in gaps:
    91→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
    92→    print()
    93→    print(colorize("  Each cluster needs a description and action steps:", "dim"))
    94→    print(
    95→        colorize(
    96→            '    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "what this cluster addresses" --steps "step 1" "step 2"',
    97→            "dim",
    98→        )
    99→    )
   100→    return False
   101→
   102→
   103→def _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   104→    """Block if open review issues aren't in any manual cluster. Return True if OK."""
   105→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   106→    if not unclustered:
   107→        return True
   108→    print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(unclustered)} review issue(s) have no cluster.", "red"))
   109→    for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   110→        short = fid.rsplit("::", 2)[-2] if "::" in fid else fid
   111→        print(colorize(f"    {short}", "yellow"))
   112→    if len(unclustered) > 10:
   113→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "yellow"))
   114→    print()
   115→    print(colorize("  Every review issue needs an action plan. Either:", "dim"))
   116→    print(colorize("    1. Add to a cluster: desloppify plan cluster add <name> <pattern>", "dim"))
   117→    print(
   118→        colorize(
   119→            '    2. Wontfix it: desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "reason" --attest "..."',
   120→            "dim",
   121→        )
   122→    )
   123→    return False
   124→
   125→
   126→def _organize_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   127→    if not report:
   128→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage organize.", "red"))
   129→        print(colorize("  Summarize your prioritized organization:", "dim"))
   130→        print(colorize("  - Did you defer contradictory issues before clustering?", "dim"))
   131→        print(colorize("  - What clusters did you create and why?", "dim"))
   132→        print(colorize("  - Explicit priority ordering: which cluster 1st, 2nd, 3rd and why?", "dim"))
   133→        print(colorize("  - What depends on what? What unblocks the most?", "dim"))
   134→        return None
   135→    if len(report) < 100:
   136→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   137→        print(colorize("  Explain what you organized, your priorities, and focus order.", "dim"))
   138→        return None
   139→    return report
   140→
   141→
   142→def _build_actual_disposition_index(plan: dict) -> dict[str, ActualDisposition]:
   143→    """Build a lookup from issue ID to its actual disposition in plan state."""
   144→    index: dict[str, ActualDisposition] = {}
   145→
   146→    for cluster_name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   147→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   148→            continue
   149→        for fid in cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   150→            index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="clustered", cluster_name=cluster_name)
   151→
   152→    for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}):
   153→        if isinstance(fid, str):
   154→            index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="skipped")
   155→
   156→    return index
   157→
   158→
   159→def _disposition_matches(intended: ReflectDisposition, actual: ActualDisposition) -> bool:
   160→    """True when the actual plan state satisfies the intended disposition."""
   161→    if intended.decision == "permanent_skip":
   162→        return actual.kind == "skipped"
   163→    if intended.decision == "cluster":
   164→        return actual.kind == "clustered" and actual.cluster_name == intended.target
   165→    return False
   166→
   167→
   168→def validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   169→    *,
   170→    plan: dict,
   171→    stages: dict,
   172→) -> list[LedgerMismatch]:
   173→    """Check that plan mutations match the reflect disposition ledger."""
   174→    raw_ledger = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("disposition_ledger")
   175→    if not raw_ledger:
   176→        return []
   177→
   178→    ledger = [ReflectDisposition.from_dict(d) for d in raw_ledger]
   179→    actuals = _build_actual_disposition_index(plan)
   180→    unplaced = ActualDisposition(kind="unplaced")
   181→
   182→    return [
   183→        LedgerMismatch(intended=d, actual=actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
   184→        for d in ledger
   185→        if not _disposition_matches(d, actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
   186→    ]
   187→
   188→
   189→def _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(
   190→    *,
   191→    plan: dict,
   192→    stages: dict,
   193→) -> bool:
   194→    """Block organize if plan state diverges from the reflect disposition ledger."""
   195→    mismatches = validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   196→        plan=plan,
   197→        stages=stages,
   198→    )
   199→    if not mismatches:
   200→        return True
   201→
   202→    print(
   203→        colorize(
   204→            f"  Cannot organize: {len(mismatches)} issue(s) diverge from the reflect plan.",
   205→            "red",
   206→        )
   207→    )
   208→    for mismatch in mismatches[:10]:
   209→        short_id = mismatch.issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
   210→        print(
   211→            colorize(
   212→                f"    {short_id}: reflect said {mismatch.expected_decision} "
   213→                f'"{mismatch.expected_target}", but {mismatch.actual_state}',
   214→                "yellow",
   215→            )
   216→        )
   217→    if len(mismatches) > 10:
   218→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(mismatches) - 10} more", "yellow"))
   219→    print()
   220→    print(
   221→        colorize(
   222→            "  Fix the plan to match the reflect ledger, or re-run reflect to update dispositions.",
   223→            "dim",
   224→        )
   225→    )
   226→    return False
   227→
   228→
   229→__all__ = [
   230→    "ActualDisposition",
   231→    "LedgerMismatch",
   232→    "_clusters_enriched_or_error",
   233→    "_manual_clusters_or_error",
   234→    "_organize_report_or_error",
   235→    "_unclustered_review_issues_or_error",
   236→    "_validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error",
   237→    "validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger",
   238→]
