```
1→"""Reflect-stage coverage-ledger parsing and issue-accounting helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from collections import Counter
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→from typing import Literal
     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import extract_issue_citations
    12→
    13→DecisionKind = Literal["cluster", "permanent_skip"]
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class ReflectDisposition:
    18→    """One issue's intended disposition as declared by the reflect stage."""
    19→
    20→    issue_id: str
    21→    decision: DecisionKind
    22→    target: str
    23→
    24→    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
    25→        """Serialize for JSON persistence in ``plan.json``."""
    26→        return {"issue_id": self.issue_id, "decision": self.decision, "target": self.target}
    27→
    28→    @classmethod
    29→    def from_dict(cls, data: dict | ReflectDisposition) -> ReflectDisposition:
    30→        """Deserialize from persisted plan data, or pass through unchanged."""
    31→        if isinstance(data, cls):
    32→            return data
    33→        issue_id = data.get("issue_id", "")
    34→        target = data.get("target", "")
    35→        decision = _decision_kind_or_none(data.get("decision")) or "cluster"
    36→        return cls(
    37→            issue_id=issue_id if isinstance(issue_id, str) else "",
    38→            decision=decision,
    39→            target=target if isinstance(target, str) else "",
    40→        )
    41→
    42→
    43→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    44→class _IdResolutionMaps:
    45→    """Pre-built lookup structures for resolving ledger tokens to issue IDs."""
    46→
    47→    short_id_buckets: dict[str, list[str]]
    48→    short_hex_map: dict[str, str]
    49→    slug_prefix_map: dict[str, str]
    50→
    51→
    52→def _build_id_resolution_maps(valid_ids: set[str]) -> _IdResolutionMaps:
    53→    short_id_buckets: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    54→    short_hex_map: dict[str, str] = {}
    55→    slug_prefix_map: dict[str, str] = {}
    56→    ambiguous_slugs: set[str] = set()
    57→    for issue_id in sorted(valid_ids):
    58→        parts = issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)
    59→        short_id = parts[-1]
    60→        slug = parts[0] if len(parts) == 2 else ""
    61→        short_id_buckets.setdefault(short_id, []).append(issue_id)
    62→        if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", short_id):
    63→            existing = short_hex_map.get(short_id)
    64→            if existing is None:
    65→                short_hex_map[short_id] = issue_id
    66→            elif existing != issue_id:
    67→                short_hex_map.pop(short_id, None)
    68→        if not slug:
    69→            continue
    70→        if slug in ambiguous_slugs:
    71→            continue
    72→        if slug in slug_prefix_map:
    73→            slug_prefix_map.pop(slug)
    74→            ambiguous_slugs.add(slug)
    75→            continue
    76→        slug_prefix_map[slug] = issue_id
    77→    return _IdResolutionMaps(
    78→        short_id_buckets=short_id_buckets,
    79→        short_hex_map=short_hex_map,
    80→        slug_prefix_map=slug_prefix_map,
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def _clean_ledger_token(raw: str) -> str:
    85→    token = raw.strip().strip("`").strip()
    86→    if token.startswith("[") and token.endswith("]"):
    87→        token = token[1:-1].strip()
    88→    return token
    89→
    90→
    91→_CANONICAL_LEDGER_PATTERNS = (
    92→    re.compile(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*->\s*(\w+)\s+[\"']([^\"']+)[\"']"),
    93→    re.compile(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*->\s*(\w+)\s+(\S+.*)"),
    94→)
    95→_COMPAT_LEDGER_PATTERNS = (
    96→    re.compile(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*:\s*(\w+)\s+[\"']?([^\"']+?)[\"']?\s*$"),
    97→    re.compile(r"-\s*([^,]+),\s*(\w+),\s*[\"']?([^\"',]+?)[\"']?\s*$"),
    98→)
    99→_TOKEN_ONLY_PATTERNS = (
   100→    re.compile(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*->"),
   101→    re.compile(r"-\s+(\S+)\s*$"),
   102→)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _parse_ledger_line_with_patterns(
   106→    line: str,
   107→    patterns: tuple[re.Pattern[str], ...],
   108→) -> tuple[str, str | None, str | None]:
   109→    for pattern in patterns:
   110→        match = pattern.match(line)
   111→        if match:
   112→            [REDACTED](match.group(1))
   113→            return token, match.group(2).strip().lower(), match.group(3).strip().strip("\"'")
   114→    return "", None, None
   115→
   116→
   117→def _parse_token_only_ledger_line(line: str) -> tuple[str, str | None, str | None]:
   118→    for pattern in _TOKEN_ONLY_PATTERNS:
   119→        match = pattern.match(line)
   120→        if not match:
   121→            continue
   122→        [REDACTED](match.group(1))
   123→        if token:
   124→            return token, None, None
   125→    return "", None, None
   126→
   127→
   128→def _extract_ledger_entry(line: str) -> tuple[str, str | None, str | None]:
   129→    """Parse one ledger line into ``(token, decision, target)``."""
   130→    token, decision, target = _parse_ledger_line_with_patterns(line, _CANONICAL_LEDGER_PATTERNS)
   131→    if token:
   132→        return token, decision, target
   133→
   134→    token, decision, target = _parse_ledger_line_with_patterns(line, _COMPAT_LEDGER_PATTERNS)
   135→    if token:
   136→        return token, decision, target
   137→
   138→    return _parse_token_only_ledger_line(line)
   139→
   140→
   141→def _resolve_token_to_id(
   142→    token: str,
   143→    valid_ids: set[str],
   144→    maps: _IdResolutionMaps,
   145→    short_id_usage: Counter[str],
   146→) -> str | None:
   147→    if token in valid_ids:
   148→        return token
   149→    bucket = maps.short_id_buckets.get(token)
   150→    if bucket:
   151→        bucket_index = short_id_usage[token]
   152→        resolved = bucket[bucket_index] if bucket_index < len(bucket) else bucket[-1]
   153→        short_id_usage[token] += 1
   154→        return resolved
   155→    for hex_token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", token):
   156→        resolved = maps.short_hex_map.get(hex_token)
   157→        if resolved:
   158→            return resolved
   159→    return maps.slug_prefix_map.get(token.lower())
   160→
   161→
   162→_CLUSTER_DECISIONS = frozenset({"cluster"})
   163→_SKIP_DECISIONS = frozenset({"skip", "dismiss", "defer", "drop", "remove"})
   164→
   165→
   166→def _normalize_decision(raw: str) -> str:
   167→    lower = raw.lower()
   168→    if lower in _CLUSTER_DECISIONS:
   169→        return "cluster"
   170→    if lower in _SKIP_DECISIONS:
   171→        return "permanent_skip"
   172→    return lower
   173→
   174→
   175→def _decision_kind_or_none(raw: object) -> DecisionKind | None:
   176→    if not isinstance(raw, str):
   177→        return None
   178→    normalized = _normalize_decision(raw)
   179→    if normalized == "cluster":
   180→        return "cluster"
   181→    if normalized == "permanent_skip":
   182→        return "permanent_skip"
   183→    return None
   184→
   185→
   186→@dataclass
   187→class _LedgerParseResult:
   188→    """Combined output of a single pass over the Coverage Ledger section."""
   189→
   190→    hits: Counter[str]
   191→    dispositions: list[ReflectDisposition]
   192→    found_section: bool
   193→
   194→
   195→def _iter_coverage_ledger_lines(report: str) -> tuple[bool, list[str]]:
   196→    found_section = False
   197→    in_ledger = False
   198→    lines: list[str] = []
   199→    for raw_line in report.splitlines():
   200→        line = raw_line.strip()
   201→        if re.fullmatch(r"##\s+Coverage Ledger", line, re.IGNORECASE):
   202→            found_section = True
   203→            in_ledger = True
   204→            continue
   205→        if in_ledger and re.match(r"##\s+", line):
   206→            break
   207→        if in_ledger:
   208→            lines.append(line)
   209→    return found_section, lines
   210→
   211→
   212→def _resolve_ledger_issue_id(
   213→    *,
   214→    token: str,
   215→    line: str,
   216→    valid_ids: set[str],
   217→    maps: _IdResolutionMaps,
   218→    short_id_usage: Counter[str],
   219→) -> str | None:
   220→    issue_id = _resolve_token_to_id(token, valid_ids, maps, short_id_usage)
   221→    if issue_id:
   222→        return issue_id
   223→    for hex_token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", line):
   224→        resolved = maps.short_hex_map.get(hex_token)
   225→        if resolved:
   226→            return resolved
   227→    return None
   228→
   229→
   230→def _append_disposition_if_supported(
   231→    dispositions: list[ReflectDisposition],
   232→    *,
   233→    issue_id: str,
   234→    decision: str | None,
   235→    target: str | None,
   236→) -> None:
   237→    parsed_decision = _decision_kind_or_none(decision)
   238→    if parsed_decision is None or not target:
   239→        return
   240→    dispositions.append(
   241→        ReflectDisposition(
   242→            issue_id=issue_id,
   243→            decision=parsed_decision,
   244→            target=target,
   245→        )
   246→    )
   247→
   248→
   249→def _walk_coverage_ledger(
   250→    report: str,
   251→    valid_ids: set[str],
   252→) -> _LedgerParseResult:
   253→    maps = _build_id_resolution_maps(valid_ids)
   254→    hits: Counter[str] = Counter()
   255→    dispositions: list[ReflectDisposition] = []
   256→    short_id_usage: Counter[str] = Counter()
   257→    found_section, ledger_lines = _iter_coverage_ledger_lines(report)
   258→
   259→    for line in ledger_lines:
   260→        token, decision, target = _extract_ledger_entry(line)
   261→        if not token:
   262→            continue
   263→
   264→        issue_id = _resolve_ledger_issue_id(
   265→            token=token,
   266→            line=line,
   267→            valid_ids=valid_ids,
   268→            maps=maps,
   269→            short_id_usage=short_id_usage,
   270→        )
   271→        if not issue_id:
   272→            continue
   273→        hits[issue_id] += 1
   274→        _append_disposition_if_supported(
   275→            dispositions,
   276→            issue_id=issue_id,
   277→            decision=decision,
   278→            target=target,
   279→        )
   280→
   281→    return _LedgerParseResult(
   282→        hits=hits,
   283→        dispositions=dispositions,
   284→        found_section=found_section,
   285→    )
   286→
   287→
   288→def parse_reflect_dispositions(
   289→    report: str,
   290→    valid_ids: set[str],
   291→) -> list[ReflectDisposition]:
   292→    """Parse structured dispositions from the Coverage Ledger section."""
   293→    return _walk_coverage_ledger(report, valid_ids).dispositions
   294→
   295→
   296→def analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   297→    *,
   298→    report: str,
   299→    valid_ids: set[str],
   300→) -> tuple[set[str], list[str], list[str]]:
   301→    """Return cited, missing, and duplicate issue IDs referenced by reflect."""
   302→    result = _walk_coverage_ledger(report, valid_ids)
   303→    if result.found_section and result.hits:
   304→        cited = set(result.hits)
   305→        duplicates = sorted(issue_id for issue_id, count in result.hits.items() if count > 1)
   306→        missing = sorted(valid_ids - cited)
   307→        return cited, missing, duplicates
   308→
   309→    maps = _build_id_resolution_maps(valid_ids)
   310→    cited = extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
   311→    for issue_id in valid_ids:
   312→        if issue_id in report:
   313→            cited.add(issue_id)
   314→
   315→    short_hits = _collect_legacy_short_hex_hits(report, maps)
   316→    cited.update(short_hits)
   317→
   318→    duplicates = sorted(issue_id for issue_id, count in short_hits.items() if count > 1)
   319→    missing = sorted(valid_ids - cited)
   320→    return cited, missing, duplicates
   321→
   322→
   323→def _collect_legacy_short_hex_hits(
   324→    report: str,
   325→    maps: _IdResolutionMaps,
   326→) -> Counter[str]:
   327→    short_hits: Counter[str] = Counter()
   328→    for token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", report):
   329→        resolved = maps.short_hex_map.get(token)
   330→        if resolved:
   331→            short_hits[resolved] += 1
   332→    return short_hits
   333→
   334→
   335→def validate_reflect_accounting(
   336→    *,
   337→    report: str,
   338→    valid_ids: set[str],
   339→) -> tuple[bool, set[str], list[str], list[str]]:
   340→    """Require the reflect report to account for each open issue exactly once."""
   341→    cited, missing, duplicates = analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   342→        report=report,
   343→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
   344→    )
   345→    if not missing and not duplicates:
   346→        return True, cited, missing, duplicates
   347→
   348→    print(
   349→        colorize(
   350→            "  Reflect report must account for every open review issue exactly once.",
   351→            "red",
   352→        )
   353→    )
   354→    if missing:
   355→        missing_short = ", ".join(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in missing[:10])
   356→        print(colorize(f"    Missing: {missing_short}", "yellow"))
   357→    if duplicates:
   358→        duplicate_short = ", ".join(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in duplicates[:10])
   359→        print(colorize(f"    Duplicated: {duplicate_short}", "yellow"))
   360→    print(colorize("  Fix the reflect blueprint before running organize.", "dim"))
   361→    if missing:
   362→        print(colorize("  Expected format — include a ## Coverage Ledger section:", "dim"))
   363→        print(colorize('    - <hash> -> cluster "cluster-name"', "dim"))
   364→        print(colorize('    - <hash> -> skip "reason"', "dim"))
   365→        print(colorize("  Also accepted: bare hashes, colon-separated, comma-separated.", "dim"))
   366→    return False, cited, missing, duplicates
   367→
   368→
   369→__all__ = [
   370→    "ReflectDisposition",
   371→    "analyze_reflect_issue_accounting",
   372→    "parse_reflect_dispositions",
   373→    "validate_reflect_accounting",
   374→]
   375→
```

```
1→"""Observe stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→

    16→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    17→from .flow_helpers import validate_stage_report_length
    18→from .records import record_observe_stage, resolve_reusable_report
    19→from .rendering import _print_observe_report_requirement
    20→
    21→
    22→def cmd_stage_observe(
    23→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    24→    *,
    25→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    26→    has_triage_in_queue_fn=has_triage_in_queue,
    27→    inject_triage_stages_fn=inject_triage_stages,
    28→) -> None:
    29→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes."""
    30→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    31→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    32→
    33→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    34→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    37→
    38→    if not has_triage_in_queue_fn(plan):
    39→        start_outcome = ensure_triage_started(
    40→            plan,
    41→            services=resolved_services,
    42→            state=state,
    43→            attestation=attestation,
    44→            start_message="  Planning mode auto-started (6 stages queued).",
    45→            deps=TriageLifecycleDeps(
    46→                has_triage_in_queue=has_triage_in_queue_fn,
    47→                inject_triage_stages=inject_triage_stages_fn,
    48→            ),
    49→        )
    50→        if start_outcome.status == "blocked":
    51→            return
    52→
    53→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    54→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    55→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
    56→
    57→    report, is_reuse = resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    58→    if not report:
    59→        _print_observe_report_requirement()
    60→        return
    61→
    62→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    63→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
    64→    if issue_count == 0:
    65→        cleared = record_observe_stage(
    66→            stages,
    67→            report=report,
    68→            issue_count=0,
    69→            cited_ids=[],
    70→            existing_stage=existing_stage,
    71→            is_reuse=is_reuse,
    72→        )
    73→        resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    74→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no issues to analyse).", "green"))
    75→        if is_reuse:
    76→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
    77→            if cleared:
    78→                print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
    79→        return
    80→
    81→    if not validate_stage_report_length(
    82→        report=report,
    83→        issue_count=issue_count,
    84→        guidance="  Describe themes, root causes, contradictions, and how issues relate.",
    85→    ):
    86→        return
    87→
    88→    from .evidence_parsing import (
    89→        format_evidence_failures,
    90→        parse_observe_evidence,
    91→        validate_observe_evidence,
    92→    )
    93→
    94→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
    95→    cited = resolved_services.extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
    96→    evidence = parse_observe_evidence(report, valid_ids)
    97→    evidence_failures = validate_observe_evidence(evidence, issue_count)
    98→    blocking = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if failure.blocking]
    99→    advisory = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if not failure.blocking]
   100→    if blocking:
   101→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(blocking, stage_label="observe"), "red"))
   102→        return
   103→    if advisory:
   104→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(advisory, stage_label="observe"), "yellow"))
   105→
   106→    assessments = [
   107→        {
   108→            "hash": entry.issue_hash,
   109→            "verdict": entry.verdict,
   110→            "verdict_reasoning": entry.verdict_reasoning,
   111→            "files_read": entry.files_read,
   112→            "recommendation": entry.recommendation,
   113→        }
   114→        for entry in evidence.entries
   115→    ]
   116→    cleared = record_observe_stage(
   117→        stages,
   118→        report=report,
   119→        issue_count=issue_count,
   120→        cited_ids=sorted(cited),
   121→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   122→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   123→        assessments=assessments,
   124→    )
   125→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   126→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   127→        plan,
   128→        "triage_observe",
   129→        actor="user",
   130→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "cited_ids": sorted(cited), "reuse": is_reuse},
   131→    )
   132→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   133→
   134→    print(colorize(f"  Observe stage recorded: {issue_count} issues analysed.", "green"))
   135→    if is_reuse:
   136→        print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   137→        if cleared:
   138→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    print(colorize("  Now confirm your analysis.", "yellow"))
   142→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe", "dim"))
   143→    print_user_message(
   144→        "Observe recorded. Before confirming — did the subagent"
   145→        " verify every issue with code reads? Check: are there"
   146→        " specific file/line citations in the report, or just"
   147→        " restated issue titles? Each issue needs a verdict:"
   148→        " genuine / false positive / exaggerated / not-worth-it. Don't confirm"
   149→        " until the analysis is backed by actual code evidence."
   150→    )
   151→
   152→_cmd_stage_observe = cmd_stage_observe
   153→
   154→
   155→__all__ = ["_cmd_stage_observe", "cmd_stage_observe"]
   156→
```

```
     2→

    10→def resolve_reusable_report(
    11→    report: str | None,
    12→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    13→) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    14→    if report:
    15→        return report, False
    16→    if existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
    17→        return existing_stage["report"], True
    18→    return None, False
    19→
    20→
    21→def record_observe_stage(
    22→    stages: dict,
    23→    *,
    24→    report: str,
    25→    issue_count: int,
    26→    cited_ids: list[str],
    27→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    28→    is_reuse: bool,
    29→    assessments: list[dict] | None = None,
    30→) -> list[str]:
    31→    stages["observe"] = {
    32→        "stage": "observe",
    33→        "report": report,
    34→        "cited_ids": cited_ids,
    35→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    36→        "issue_count": issue_count,
    37→    }
    38→    if assessments is not None:
    39→        stages["observe"]["assessments"] = assessments
    40→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    41→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    42→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    43→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
    44→    if not is_reuse:
    45→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    46→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    47→    return cleared
    48→
    49→

    63→        "issue_count": issue_count,

    67→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    68→    return cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "organize")

   100→        "report": report,

   104→        stages["sense-check"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   105→        stages["sense-check"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
```

```
1→"""Structured evidence parsing and validation for triage stage submissions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→
     9→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10→# Shared constants
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→
    13→VERDICT_KEYWORDS = frozenset({
    14→    "genuine",
    15→    "false positive",
    16→    "false-positive",
    17→    "exaggerated",
    18→    "over-engineering",
    19→    "over engineering",
    20→    "not-worth-it",
    21→    "not worth it",
    22→})
    23→
    24→# Permissive path regex — detects whether *any* file path was mentioned.
    25→# NOT used to validate existence (that's enrich-only in validation.enrich_checks).
    26→_EVIDENCE_PATH_RE = re.compile(r"[\w.@-]+/[\w./@-]+\.\w+")
    27→
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→# Data classes
    30→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31→
    32→@dataclass
    33→class EvidenceFailure:
    34→    """A single evidence validation failure."""
    35→
    36→    code: str
    37→    message: str
    38→    blocking: bool = True
    39→
    40→
    41→@dataclass
    42→class ObserveAssessment:
    43→    """A parsed per-issue assessment from an observe report (template format)."""
    44→
    45→    issue_hash: str
    46→    verdict: str  # normalised keyword
    47→    verdict_reasoning: str = ""
    48→    files_read: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    49→    recommendation: str = ""
    50→
    51→
    52→@dataclass
    53→class ObserveEvidence:
    54→    """Parsed observe-report evidence."""
    55→
    56→    entries: list[ObserveAssessment] = field(default_factory=list)
    57→    unparsed_citation_count: int = 0  # hashes cited without a verdict
    58→    has_parseable_ids: bool = True  # False if valid_ids had no hex-hash IDs
    59→
    60→
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→# OBSERVE evidence parsing — structured template format
    63→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    64→
    65→def _normalise_verdict(raw: str) -> str | None:
    66→    """Return a normalised verdict keyword, or None if not recognised."""
    67→    lower = raw.lower().strip()
    68→    for kw in VERDICT_KEYWORDS:
    69→        if kw in lower:
    70→            # Normalise to canonical form
    71→            canonical = kw.replace("-", " ")
    72→            return canonical
    73→    return None
    74→
    75→
    76→def _build_short_map(valid_ids: set[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    77→    """Build short-hash → full-id map from valid issue IDs."""
    78→    short_map: dict[str, str] = {}
    79→    for fid in valid_ids:
    80→        short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
    81→        if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{6,}", short):
    82→            short_map[short] = fid
    83→    return short_map
    84→
    85→
    86→def _is_valid_hash(raw_hash: str, short_map: dict[str, str], valid_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    87→    """Check if a hash is known in either the short map or valid IDs."""
    88→    return raw_hash in short_map or raw_hash in valid_ids
    89→
    90→
    91→# --- YAML-like template parser ---
    92→
    93→# Matches lines like:  - hash: abc12345  or  hash: abc12345
    94→_YAML_HASH_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*-?\s*hash\s*:\s*([0-9a-f]{6,})", re.IGNORECASE)
    95→# Matches lines like:  verdict: genuine  or  verdict: false-positive
    96→_YAML_VERDICT_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*verdict\s*:\s*(.+)", re.IGNORECASE)
    97→# Matches lines like:  verdict_reasoning: some reason
    98→_YAML_REASONING_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*verdict_reasoning\s*:\s*(.+)", re.IGNORECASE)
    99→# Matches lines like:  files_read: [src/foo.ts, src/bar.ts]  or  files_read: src/foo.ts
   100→_YAML_FILES_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*files_read\s*:\s*(.+)", re.IGNORECASE)
   101→# Matches lines like:  recommendation: do something
   102→_YAML_RECOMMENDATION_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*recommendation\s*:\s*(.+)", re.IGNORECASE)
   103→
   104→def _parse_yaml_files_field(raw: str) -> list[str]:
   105→    """Parse the files_read field — supports [a, b] list or single path."""
   106→    raw = raw.strip()
   107→    # Strip surrounding brackets
   108→    if raw.startswith("[") and raw.endswith("]"):
   109→        raw = raw[1:-1]
   110→    # Split on commas
   111→    paths = [p.strip().strip("'\"") for p in raw.split(",")]
   112→    return [p for p in paths if p]
   113→
   114→
   115→def _flush_yaml_entry(
   116→    current: dict,
   117→    short_map: dict[str, str],
   118→    valid_ids: set[str],
   119→) -> ObserveAssessment | None:
   120→    """Convert a collected YAML-like entry dict into an ObserveAssessment, or None if invalid."""
   121→    raw_hash = current.get("hash", "").lower()
   122→    if not raw_hash or not _is_valid_hash(raw_hash, short_map, valid_ids):
   123→        return None
   124→    raw_verdict = current.get("verdict", "")
   125→    verdict = _normalise_verdict(raw_verdict)
   126→    if not verdict:
   127→        return None
   128→    return ObserveAssessment(
   129→        issue_hash=raw_hash,
   130→        verdict=verdict,
   131→        verdict_reasoning=current.get("verdict_reasoning", ""),
   132→        files_read=current.get("files_read", []),
   133→        recommendation=current.get("recommendation", ""),
   134→    )
   135→
   136→
   137→def _parse_yaml_template(
   138→    report: str,
   139→    short_map: dict[str, str],
   140→    valid_ids: set[str],
   141→) -> list[ObserveAssessment]:
   142→    """Parse YAML-like template entries from report text."""
   143→    entries: list[ObserveAssessment] = []
   144→    current: dict | None = None
   145→
   146→    for line in report.splitlines():
   147→        m_hash = _YAML_HASH_RE.match(line)
   148→        if m_hash:
   149→            # Flush previous entry
   150→            if current is not None:
   151→                entry = _flush_yaml_entry(current, short_map, valid_ids)
   152→                if entry:
   153→                    entries.append(entry)
   154→            current = {"hash": m_hash.group(1).lower()}
   155→            continue
   156→
   157→        if current is None:
   158→            continue
   159→
   160→        m_verdict = _YAML_VERDICT_RE.match(line)
   161→        if m_verdict:
   162→            current["verdict"] = m_verdict.group(1).strip()
   163→            continue
   164→
   165→        m_reasoning = _YAML_REASONING_RE.match(line)
   166→        if m_reasoning:
   167→            current["verdict_reasoning"] = m_reasoning.group(1).strip()
   168→            continue
   169→
   170→        m_files = _YAML_FILES_RE.match(line)
   171→        if m_files:
   172→            current["files_read"] = _parse_yaml_files_field(m_files.group(1))
   173→            continue
   174→
   175→        m_rec = _YAML_RECOMMENDATION_RE.match(line)
   176→        if m_rec:
   177→            current["recommendation"] = m_rec.group(1).strip()
   178→            continue
   179→
   180→    # Flush final entry
   181→    if current is not None:
   182→        entry = _flush_yaml_entry(current, short_map, valid_ids)
   183→        if entry:
   184→            entries.append(entry)
   185→
   186→    return entries
   187→
   188→
   189→def parse_observe_evidence(report: str, valid_ids: set[str]) -> ObserveEvidence:
   190→    """Parse assessment entries from an observe-stage report.
   191→
   192→    Supports the YAML-like template format:
   193→    hash/verdict/verdict_reasoning/files_read/recommendation
   194→    """
   195→    short_map = _build_short_map(valid_ids)
   196→    entries = _parse_yaml_template(report, short_map, valid_ids)
   197→
   198→    # Count hashes that appear in report but weren't parsed as entries
   199→    all_hashes_in_report = set(re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", report.lower()))
   200→    valid_short_hashes = set(short_map.keys())
   201→    cited_hashes = all_hashes_in_report & valid_short_hashes
   202→    parsed_hashes = {e.issue_hash for e in entries}
   203→    unparsed = len(cited_hashes - parsed_hashes)
   204→
   205→    return ObserveEvidence(
   206→        entries=entries,
   207→        unparsed_citation_count=unparsed,
   208→        has_parseable_ids=bool(short_map),
   209→    )
   210→
   211→
   212→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   213→# OBSERVE evidence validation — field presence only
   214→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   215→
   216→_OBSERVE_TEMPLATE_HINT = (
   217→    "  Required template per issue:\n"
   218→    "    - hash: <hash>\n"
   219→    "      verdict: genuine | false-positive | exaggerated | over-engineering | not-worth-it\n"
   220→    "      verdict_reasoning: <why this verdict>\n"
   221→    "      files_read: [<file paths>]\n"
   222→    "      recommendation: <what to do>"
   223→)
   224→
   225→
   226→def validate_observe_evidence(
   227→    evidence: ObserveEvidence,
   228→    issue_count: int,
   229→) -> list[EvidenceFailure]:
   230→    """Validate parsed observe evidence — field presence only, no quality thresholds."""
   231→    if issue_count == 0:
   232→        return []
   233→
   234→    # If valid_ids contained no hex-hash IDs, verdict parsing is impossible — skip.
   235→    if not evidence.has_parseable_ids:
   236→        return []
   237→
   238→    failures: list[EvidenceFailure] = []
   239→
   240→    if not evidence.entries:
   241→        failures.append(EvidenceFailure(
   242→            code="no_verdicts",
   243→            message=(
   244→                "No per-issue assessment entries found in report.\n"
   245→                "\n"
   246→                f"{_OBSERVE_TEMPLATE_HINT}\n"
   247→                "\n"
   248→                "  Example:\n"
   249→                "    - hash: 34580232\n"
   250→                "      verdict: false-positive\n"
   251→                "      verdict_reasoning: Uses branded string union KnownTaskType\n"
   252→                "      files_read: [src/types/database.ts]\n"
   253→                "      recommendation: No action needed"
   254→            ),
   255→        ))
   256→        return failures
   257→
   258→    # Check field presence — no quality thresholds
   259→    missing_reasoning: list[ObserveAssessment] = []
   260→    missing_files: list[ObserveAssessment] = []
   261→    missing_recommendation: list[ObserveAssessment] = []
   262→
   263→    for entry in evidence.entries:
   264→        if not entry.verdict_reasoning.strip():
   265→            missing_reasoning.append(entry)
   266→        if not entry.files_read:
   267→            missing_files.append(entry)
   268→        if not entry.recommendation.strip():
   269→            missing_recommendation.append(entry)
   270→
   271→    if missing_reasoning:
   272→        examples = "\n".join(
   273→            f"    [{e.issue_hash}] {e.verdict}" for e in missing_reasoning[:5]
   274→        )
   275→        failures.append(EvidenceFailure(
   276→            code="missing_verdict_reasoning",
   277→            message=(
   278→                f"{len(missing_reasoning)} of {len(evidence.entries)} assessment(s) "
   279→                f"have no verdict_reasoning.\n"
   280→                "\n"
   281→                f"{_OBSERVE_TEMPLATE_HINT}\n"
   282→                "\n"
   283→                f"  Entries missing reasoning:\n{examples}"
   284→            ),
   285→        ))
   286→
   287→    if missing_files:
   288→        examples = "\n".join(
   289→            f"    [{e.issue_hash}] {e.verdict}" for e in missing_files[:5]
   290→        )
   291→        failures.append(EvidenceFailure(
   292→            code="missing_files_read",
   293→            message=(
   294→                f"{len(missing_files)} of {len(evidence.entries)} assessment(s) "
   295→                f"have no files_read.\n"
   296→                "\n"
   297→                f"{_OBSERVE_TEMPLATE_HINT}\n"
   298→                "\n"
   299→                f"  Entries missing files_read:\n{examples}"
   300→            ),
```

```
1→"""Stage-gate and coverage helpers for triage command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_IDS
     7→
     8→from ..review_coverage import cluster_issue_ids, live_active_triage_issue_ids
     9→
    10→
    11→def _require_triage_pending(plan: dict, *, action: str) -> bool:
    12→    """Require at least one triage stage ID to be present in queue for an action."""
    13→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    14→    if order & TRIAGE_IDS:
    15→        return True
    16→    print(colorize(f"  No triage stage in the queue — nothing to {action}.", "yellow"))
    17→    return False
    18→
    19→
    20→def _validate_stage_report(
    21→    report: str | None,
    22→    *,
    23→    stage: str,
    24→    min_chars: int,
    25→    missing_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    26→    short_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    27→) -> str | None:
    28→    """Validate staged report presence/length and print consistent guidance."""
    29→    if not report:
    30→        print(colorize(f"  --report is required for --stage {stage}.", "red"))
    31→        for line in missing_guidance or []:
    32→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    33→        return None
    34→    cleaned = report.strip()
    35→    if len(cleaned) < min_chars:
    36→        print(
    37→            colorize(
    38→                f"  Report too short: {len(cleaned)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).",
    39→                "red",
    40→            )
    41→        )
    42→        for line in short_guidance or []:
    43→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    44→        return None
    45→    return cleaned
    46→
    47→
    48→def unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    49→    """Return clusters with issues that are missing required enrichment.
    50→
    51→    Requirements:
    52→    - Every cluster needs a description and at least one action_step.
    53→    - Small clusters (< 5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue,
    54→      so each item has a concrete plan. Large clusters (>= 5) just need
    55→      steps overall (cluster-level plan is sufficient).
    56→    """
    57→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    58→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    59→        issue_ids = cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    60→        if not issue_ids:
    61→            continue
    62→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    63→            continue
    64→        missing: list[str] = []
    65→        if not cluster.get("description"):
    66→            missing.append("description")
    67→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
    68→        issue_count = len(issue_ids)
    69→        if not steps:
    70→            missing.append("action_steps")
    71→        elif issue_count < 5 and len(steps) < issue_count:
    72→            missing.append(
    73→                f"action_steps (have {len(steps)}, need >= {issue_count} for small cluster)"
    74→            )
    75→        if missing:
    76→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    77→    return gaps
    78→
    79→
    80→def unclustered_review_issues(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> list[str]:
    81→    """Return review issue IDs that aren't in any manual cluster.
    82→
    83→    When *state* is provided, uses open review/concerns issues from state
    84→    (the canonical source). Falls back to scanning queue_order for backwards
    85→    compatibility.
    86→    """
    87→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    88→    clustered_ids: set[str] = set()
    89→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    90→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    91→            clustered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    92→    skipped_ids = {
    93→        fid for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}).keys() if isinstance(fid, str)
    94→    }
    95→
    96→    if state is not None:
    97→        review_ids = [
    98→            fid for fid, finding in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    99→            if finding.get("status") == "open"
   100→            and finding.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
```

```
    20→class AutoConfirmStageRequest:
    21→    """Configuration for one fold-confirm stage auto-confirmation."""
    22→
    23→    stage_name: str
    24→    stage_label: str
    25→    blocked_heading: str
    26→    confirm_cmd: str

    30→
    31→
    32→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    33→class StagePrerequisite:
    34→    """One required upstream stage for a triage workflow seam."""
    35→
    36→    stage_name: str
    37→    require_confirmation: bool = False
    38→
    39→
    40→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    41→class ReflectAutoConfirmDeps:
    42→    """Dependency bundle for reflect auto-confirm flows."""
    43→
    44→    triage_input: object | None = None
    45→    command_runtime_fn: object | None = None
    46→    collect_triage_input_fn: object = collect_triage_input
    47→    detect_recurring_patterns_fn: object = detect_recurring_patterns
    48→    save_plan_fn: object | None = None
    49→
    50→
    51→_STAGE_PREREQUISITES = {
    52→    "organize": (
    53→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    54→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
    55→    ),
    56→    "enrich": (
    57→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    58→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
    59→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    60→    ),
    61→    "sense-check": (
    62→        StagePrerequisite("enrich", require_confirmation=True),
    63→    ),
    64→    "complete:organize": (
    65→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    66→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    67→    ),
    68→    "complete:enrich": (
    69→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    70→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    71→        StagePrerequisite("enrich"),
    72→    ),
    73→    "complete:sense-check": (
    74→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    75→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
    76→        StagePrerequisite("enrich"),
    77→        StagePrerequisite("sense-check"),
    78→    ),
    79→}
    80→
    81→
    82→def missing_stage_prerequisite(
    83→    stages: dict,
    84→    *,
    85→    flow: str,
    86→) -> StagePrerequisite | None:
    87→    """Return the first missing upstream stage required by a triage flow."""
    88→    for prerequisite in _STAGE_PREREQUISITES.get(flow, ()):
    89→        stage_record = stages.get(prerequisite.stage_name)
    90→        if stage_record is None:
    91→            return prerequisite
    92→        if prerequisite.require_confirmation and not stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
    93→            return prerequisite
    94→    return None
    95→
    96→
    97→def require_prerequisite(
    98→    stages: dict,
    99→    *,
   100→    flow: str,
   101→    messages: dict[str, tuple[str, str]],
   102→) -> bool:
   103→    """Print consistent prerequisite guidance for one triage flow."""
   104→    missing = missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow=flow)
   105→    if missing is None:
   106→        return True
   107→    blocked_heading, command_hint = messages[missing.stage_name]
   108→    print(colorize(blocked_heading, "red"))
   109→    print(colorize(command_hint, "dim"))
   110→    return False
   111→
   112→
   113→def confirm_stage(
   114→    *,
   115→    plan: dict,
   116→    stage_record: dict,
   117→    attestation: str | None,
   118→    request: AutoConfirmStageRequest,
   119→    save_plan_fn=None,
   120→    utc_now_fn=None,
   121→) -> bool:
   122→    """Auto-confirm one recorded stage with a validated attestation."""
   123→    resolved_save_plan = save_plan_fn or save_plan
   124→    resolved_utc_now = utc_now_fn or utc_now
   125→    if stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
   126→        return True
   127→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   128→        print(colorize(f"  {request.blocked_heading}", "red"))
   129→        print(colorize(f"  Run: {request.confirm_cmd}", "dim"))
   130→        print(colorize(f"  {request.inline_hint}", "dim"))
   131→        return False
   132→
   133→    confirmed_text = attestation.strip()
   134→    validation_err = validate_attestation(
   135→        confirmed_text,
   136→        request.stage_name,
   137→        dimensions=request.dimensions,
   138→        cluster_names=request.cluster_names,
   139→    )
   140→    if validation_err:
   141→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   142→        return False
   143→
   144→    stage_record["confirmed_at"] = resolved_utc_now()
   145→    stage_record["confirmed_text"] = confirmed_text
   146→    resolved_save_plan(plan)
   147→    print(colorize(f"  ✓ {request.stage_label} auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   148→    return True
   149→
   150→
   151→def auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
   152→    *,
   153→    plan: dict,
   154→    stages: dict,
   155→    attestation: str | None,
   156→    triage_input,
   157→    save_plan_fn=None,
   158→    utc_now_fn=None,
   159→) -> bool:
   160→    """Auto-confirm observe inline when the report already exists."""
   161→    observe_stage = stages.get("observe")
   162→    if observe_stage is None:
   163→        return False
   164→    _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   165→    return confirm_stage(
   166→        plan=plan,
   167→        stage_record=observe_stage,
   168→        attestation=attestation,
   169→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
   170→            stage_name="observe",
   171→            stage_label="Observe",
   172→            blocked_heading="Cannot reflect: observe stage not confirmed.",
   173→            confirm_cmd="desloppify plan triage --confirm observe",
   174→            inline_hint="Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm observe inline.",
   175→            dimensions=dim_names,
   176→        ),
   177→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   178→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   179→    )
   180→
   181→
   182→def auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   183→    *,
   184→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   185→    plan: dict,
   186→    stages: dict,
   187→    attestation: str | None,
   188→    deps: ReflectAutoConfirmDeps | None = None,
   189→    utc_now_fn=None,
   190→) -> bool:
   191→    """Auto-confirm reflect after validating its issue accounting."""
   192→    reflect_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   193→    if reflect_stage is None:
   194→        return False
   195→
   196→    resolved_deps = deps or ReflectAutoConfirmDeps()
   197→    triage_input = resolved_deps.triage_input
   198→    if triage_input is None:
   199→        runtime_factory = resolved_deps.command_runtime_fn or command_runtime
   200→        runtime = runtime_factory(args)
```

```
1→"""Plan-state accessors for triage workflow helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, cast
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import Cluster, PlanModel
     8→
     9→
    10→def ensure_queue_order(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
    11→    """Return queue order, creating the stored list when missing."""
    12→    order = plan.get("queue_order")
    13→    if isinstance(order, list):
    14→        return order
    15→    normalized: list[str] = []
    16→    plan["queue_order"] = normalized
    17→    return normalized
    18→
    19→
    20→def ensure_skipped_map(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Any]:
    21→    """Return skipped metadata, creating the stored map when missing."""
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped")
    23→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
    24→        return cast(dict[str, Any], skipped)
    25→    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    26→    plan["skipped"] = normalized
    27→    return normalized
    28→
    29→
    30→def ensure_cluster_map(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
    31→    """Return cluster map, creating the stored map when missing."""
    32→    clusters = plan.get("clusters")
    33→    if isinstance(clusters, dict):
    34→        return cast(dict[str, Cluster], clusters)
    35→    normalized: dict[str, Cluster] = {}
    36→    plan["clusters"] = normalized
    37→    return normalized
    38→
    39→
    40→def ensure_triage_meta(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Any]:
    41→    """Return triage metadata, creating the stored map when missing."""
    42→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta")
    43→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    44→        return cast(dict[str, Any], meta)
    45→    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    46→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = normalized
    47→    return normalized
    48→
    49→
    50→def ensure_execution_log(plan: PlanModel) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    51→    """Return execution log, creating the stored list when missing."""
    52→    log = plan.get("execution_log")
    53→    if isinstance(log, list):
    54→        normalized = [entry for entry in log if isinstance(entry, dict)]
    55→        if normalized is not log:
    56→            plan["execution_log"] = normalized
    57→        return normalized
    58→    normalized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    59→    plan["execution_log"] = normalized
    60→    return normalized
    61→
    62→
    63→def normalized_issue_id_list(raw_ids: object) -> list[str]:
    64→    """Normalize a raw stored ID collection to strings only."""
    65→    if not isinstance(raw_ids, list):
    66→        return []
    67→    return [issue_id for issue_id in raw_ids if isinstance(issue_id, str)]
    68→
    69→
    70→__all__ = [
    71→    "ensure_cluster_map",
    72→    "ensure_execution_log",
    73→    "ensure_queue_order",
    74→    "ensure_skipped_map",
    75→    "ensure_triage_meta",
    76→    "normalized_issue_id_list",
    77→]
    78→
```

```
    26→def _require_enriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> bool:
    27→    from ..stages.helpers import unenriched_clusters  # noqa: PLC0415
    28→
    29→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
    30→    if not gaps:
    31→        return True
    32→    print(colorize(f"\n  Cannot confirm: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment.", "red"))
    33→    for name, missing in gaps:
    34→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
    35→    print(colorize("  Small clusters (<5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue.", "dim"))
    36→    print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))
    37→    return False
    38→
    39→
    40→def _require_clustered_review_issues(plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
    41→    from ..stages.helpers import unclustered_review_issues  # noqa: PLC0415
    42→
    43→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
    44→    if not unclustered:
    45→        return True
    46→    print(colorize(f"\n  Cannot confirm: {len(unclustered)} review issue(s) have no action plan.", "red"))
    47→    for fid in unclustered[:5]:
    48→        short = fid.rsplit("::", 2)[-2] if "::" in fid else fid
    49→        print(colorize(f"    {short}", "yellow"))
    50→    if len(unclustered) > 5:
    51→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 5} more", "yellow"))
    52→    print(colorize("  Add each to a cluster or wontfix it before confirming.", "dim"))
    53→    return False
    54→
    55→
    56→def _print_reflect_activity_summary(plan: dict, stages: dict) -> None:
    57→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
    58→    if not reflect_ts:
    59→        return
    60→    activity = count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
    61→    if activity:
    62→        print("  Since reflect, you have:")
    63→        for action, count in sorted(activity.items()):
    64→            print(f"    {action}: {count}")
    65→        return
    66→    print("  No logged plan operations since reflect.")
    67→
    68→
    69→def _print_cluster_shape_warnings(plan: dict) -> None:
    70→    from ..validation.core import (  # noqa: PLC0415
    71→        _cluster_file_overlaps,
    72→        _clusters_with_directory_scatter,
    73→        _clusters_with_high_step_ratio,
    74→    )
    75→
    76→    scattered = _clusters_with_directory_scatter(plan)
    77→    if scattered:
    78→        print(colorize(f"\n  Warning: {len(scattered)} cluster(s) span many unrelated directories:", "yellow"))
    79→        for name, dir_count, sample_dirs in scattered:
    80→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {dir_count} directories — likely grouped by theme, not area", "yellow"))
    81→            for directory in sample_dirs[:3]:
    82→                print(colorize(f"      {directory}", "dim"))
    83→        print(colorize("  Consider splitting into area-focused clusters (same files in same PR).", "dim"))
    84→
    85→    high_ratio = _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(plan)
    86→    if high_ratio:
    87→        print(colorize(f"\n  Warning: {len(high_ratio)} cluster(s) have step count ≥ issue count:", "yellow"))
    88→        for name, steps, issues, ratio in high_ratio:
    89→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {steps} steps for {issues} issues ({ratio:.1f}x)", "yellow"))
    90→        print(colorize("  Steps should consolidate changes to the same file. 1:1 means each issue is its own step.", "dim"))
    91→
    92→    _print_cluster_overlap_notes(plan, _cluster_file_overlaps(plan))
    93→
    94→
    95→def _print_cluster_overlap_notes(plan: dict, overlaps: list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]]) -> None:
    96→    if not overlaps:
    97→        return
    98→    clusters_dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    99→    print(colorize(f"\n  Note: {len(overlaps)} cluster pair(s) reference the same files:", "yellow"))
   100→    for left, right, files in overlaps[:5]:
   101→        print(colorize(f"    {left} ↔ {right}: {len(files)} shared file(s)", "yellow"))
   102→    needs_dep = []
   103→    for left, right, files in overlaps:
   104→        left_deps = set(clusters_dict.get(left, {}).get("depends_on_clusters", []))
   105→        right_deps = set(clusters_dict.get(right, {}).get("depends_on_clusters", []))
   106→        if right not in left_deps and left not in right_deps:
   107→            needs_dep.append((left, right, files))
   108→    if not needs_dep:
   109→        return
   110→    print(colorize("  These pairs have no dependency relationship — add one to prevent merge conflicts:", "dim"))
   111→    for left, right, _files in needs_dep[:5]:
   112→        print(colorize(f"    desloppify plan cluster update {right} --depends-on {left}", "dim"))
   113→        print(colorize(f"    # or: desloppify plan cluster update {left} --depends-on {right}", "dim"))
   114→
   115→
   116→def _print_orphaned_cluster_notes(all_clusters: dict) -> None:
   117→    for name, cluster in all_clusters.items():
   118→        deps = cluster.get("depends_on_clusters", [])
   119→        if name in deps:
   120→            print(colorize(f"  Warning: {name} depends on itself.", "yellow"))
   121→
   122→    orphaned = [
   123→        (name, len(cluster.get("action_steps", [])))
   124→        for name, cluster in all_clusters.items()
   125→        if not cluster.get("auto") and not cluster_issue_ids(cluster) and cluster.get("action_steps")
   126→    ]
   127→    if not orphaned:
   128→        return
   129→    print(colorize(f"\n  Note: {len(orphaned)} cluster(s) have steps but no issues:", "yellow"))
   130→    for name, step_count in orphaned:
   131→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: {step_count} steps, 0 issues", "yellow"))
   132→    print(colorize("  These may need issues added, or may be leftover from resolved work.", "dim"))
   133→
   134→
   135→def confirm_organize(
   136→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   137→    plan: dict,
   138→    stages: dict,
   139→    attestation: str | None,
   140→    *,
   141→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   142→) -> None:
   143→    """Show full plan summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
   144→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   145→    if not ensure_stage_is_confirmable(stages, stage="organize"):
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   149→    state = runtime.state
   150→
   151→    print(colorize("  Stage: ORGANIZE — Defer contradictions, cluster, & prioritize", "bold"))
   152→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 63, "dim"))
   153→
   154→    _print_reflect_activity_summary(plan, stages)
   155→
   156→    print(colorize("\n  Plan:", "bold"))
   157→    show_plan_summary(plan, state)
   158→
   159→    organize_clusters = [
   160→        name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")
   161→    ]
   162→    if not _require_enriched_clusters(plan):
   163→        return
   164→    if not _require_clustered_review_issues(plan, state):
   165→        return
   166→
   167→    _print_cluster_shape_warnings(plan)
   168→    all_clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   169→    _print_orphaned_cluster_notes(all_clusters)
   170→
   171→    organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids_from_state(state))
   172→    if not finalize_stage_confirmation(
   173→        plan=plan,
   174→        stages=stages,
   175→        request=StageConfirmationRequest(
   176→            stage="organize",
   177→            attestation=attestation,
   178→            min_attestation_len=MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN,
   179→            command_hint='desloppify plan triage --confirm organize --attestation "This plan is correct..."',
   180→            validation_stage="organize",
   181→            validate_attestation_fn=validate_attestation,
   182→            validation_kwargs={"cluster_names": organize_clusters},
   183→            log_action="triage_confirm_organize",
   184→            log_detail={"coverage": f"{organized}/{total}"},
   185→            not_satisfied_hint="If not, adjust clusters, priorities, or queue order before completing.",
   186→        ),
   187→        services=resolved_services,
   188→    ):
   189→        return
   190→    print_user_message(
   191→        "Hey — organize is confirmed. Next: enrich your steps"
   192→        " with detail and issue_refs so they're executor-ready."
   193→        " Run `desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report \"...\"`."
   194→        " You can still reorganize (add/remove clusters, reorder)"
   195→        " during the enrich stage."
   196→    )
   197→
   198→
   199→__all__ = ["confirm_organize"]
   200→
```

1→"""Per-stage instruction text blocks for triage prompts."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from .stage_prompts_instruction_shared import (
     6→    PromptMode,
     7→    observe_example_report_quality,
     8→    observe_false_positive_guidance,
     9→    observe_structured_template,
    10→)
    11→
    12→
    13→def _observe_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
    14→    tail = """\
    15→When done, run:
    16→```
    17→desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "<your analysis with structured assessments>"
    18→```
    19→"""
    20→    if mode == "output_only":
    21→        tail = """\
    22→When done, write a plain-text observe report with structured assessments per issue.
    23→The orchestrator records and confirms the stage.
    24→"""
    25→    return f"""\
    26→## OBSERVE Stage Instructions
    27→
    28→Your task: verify every open review issue against the actual source code.
    29→
    30→{observe_false_positive_guidance()}
    31→
    32→Do NOT analyze themes, strategy, or relationships between issues. That's the next stage (reflect).
    33→Just verify: is each issue real?
    34→
    35→**CRITICAL: You must cite specific issue IDs (hash prefixes like [abcd1234]) in your report.**
    36→The confirmation gate requires citing at least 10% of issues (or 5, whichever is smaller).
    37→
    38→**USE SUBAGENTS to parallelize this work.** Launch parallel subagents — one per dimension
    39→group — to investigate concurrently. Each subagent MUST:
    40→- Open and read the actual source file for EVERY assigned issue
    41→- Verify specific claims: count the actual casts, props, returns, line count
    42→- Check if the suggested fix already exists (common false positive)
    43→- Report a clear verdict per issue: genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering / not-worth-it
    44→
    45→Example subagent split for 90 issues across 17 dimensions:
    46→- Subagent 1: architecture + organization (cross_module_architecture, package_organization, high_level_elegance)
    47→- Subagent 2: abstraction + design (abstraction_fitness, design_coherence, mid_level_elegance)
    48→- Subagent 3: duplicates + contracts (contract_coherence, api_surface_coherence, low_level_elegance)
    49→- Subagent 4: migrations + debt + conventions (incomplete_migration, ai_generated_debt, convention_outlier, naming_quality)
    50→- Subagent 5: type safety + errors + tests (type_safety, error_consistency, test_strategy, initialization_coupling, dependency_health)
    51→
    52→### Structured Assessment Template
    53→
    54→**Your report MUST include a structured assessment for EVERY issue.** Copy and fill out this
    55→template for each issue:
    56→
    57→{observe_structured_template()}
    58→
    59→**Example:**
    60→```
    61→- hash: 34580232
    62→  verdict: false-positive
    63→  verdict_reasoning: Uses branded string union KnownTaskType with ~25 literals in src/types/database.ts line 50. The issue describes code that doesn't exist.
    64→  files_read: [src/types/database.ts]
    65→  recommendation: No action needed — issue is inaccurate
    66→
    67→- hash: b634fc71
    68→  verdict: genuine
    69→  verdict_reasoning: Confirmed 65 properties at lines 217-282. Mixes pane lifecycle, filters, gallery data, interaction, and navigation.
    70→  files_read: [src/shared/components/GenerationsPane/hooks/useGenerationsPaneController.ts]
    71→  recommendation: Decompose into focused sub-hooks
    72→```
    73→
    74→{observe_example_report_quality()}
    75→
    76→**Validation checks (all blocking):**
    77→- Every entry must have a recognized `verdict` keyword
    78→- Every entry must have non-empty `verdict_reasoning`
    79→- Every entry must have non-empty `files_read` list
    80→- Every entry must have non-empty `recommendation`
    81→
    82→- Template fields left empty or with placeholder text
    83→
    84→{tail}
    85→"""
    86→
    87→
    88→def _reflect_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
    89→    tail = """\
    90→When done, run:
    91→```
    92→desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "<your strategy with cluster blueprint>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning dimensions or recurring patterns>"
    93→```
    94→"""
    95→    if mode == "output_only":
    96→        tail = """\
    97→When done, write a plain-text reflect report with a concrete cluster blueprint.
    98→The orchestrator records and confirms the stage.
    99→"""
   100→    return f"""\
   101→## REFLECT Stage Instructions
   102→
   103→Your task: using the verdicts from observe, design the cluster structure.
   104→
   105→**A strategy is NOT a restatement of observe.** Observe says "here's what I found." Reflect
   106→says "here's what we should DO about it, and here's what we should NOT do, and here's WHY."
   107→
   108→**The Structured Observe Assessments table (provided below) is your primary input.** It contains
   109→a per-issue verdict (genuine/false-positive/exaggerated/over-engineering/not-worth-it) with reasoning. Use
   110→these verdicts as authoritative — do not second-guess observe unless you have specific evidence.
   111→Issues with verdict `false-positive`, `over-engineering`, or `not-worth-it` should go into skip lines, not clusters.
   112→
   113→### What you must do:
   114→
   115→1. **Filter:** which issues are genuine (from the observe assessments table)?
   116→2. **Map:** for each genuine issue, what file/directory does it touch?
   117→3. **Group:** which issues share files or directories? These become clusters.
   118→4. **Skip:** which issues should be skipped? Apply YAGNI: if the fix is more complex than the
   119→   problem, skip it. Valid skip reasons:
   120→   - "the fix would add a 50-line abstraction to save 3 lines of duplication"
   121→   - "the current code is clear and simple despite being theoretically suboptimal"
   122→   - "observe verdict: not-worth-it — the improvement is marginal"
   123→   - "fixing this requires touching 8 files for a naming consistency issue nobody notices"
   124→   Invalid: "low priority" (everything is low priority compared to something)
   125→5. **Order:** which clusters depend on others? What's the execution sequence?
   126→6. **Check recurring patterns** — compare current issues against resolved history. If the same
   127→   dimension keeps producing issues, that's a root cause that needs addressing, not just
   128→   another round of fixes.
   129→7. **Account for every issue exactly once** — every open issue hash must appear in exactly one
   130→   cluster line or one skip line. Do not drop hashes, and do not repeat a hash in multiple
   131→   clusters or in both a cluster and a skip.
   132→
   133→### Your report MUST include both a coverage ledger and a concrete cluster blueprint
   134→
   135→This blueprint is what the organize stage will execute. Be specific:
   136→```
   137→## Coverage Ledger
   138→- a5996373 -> cluster "travel-structure-contract-unification"
   139→- fb113678 -> skip "false-positive-current-code"
   140→
   141→## Cluster Blueprint
   142→Cluster "media-lightbox-hooks" (all in src/domains/media-lightbox/)
   143→Cluster "task-typing" (both touch src/types/database.ts)
   144→
   145→## Skip Decisions
   146→Skip "false-positive-current-code" (false positive per observe)
   147→```
   148→
   149→### Hard accounting rule
   150→
   151→- Start your report with a `## Coverage Ledger` section.
   152→- In that section, mention each issue hash **once and only once** on its own ledger line.
   153→- Do **not** mention issue hashes again in cluster rationale paragraphs, recurring-pattern notes,
   154→  or ordering explanations. After the ledger, refer to clusters by name.
   155→- Before finishing, do a self-check: the ledger must cover all open issue hashes exactly once.
   156→
   157→### What a LAZY reflect looks like (will be rejected):
   158→- Restating observe findings in slightly different words
   159→- "We should prioritize high-impact items and defer low-priority ones"
   160→- A bulleted list of dimensions without any strategic thinking
   161→- Ignoring recurring patterns
   162→- No `## Coverage Ledger`
   163→- No cluster blueprint (just vague grouping ideas)
   164→- Missing or duplicated issue hashes
   165→
   166→### What a GOOD reflect looks like:
   167→- "50% false positive rate. Of 34 issues, 17 are genuine. 10 of those are batch-scriptable
   168→  convention fixes (zero risk, 30 min) — cluster 'convention-batch'. The remaining 7 split into
   169→  3 clusters by file proximity: 'media-lightbox-hooks' (issues X,Y,Z — all in src/domains/media-lightbox/),
   170→  'timeline-cleanup' (issues A,B,C — touching Timeline components), 'task-typing' (issues D,E).
   171→  Skip: issue W (false positive), issue V (over-engineering), issue U (not-worth-it:
   172→  adds ErrorBoundary wrapper around 3 components that already handle errors inline —
   173→  technically cleaner but doubles the JSX nesting depth for no behavioral change).
   174→  design_coherence recurs (2 resolved, 5 open) but only 1 of the 5 actually warrants work."
   175→
   176→{tail}
   177→"""
   178→
   179→
   180→def _organize_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
   181→    intro = (
   182→        "The reflect report contains a specific plan: which clusters to create, which issues go\n"
   183→        "where, what to skip. The reflect report included above is authoritative. Do not go\n"
   184→        "search old triage runs for a different blueprint unless you find a concrete mismatch.\n"
   185→        "Build it using the CLI. If something doesn't work as planned\n"
   186→        "(issue hash doesn't match, file proximity doesn't hold), adjust and document why."
   187→    )
   188→    process_block = """\
   189→2. **Skip issues that observe flagged as false-positive, over-engineering, or not-worth-it.** This is mandatory,
   190→   not optional. Check the **Structured Observe Assessments** table (provided below) — every
   191→   issue with verdict `false-positive`, `over-engineering`, or `not-worth-it` MUST be skipped. Use the observe
   192→   `verdict_reasoning` as the basis for your skip note:
   193→   ```
   194→   desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "<reason from observe verdict>" --attest "I have reviewed this triage skip against the code and I am not gaming the score by suppressing a real defect."
   195→   ```
   196→   Do NOT cluster an issue that observe determined is not a real defect.
   197→3. Create clusters as specified in the blueprint:
   198→   `desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."`
   199→4. Add issues: `desloppify plan cluster add <name> <patterns...>`
   200→5. Add steps that consolidate: one step per file or logical change, NOT one step per issue
   201→6. Set `--effort` on each step individually (trivial/small/medium/large)
   202→7. Set `--depends-on` when clusters touch overlapping files
   203→"""
   204→    tail = """\
   205→When done, run:
   206→```
   207→desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "<summary of priorities and organization>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning cluster names>"
   208→```
   209→"""
   210→    if mode == "output_only":
   211→        intro = (
   212→            "The reflect report contains a specific plan: which clusters to create, which issues go\n"
   213→            "where, what to skip. The reflect report included above is authoritative. Do not go\n"
   214→            "search old triage runs for a different blueprint unless you find a concrete mismatch.\n"
   215→            "Translate that plan into a precise organize report. If something\n"
   216→            "doesn't work as planned (issue hash doesn't match, file proximity doesn't hold), adjust\n"
   217→            "and document why."
   218→        )
   219→        process_block = """\
   220→2. **Skip issues that observe flagged as false-positive, over-engineering, or not-worth-it.** This is mandatory.
   221→   Check the **Structured Observe Assessments** table (provided below) — every issue with
   222→   verdict `false-positive`, `over-engineering`, or `not-worth-it` MUST be skipped. Use the observe
   223→   `verdict_reasoning` as the basis for your skip justification.
   224→   Do NOT cluster an issue that observe determined is not a real defect.
   225→3. Define the clusters exactly as they should be created.
   226→4. Assign every kept issue to a cluster.
   227→5. Consolidate steps: one step per file or logical change, NOT one step per issue.
   228→6. Assign an effort level to each planned step (trivial/small/medium/large).
   229→7. Call out cross-cluster dependencies when clusters touch overlapping files.
   230→"""
   231→        tail = """\
   232→When done, write a plain-text organize report that names the clusters, their issue membership,
   233→their consolidated steps, and any skip/dependency decisions. The orchestrator records the stage.
   234→"""
   235→    return f"""\
   236→## ORGANIZE Stage Instructions
   237→
   238→Your task: execute the cluster blueprint from the reflect stage.
   239→

## Comprehensive Analysis of Reflect's Disposition Ledger and Organize's Consumption

Based on my thorough code exploration, here's the exact technical documentation:

### 1. **ReflectDisposition Parsing: Structure and Valid Decision Values**

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/reflect_accounting.py` (lines 16-40)

The `ReflectDisposition` is a frozen dataclass:
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReflectDisposition:
    """One issue's intended disposition as declared by the reflect stage."""
    issue_id: str
    decision: DecisionKind
    target: str
```

**Valid decision values** (`DecisionKind` type literal, line 13):
- `"cluster"` — issue belongs in a named cluster
- `"permanent_skip"` — issue is skipped (not worked on)

**How parsing happens** (reflect.py line 92):
```python
disposition_ledger = parse_reflect_dispositions(report, valid_ids)
```

**The parsing function** (`parse_reflect_dispositions()`, lines 288-293) extracts dispositions from a `## Coverage Ledger` section in the reflect report. It supports multiple formats:

- **Canonical**: `- a5996373 -> cluster "cluster-name"` or `- a5996373 -> skip "reason"`
- **Compat**: `- a5996373: cluster "name"` or `- a5996373, cluster, name`
- **Token-only**: `- a5996373 ->`

**Normalization** (`_normalize_decision()`, lines 166-172):
- Raw input `"skip"`, `"dismiss"`, `"defer"`, `"drop"`, `"remove"` → normalized to `"permanent_skip"`
- Raw input `"cluster"` → stays `"cluster"`
- **Important**: Raw input values like `"false-positive"` or `"over-engineering"` from the observe verdicts are **NOT** valid decision types. They cannot be used directly in the ledger.

**Persistence** (reflect.py lines 133-134):
```python
if disposition_ledger:
    reflect_stage["disposition_ledger"] = [d.to_dict() for d in disposition_ledger]
```
Each disposition serializes as: `{"issue_id": "...", "decision": "cluster"|"permanent_skip", "target": "..."}`

---

### 2. **How Organize Consumes the Disposition Ledger**

**Direct ledger reading** (organize.py, line 207-208):
Organize does NOT read the disposition_ledger directly. Instead, it calls:
```python
if not _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(plan=plan, stages=stages):
    return None
```

**Validation function** (`organize_policy.py` lines 189-226, `validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger()` lines 168-186):

1. **Extracts the ledger** from plan.json:
   ```python
   raw_ledger = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("disposition_ledger")
   if not raw_ledger:
       return []  # No ledger = validation passes
   ledger = [ReflectDisposition.from_dict(d) for d in raw_ledger]
   ```

2. **Builds an "actual disposition" index** from plan state (`_build_actual_disposition_index()`, lines 142-156):
   ```python
   index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="clustered", cluster_name=cluster_name)
   # OR
   index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="skipped")
   ```
   - Scans `plan["clusters"]` for manual (non-auto) clusters
   - Scans `plan["skipped"]` for permanently skipped issues
   - Dismissal is tracked separately in `meta["dismissed_ids"]` (but **not** validated against the disposition ledger)

3. **Reconciles intended vs. actual** (`_disposition_matches()`, lines 159-165):
   ```python
   def _disposition_matches(intended: ReflectDisposition, actual: ActualDisposition) -> bool:
       if intended.decision == "permanent_skip":
           return actual.kind == "skipped"
       if intended.decision == "cluster":
           return (actual.kind == "clustered" 
                   and actual.cluster_name == intended.target)
       return False
   ```

4. **Reports mismatches** if organize's plan state diverges from reflect's ledger:
   ```python
   mismatches = [
       LedgerMismatch(intended=d, actual=actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
       for d in ledger
       if not _disposition_matches(d, actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
   ]
   ```

**Error message example** (organize_policy.py lines 210-216):
```
Cannot organize: 5 issue(s) diverge from the reflect plan.
  a1b2c3d4: reflect said cluster "media-lightbox", but clustered in "other-cluster"
  e5f6g7h8: reflect said permanent_skip, but not skipped, not clustered
```

**Key finding**: There is **NO Python validation** connecting observe verdicts (`false-positive`, `over-engineering`) to organize skip decisions. The ledger only enforces that the plan state matches what reflect declared — it doesn't validate that reflect's decisions were correct.

### 5. **Review Coverage Tracking: What Counts as "Dispositioned"**

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/review_coverage.py` (lines 100-119)

**Dispositioned definition** (`undispositioned_triage_issue_ids()` lines 100-119):
```python
def undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel | None = None,
) -> list[str]:
    """Return frozen triage issues still lacking cluster/skip/dismiss coverage."""
    target_ids = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    if not target_ids:
        return []
    covered_ids: set[str] = set()
    
    # Add manually clustered issues
    for cluster in ensure_cluster_map(plan).values():
        if cluster.get("auto"):
            continue
        covered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    
    # Add skipped issues
    covered_ids.update(
        issue_id for issue_id in ensure_skipped_map(plan) 
        if isinstance(issue_id, str)
    )
    
    # Add dismissed issues
    covered_ids.update(
        normalized_issue_id_list(ensure_triage_meta(plan).get("dismissed_ids"))
    )
    
    return sorted(
        issue_id for issue_id in target_ids 
        if issue_id not in covered_ids
    )
```

**What counts as "covered/dispositioned"**:
1. **In a manual (non-auto) cluster** — `cluster.get("auto")` must be False
2. **Skipped** — entry in `plan["skipped"]` dict
3. **Dismissed** — in `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["dismissed_ids"]` list

**What does NOT count**:
- Being in an auto-cluster
- Being referenced in the reflect disposition_ledger
- Being linked from anything

3. **What gets validated** (`_require_clustered_review_issues()` lines 40-53):

The instructions tell the LLM "must skip false-positive issues" but there's no automated enforcement.
