## Summary of Flow

```
Observe Report
  ↓ (parse evidence)
Assessments list (hash, verdict, files_read, recommendation)
  ↓ (stored in plan.json)
stages["observe"]["assessments"]
  ↓ (included in LLM prompt)
build_stage_prompt() formats table + evidence
  ↓
Reflect LLM receives assessments + makes decisions
  ↓ (parse ledger)
Disposition ledger (issue_id, decision: cluster|permanent_skip, target)
  ↓ (stored in plan.json)
stages["reflect"]["disposition_ledger"]
  ↓ (validate against)
organize validates that plan state matches ledger
  ↓
If mismatch: error
If match: proceed to enrichment
```

**The critical missing piece**: There's no validation that issues with observe verdicts of `false-positive`, `over-engineering`, or `not-worth-it` actually ended up skipped. Reflect creates a ledger, organize validates the ledger is satisfied, but the decision quality itself is never checked.

> DEVELOPER

I need to design a refactor of the triage state architecture for a codebase health tool called desloppify. Here's the context:

## Current Architecture

The triage workflow has 6 stages: observe → reflect → organize → enrich → sense-check → commit. Each stage is run by an LLM agent that reads code and produces structured output.

**The problem**: These stages operate on different, loosely-coupled state representations:

1. **Observe** writes `assessments` list to `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"]["observe"]["assessments"]` — a list of `{hash, verdict, verdict_reasoning, files_read, recommendation}` dicts. Verdicts are: genuine, false-positive, exaggerated, over-engineering, not-worth-it.

2. **Reflect** writes `disposition_ledger` to `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["triage_stages"]["reflect"]["disposition_ledger"]` — a list of `{issue_id, decision: "cluster"|"permanent_skip", target}` objects. It also writes `cluster_blueprint`.

3. **Organize** creates actual clusters in `plan["clusters"]` and skip entries in `plan["skipped"]`. This is the first stage that mutates real plan state.

4. **Enrich** updates cluster action_steps with detail/effort/issue-refs.

5. **Sense-check** verifies step details against code.

**Key gaps**:
- Observe verdicts (false-positive, not-worth-it) don't change state. They're just prompt text that tells the next LLM "you should skip these." No Python enforcement.
- Reflect's disposition_ledger is a separate structure from observe's assessments. Organize validates against the ledger but not against observe verdicts.
- New review issues can appear between stages, visible in counts but not triaged.
- Coverage tracking (`undispositioned_triage_issue_ids`) looks at clusters/skips/dismissed but not at observe/reflect decisions.

## Desired Architecture

**One shared per-issue disposition map** that every stage writes to, so each stage builds on concrete state rather than interpreting prose. All stages work on the same structure.

```python
# In epic_triage_meta:
"issue_dispositions": {
    "<issue_id>": {
        # Observe writes:
        "verdict": "genuine",
        "verdict_reasoning": "...",
        "files_read": [...],
        "recommendation": "...",
        
        # Reflect writes (for genuine issues):
        "decision": "cluster",  # or "skip" 
        "target": "cluster-name",  # or skip reason
        
        # Organize confirms:
        "placed": True,  # cluster assigned or skip created
        
        # Enrich confirms:
        "enriched": True,  # steps detailed
        
        # Sense-check confirms:
        "verified": True,  # verified against code
    }
}
```

**Key behaviors**:
1. When observe records a non-genuine verdict (false-positive, over-engineering, not-worth-it), the system auto-sets `decision: "skip"` and creates the skip entry in `plan["skipped"]` immediately. These issues are dispositioned in one step — reflect/organize never see them.
2. Reflect only needs to decide about genuine issues (cluster assignment). It writes `decision` and `target` to the map.
3. Organize validates that the map's decisions are realized in plan state (clusters exist, issues assigned). Marks `placed: True`.
4. Coverage tracking becomes: count issues without `placed: True`.
5. Validation enforces consistency: non-genuine verdict → must be skip. Genuine + decision=cluster → must be in the target cluster.

## Files I know about

Schema:
- `desloppify/engine/_plan/schema/__init__.py` — PlanModel, EpicTriageMeta, TriageStagePayload, Cluster TypedDicts. PLAN_VERSION = 8.

Stage implementations:
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/observe.py` — cmd_stage_observe(), parses evidence, records assessments
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/reflect.py` — parses disposition_ledger from report
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/organize.py` — creates clusters/skips via CLI
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/records.py` — record_observe_stage(), record_reflect_stage(), etc.

Validation:
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/evidence_parsing.py` — parse_observe_evidence(), VERDICT_KEYWORDS
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/reflect_accounting.py` — ReflectDisposition, parse_reflect_dispositions()
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/organize_policy.py` — validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger()

Coverage:
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/review_coverage.py` — undispositioned_triage_issue_ids()

Prompts:
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_prompts.py` — build_stage_prompt(), _format_assessments_table()
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/runner/stage_prompts_instruction_blocks.py` — per-stage instruction text

Skip/dismiss:
- `desloppify/engine/_plan/operations/skip.py` — skip_items(), SkipEntry
- `desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/dismiss.py` — dismiss_triage_issues()

Lifecycle:
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/lifecycle.py` — ensure_triage_started()
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stage_queue.py` — cascade_clear_later_confirmations()
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/completion_flow.py` — apply_completion()

## Design Questions

1. Should this be phased (disposition map first, auto-skip second, legacy removal third)? Or is it clean enough to do in one pass?
2. How should existing plan.json files migrate? There's an existing data migration policy: keep migration code for old formats.
3. Should the stage reports (narrative text) still be kept? They're useful for human review. The dispositions map would be the authoritative structured data; reports remain as human-readable narrative.
4. What about the cascade_clear behavior? Currently re-running observe clears all downstream confirmations. With the disposition map, re-running observe should also clear the disposition fields written by later stages.
5. How should the prompt builders change? Currently they format assessments as a table. They should format the disposition map instead, showing accumulated state per issue.

Design a clean, phased implementation plan. Focus on: what data structures change, what Python logic changes, what can be removed, and what validation tightens. Keep it practical — this is a real codebase with ~5200 tests.

> AGENT

I'll start by thoroughly exploring the current codebase to understand the existing state structures, stage implementations, validation logic, and data flow before designing the plan.

```
   214→    return {

   216→        "created": now,
   217→        "updated": now,

   235→
   236→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   237→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   238→
   239→    Runtime contract is v8-only. Legacy payloads are upgraded in-place once.
   240→    """
   241→    defaults = empty_plan()
   242→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   243→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   244→    _upgrade_plan_to_v8(plan)
   245→    subjective_defer_meta = plan.get("subjective_defer_meta")
   246→    if isinstance(subjective_defer_meta, dict):
   247→        subjective_defer_meta.pop("force_visible_ids", None)
   248→    epic_triage_meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta")
   249→    if isinstance(epic_triage_meta, dict):
   250→        epic_triage_meta.pop("triage_force_visible", None)
   251→
   252→
   253→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   254→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   255→    return {
   256→        name: cluster
   257→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   258→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   259→    }
   260→
   261→
   262→def tracked_plan_ids(plan: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
   263→    """Collect all issue IDs the plan is actively tracking.
   264→
   265→    Includes queue_order, skipped, overrides, and cluster members/step refs.
   266→    Returns an empty set for None or non-dict plans.
   267→    """
   268→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   269→        return set()
   270→    tracked: set[str] = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   271→    tracked.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   272→    tracked.update(plan.get("overrides", {}).keys())
   273→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   274→        tracked.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   275→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", []):
   276→            if isinstance(step, dict):
   277→                tracked.update(step.get("issue_refs", []))
   278→    return tracked
   279→
   280→
   281→def planned_objective_ids(
   282→    all_objective_ids: set[str],
   283→    plan: dict[str, Any] | None,
   284→) -> set[str]:
   285→    """Return the subset of objective IDs the plan considers active work.
   286→
   287→    Pre-triage (plan tracks nothing): all objective IDs are treated as
   288→    planned work.
   289→
   290→    Once the plan tracks anything, only the intersection with live
   291→    objective IDs counts as planned. This lets stale queue_order entries
   292→    fail closed into postflight/backlog instead of broadening execution
   293→    back out to the entire objective backlog.
   294→    """
   295→    tracked = tracked_plan_ids(plan)
   296→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys()) if isinstance(plan, dict) else set()
   297→    active_tracked = {
   298→        issue_id
   299→        for issue_id in tracked - skipped_ids
   300→        if not any(issue_id.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   301→    }
   302→    if not active_tracked:
   303→        return set(all_objective_ids)
   304→    return all_objective_ids & active_tracked
   305→
   306→
   307→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   308→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   309→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   310→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   311→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   312→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   313→
   314→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   315→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   316→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   317→    if overlap:
   318→        raise ValueError(
   319→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   320→        )
   321→
   322→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   323→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   324→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   325→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] must be an object")
   326→        if "kind" not in entry:
   327→            raise ValueError(f"plan.skipped[{fid!r}] missing required key 'kind'")
   328→        kind = entry["kind"]
   329→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   330→            raise ValueError(
   331→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   332→            )
   333→
   334→
   335→__all__ = [
   336→    "ActionStep",
   337→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   338→    "EpicTriageMeta",
   339→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   340→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   341→    "Cluster",
   342→    "CommitRecord",
   343→    "ItemOverride",
   344→    "LastTriageSnapshot",
   345→    "PlanModel",
   346→    "PlanStartScores",
   347→    "RefreshState",
   348→    "SkipEntry",
   349→    "SupersededEntry",
   350→    "TriageStagePayload",
   351→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   352→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   353→    "empty_plan",
   354→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   355→    "planned_objective_ids",
   356→    "tracked_plan_ids",
   357→    "triage_clusters",
   358→    "validate_plan",
   359→]
```

```
    10→def resolve_reusable_report(
    11→    report: str | None,
    12→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    13→) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    14→    if report:
    15→        return report, False
    16→    if existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
    17→        return existing_stage["report"], True
    18→    return None, False
    19→
    20→
    21→def record_observe_stage(
    22→    stages: dict,
    23→    *,
    24→    report: str,
    25→    issue_count: int,
    26→    cited_ids: list[str],
    27→    existing_stage: dict | None,
    28→    is_reuse: bool,
    29→    assessments: list[dict] | None = None,
    30→) -> list[str]:
    31→    stages["observe"] = {
    32→        "stage": "observe",
    33→        "report": report,
    34→        "cited_ids": cited_ids,
    35→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    36→        "issue_count": issue_count,
    37→    }
    38→    if assessments is not None:
    39→        stages["observe"]["assessments"] = assessments
    40→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
    41→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    42→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
    43→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
    44→    if not is_reuse:
    45→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    46→        stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    47→    return cleared
    48→
    49→

    61→        "cited_ids": [],
    62→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    63→        "issue_count": issue_count,

    66→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
    67→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
```

```
1→"""Observe stage command flow."""

     6→

     8→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message

    10→from ..stage_queue import (
    11→    has_triage_in_queue,
    12→    inject_triage_stages,
    13→    print_cascade_clear_feedback,
    14→)
    15→from ..lifecycle import TriageLifecycleDeps, ensure_triage_started
    16→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    17→from .flow_helpers import validate_stage_report_length
    18→from .records import record_observe_stage, resolve_reusable_report
    19→from .rendering import _print_observe_report_requirement
    20→
    21→
    22→def cmd_stage_observe(
    23→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    24→    *,
    25→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    26→    has_triage_in_queue_fn=has_triage_in_queue,
    27→    inject_triage_stages_fn=inject_triage_stages,
    28→) -> None:
    29→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes."""
    30→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    31→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    32→
    33→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    34→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    37→
    38→    if not has_triage_in_queue_fn(plan):
    39→        start_outcome = ensure_triage_started(
    40→            plan,
    41→            services=resolved_services,
    42→            state=state,
    43→            attestation=attestation,
    44→            start_message="  Planning mode auto-started (6 stages queued).",
    45→            deps=TriageLifecycleDeps(
    46→                has_triage_in_queue=has_triage_in_queue_fn,
    47→                inject_triage_stages=inject_triage_stages_fn,
    48→            ),
    49→        )
    50→        if start_outcome.status == "blocked":
    51→            return
    52→
    53→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    54→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    55→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
    56→
    57→    report, is_reuse = resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    58→    if not report:
    59→        _print_observe_report_requirement()
    60→        return
    61→
    62→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    63→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
    64→    if issue_count == 0:
    65→        cleared = record_observe_stage(
    66→            stages,
    67→            report=report,
    68→            issue_count=0,
    69→            cited_ids=[],
    70→            existing_stage=existing_stage,
    71→            is_reuse=is_reuse,
    72→        )
    73→        resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    74→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no issues to analyse).", "green"))
    75→        if is_reuse:
    76→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
    77→            if cleared:
    78→                print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
    79→        return
    80→
    81→    if not validate_stage_report_length(
    82→        report=report,
    83→        issue_count=issue_count,
    84→        guidance="  Describe themes, root causes, contradictions, and how issues relate.",
    85→    ):
    86→        return
    87→
    88→    from .evidence_parsing import (
    89→        format_evidence_failures,
    90→        parse_observe_evidence,
    91→        validate_observe_evidence,
    92→    )
    93→
    94→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
    95→    cited = resolved_services.extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
    96→    evidence = parse_observe_evidence(report, valid_ids)
    97→    evidence_failures = validate_observe_evidence(evidence, issue_count)
    98→    blocking = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if failure.blocking]
    99→    advisory = [failure for failure in evidence_failures if not failure.blocking]
   100→    if blocking:
   101→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(blocking, stage_label="observe"), "red"))
   102→        return
   103→    if advisory:
   104→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(advisory, stage_label="observe"), "yellow"))
   105→
   106→    assessments = [
   107→        {
   108→            "hash": entry.issue_hash,
   109→            "verdict": entry.verdict,
   110→            "verdict_reasoning": entry.verdict_reasoning,
   111→            "files_read": entry.files_read,
   112→            "recommendation": entry.recommendation,
   113→        }
   114→        for entry in evidence.entries
   115→    ]
   116→    cleared = record_observe_stage(
   117→        stages,
   118→        report=report,
   119→        issue_count=issue_count,
   120→        cited_ids=sorted(cited),
   121→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   122→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   123→        assessments=assessments,
   124→    )
   125→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   126→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   127→        plan,
   128→        "triage_observe",
   129→        actor="user",
   130→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "cited_ids": sorted(cited), "reuse": is_reuse},
   131→    )
   132→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   133→
   134→    print(colorize(f"  Observe stage recorded: {issue_count} issues analysed.", "green"))
   135→    if is_reuse:
   136→        print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   137→        if cleared:
   138→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    print(colorize("  Now confirm your analysis.", "yellow"))
   142→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe", "dim"))
   143→    print_user_message(
   144→        "Observe recorded. Before confirming — did the subagent"
   145→        " verify every issue with code reads? Check: are there"
   146→        " specific file/line citations in the report, or just"
   147→        " restated issue titles? Each issue needs a verdict:"
   148→        " genuine / false positive / exaggerated / not-worth-it. Don't confirm"
   149→        " until the analysis is backed by actual code evidence."
   150→    )
   151→
   152→_cmd_stage_observe = cmd_stage_observe
   153→
   154→
   155→__all__ = ["_cmd_stage_observe", "cmd_stage_observe"]
   156→
```

```
1→"""Reflect stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.state_io import utc_now
     9→
    10→from ..display.dashboard import print_reflect_result
    11→from ..stage_queue import cascade_clear_later_confirmations, has_triage_in_queue
    12→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    13→from ..validation.core import (
    14→    ReflectDisposition,
    15→    _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions,
    16→    parse_reflect_dispositions,
    17→)
    18→from ..validation.reflect_accounting import validate_reflect_accounting
    19→from ..validation.stage_policy import auto_confirm_observe_if_attested
    20→from .flow_helpers import validate_stage_report_length
    21→from .records import resolve_reusable_report
    22→from .rendering import _print_reflect_report_requirement
    23→
    24→
    25→def _validate_reflect_submission(
    26→    *,
    27→    report: str,
    28→    plan: dict,
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    stages: dict,
    31→    attestation: str | None,
    32→    services: TriageServices,
    33→) -> tuple[object, int, dict, list[str], set[str], list[str], list[str], list[ReflectDisposition]] | None:
    34→    if "observe" not in stages:
    35→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
    36→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    37→        return None
    38→
    39→    triage_input = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    40→    if not auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
    41→        plan=plan,
    42→        stages=stages,
    43→        attestation=attestation,
    44→        triage_input=triage_input,
    45→        save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
    46→    ):
    47→        return None
    48→
    49→    issue_count = len(triage_input.open_issues)
    50→    if not validate_stage_report_length(
    51→        report=report,
    52→        issue_count=issue_count,
    53→        guidance="  Describe how current issues relate to previously completed work.",
    54→    ):
    55→        return None
    56→
    57→    recurring = services.detect_recurring_patterns(
    58→        triage_input.open_issues,
    59→        triage_input.resolved_issues,
    60→    )
    61→    recurring_dims = sorted(recurring.keys())
    62→    if not _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
    63→        report=report,
    64→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
    65→        recurring=recurring,
    66→    ):
    67→        return None
    68→
    69→    valid_ids = set(triage_input.open_issues.keys())
    70→    accounting_ok, cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = validate_reflect_accounting(
    71→        report=report,
    72→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
    73→    )
    74→    if not accounting_ok:
    75→        return None
    76→
    77→    from .evidence_parsing import (
    78→        format_evidence_failures,
    79→        validate_reflect_skip_evidence,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→    blocking_skips = [
    83→        failure
    84→        for failure in validate_reflect_skip_evidence(report)
    85→        if failure.blocking
    86→    ]
    87→    if blocking_skips:
    88→        print(colorize(format_evidence_failures(blocking_skips, stage_label="reflect"), "red"))
    89→        return None
    90→
    91→    # Parse structured disposition ledger from Coverage Ledger section
    92→    disposition_ledger = parse_reflect_dispositions(report, valid_ids)
    93→
    94→    return (
    95→        triage_input,
    96→        issue_count,
    97→        recurring,
    98→        recurring_dims,
    99→        cited_ids,
   100→        missing_ids,
   101→        duplicate_ids,
   102→        disposition_ledger,
   103→    )
   104→
   105→
   106→def _persist_reflect_stage(
   107→    *,
   108→    plan: dict,
   109→    meta: dict,
   110→    stages: dict,
   111→    report: str,
   112→    issue_count: int,
   113→    cited_ids: set[str],
   114→    missing_ids: list[str],
   115→    duplicate_ids: list[str],
   116→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   117→    disposition_ledger: list[ReflectDisposition],
   118→    existing_stage: dict | None,
   119→    is_reuse: bool,
   120→    services: TriageServices,
   121→) -> tuple[dict, list[str]]:
   122→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   123→    reflect_stage: dict = {
   124→        "stage": "reflect",
   125→        "report": report,
   126→        "cited_ids": sorted(cited_ids),
   127→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   128→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   129→        "missing_issue_ids": missing_ids,
   130→        "duplicate_issue_ids": duplicate_ids,
   131→        "recurring_dims": recurring_dims,
   132→    }
   133→    if disposition_ledger:
   134→        reflect_stage["disposition_ledger"] = [d.to_dict() for d in disposition_ledger]
   135→    stages["reflect"] = reflect_stage
   136→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   137→        reflect_stage["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   138→        reflect_stage["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   139→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "reflect")
   140→
   141→    services.save_plan(plan)
   142→    services.append_log_entry(
   143→        plan,
   144→        "triage_reflect",
   145→        actor="user",
   146→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "reuse": is_reuse, "recurring_dims": recurring_dims},
   147→    )
   148→    services.save_plan(plan)
   149→    return reflect_stage, cleared
   150→
   151→
   152→def _cmd_stage_reflect(
   153→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   154→    *,
   155→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   156→) -> None:
   157→    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current issues against completed work."""
   158→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   159→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   160→
   161→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   162→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   163→    state = runtime.state
   164→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   165→
   166→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   167→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to reflect on.", "yellow"))
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   171→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   172→    existing_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   173→
   174→    report, is_reuse = resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   175→    if not report:
   176→        _print_reflect_report_requirement()
   177→        return
   178→
   179→    submission = _validate_reflect_submission(
   180→        report=report,
   181→        plan=plan,
   182→        state=state,
   183→        stages=stages,
   184→        attestation=attestation,
   185→        services=resolved_services,
   186→    )
   187→    if submission is None:
   188→        return
   189→    (
   190→        triage_input, issue_count, recurring, recurring_dims,
   191→        cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids, disposition_ledger,
   192→    ) = submission
   193→    reflect_stage, cleared = _persist_reflect_stage(
   194→        plan=plan,
   195→        meta=meta,
   196→        stages=stages,
   197→        report=report,
   198→        issue_count=issue_count,
   199→        cited_ids=cited_ids,
   200→        missing_ids=missing_ids,
   201→        duplicate_ids=duplicate_ids,
   202→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   203→        disposition_ledger=disposition_ledger,
   204→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   205→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   206→        services=resolved_services,
   207→    )
   208→
   209→    print_reflect_result(
   210→        issue_count=issue_count,
   211→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   212→        recurring=recurring,
   213→        report=report,
   214→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   215→        cleared=cleared,
   216→        stages=stages,
   217→    )
   218→
   219→
   220→def cmd_stage_reflect(
   221→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   222→    *,
   223→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   224→) -> None:
   225→    """Public entrypoint for reflect stage recording."""
   226→    _cmd_stage_reflect(args, services=services)
   227→
   228→
   229→__all__ = ["_cmd_stage_reflect", "cmd_stage_reflect"]
```

```
1→"""Organize stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→
     9→from ..display.dashboard import print_organize_result
    10→from ..completion_flow import count_log_activity_since
    11→from ..review_coverage import open_review_ids_from_state
    12→from ..stage_queue import has_triage_in_queue
    13→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    14→from ..validation.stage_policy import (
    15→    ReflectAutoConfirmDeps,
    16→    auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize,
    17→)
    18→from ..validation.organize_policy import (
    19→    _clusters_enriched_or_error,
    20→    _manual_clusters_or_error,
    21→    _organize_report_or_error,
    22→    _unclustered_review_issues_or_error,
    23→    _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error,
    24→)
    25→from ..validation.core import _require_reflect_stage_for_organize
    26→from .records import record_organize_stage
    27→
    28→
    29→def _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
    30→    *,
    31→    plan: dict,
    32→    stages: dict,
    33→    manual_clusters: list[str],
    34→    open_review_ids: set[str],
    35→    is_reuse: bool,
    36→    attestation: str | None,
    37→) -> bool:
    38→    """Require enough cluster operations after reflect unless explicitly attested."""
    39→    if not open_review_ids:
    40→        return True
    41→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
    42→    if not reflect_ts or is_reuse:
    43→        return True
    44→    activity = count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
    45→    cluster_ops = sum(
    46→        activity.get(key, 0)
    47→        for key in ("cluster_create", "cluster_add", "cluster_update", "cluster_remove")
    48→    )
    49→    min_ops = max(3, len(manual_clusters))
    50→    if cluster_ops >= min_ops:
    51→        return True
    52→    if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 40:
    53→        print(
    54→            colorize(
    55→                f"  Note: only {cluster_ops} cluster op(s) logged (expected {min_ops}+). "
    56→                "Proceeding with attestation override.",
    57→                "yellow",
    58→            )
    59→        )
    60→        return True
    61→    print(
    62→        colorize(
    63→            f"  Cannot organize: only {cluster_ops} cluster operation(s) logged "
    64→            f"since reflect (need {min_ops}+).",
    65→            "red",
    66→        )
    67→    )
    68→    print(
    69→        colorize(
    70→            "  Cluster operations (create/add/update/remove) are logged automatically\n"
    71→            "  when you use the CLI. Did you create clusters, add issues, and enrich them?",
    72→            "dim",
    73→        )
    74→    )
    75→    print(
    76→        colorize(
    77→            '  Override: pass --attestation "reason why fewer ops are expected" (40+ chars).',
    78→            "dim",
    79→        )
    80→    )
    81→    return False
    82→
    83→
    84→def _validate_organize_submission(
    85→    *,
    86→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    87→    plan: dict,
    88→    state: dict,
    89→    stages: dict,
    90→    report: str | None,
    91→    attestation: str | None,
    92→    is_reuse: bool,
    93→    services: TriageServices,
    94→) -> tuple[list[str], str] | None:
    95→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    96→    triage_input = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    97→    if not auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
    98→        args=args,
    99→        plan=plan,
   100→        stages=stages,
   101→        attestation=attestation,
   102→        deps=ReflectAutoConfirmDeps(
   103→            triage_input=triage_input,
   104→            detect_recurring_patterns_fn=services.detect_recurring_patterns,
   105→            save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
   106→        ),
   107→    ):
   108→        return None
   109→
   110→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_or_error(plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids)
   111→    if manual_clusters is None:
   112→        return None
   113→    if not _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan):
   114→        return None
   115→    if not _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan, state):
   116→        return None
   117→    if not _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(
   118→        plan=plan, stages=stages,
   119→    ):
   120→        return None
   121→    if not _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
   122→        plan=plan,
   123→        stages=stages,
   124→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   125→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   126→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   127→        attestation=attestation,
   128→    ):
   129→        return None
   130→
   131→    normalized_report = _organize_report_or_error(report)
   132→    if normalized_report is None:
   133→        return None
   134→
   135→    from .evidence_parsing import (
   136→        format_evidence_failures,
   137→        validate_report_references_clusters,
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    cluster_ref_failures = validate_report_references_clusters(normalized_report, manual_clusters)
   141→    if cluster_ref_failures:
   142→        print(
   143→            colorize(
   144→                format_evidence_failures(cluster_ref_failures, stage_label="organize"),
   145→                "red",
   146→            )
   147→        )
   148→        return None
   149→    return manual_clusters, normalized_report
   150→
   151→
   152→def _persist_organize_stage(
   153→    *,
   154→    plan: dict,
   155→    meta: dict,
   156→    report: str,
   157→    open_review_ids: set[str],
   158→    existing_stage: dict | None,
   159→    is_reuse: bool,
   160→    manual_clusters: list[str],
   161→    services: TriageServices,
   162→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
   163→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   164→    cleared = record_organize_stage(
   165→        stages,
   166→        report=report,
   167→        issue_count=len(open_review_ids),
   168→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   169→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   170→    )
   171→    services.save_plan(plan)
   172→    services.append_log_entry(
   173→        plan,
   174→        "triage_organize",
   175→        actor="user",
   176→        detail={"cluster_count": len(manual_clusters), "reuse": is_reuse},
   177→    )
   178→    services.save_plan(plan)
   179→    return cleared, stages
   180→
   181→
   182→def _cmd_stage_organize(
   183→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   184→    *,
   185→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   186→) -> None:
   187→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment."""
   188→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   189→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   190→
   191→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   192→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   193→
   194→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   195→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
   196→        return
   197→
   198→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   199→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   200→    existing_stage = stages.get("organize")
   201→
   202→    is_reuse = False
   203→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   204→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   205→        is_reuse = True
   206→
   207→    if not _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages):
   208→        return
   209→
   210→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   211→    state = runtime.state
   212→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   213→
   214→    validated = _validate_organize_submission(
   215→        args=args,
   216→        plan=plan,
   217→        state=state,
   218→        stages=stages,
   219→        report=report,
   220→        attestation=attestation,
   221→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   222→        services=resolved_services,
   223→    )
   224→    if validated is None:
   225→        return
   226→    manual_clusters, normalized_report = validated
   227→    cleared, stages = _persist_organize_stage(
   228→        plan=plan,
   229→        meta=meta,
   230→        report=normalized_report,
   231→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   232→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   233→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   234→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   235→        services=resolved_services,
   236→    )
   237→
   238→    print_organize_result(
   239→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   240→        plan=plan,
   241→        report=normalized_report,
   242→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   243→        cleared=cleared,
   244→        stages=stages,
   245→    )
   246→
   247→
   248→def cmd_stage_organize(
```

```
1→"""Review-coverage helpers for triage planning."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.shared.cluster_membership import cluster_issue_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import open_review_ids
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import Cluster, PlanModel
     9→
    10→from .plan_state_access import (
    11→    ensure_cluster_map,
    12→    ensure_queue_order,
    13→    ensure_skipped_map,
    14→    ensure_triage_meta,
    15→    normalized_issue_id_list,
    16→)
    17→
    18→[REDACTED]
    19→[REDACTED]
    20→[REDACTED]
    21→
    22→
    23→def open_review_ids_from_state(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    24→    """Return open review IDs from the current state snapshot."""
    25→    return open_review_ids(state)
    26→
    27→
    28→def has_open_review_issues(state: StateModel | dict | None) -> bool:
    29→    """Return True when any open review issues exist."""
    30→    return bool(open_review_ids_from_state(state or {}))
    31→
    32→
    33→def plan_review_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
    34→    """Return review/concerns IDs currently represented in queue_order."""
    35→    return [
    36→        fid
    37→        for fid in ensure_queue_order(plan)
    38→        if not fid.startswith("triage::")
    39→        and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
    40→        and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
    41→    ]
    42→
    43→
    44→def coverage_open_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    45→    """Return the frozen or live open review IDs covered by this triage run."""
    46→    active_ids = normalized_issue_id_list(
    47→        ensure_triage_meta(plan).get(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY)
    48→    )
    49→    if active_ids:
    50→        return set(active_ids)
    51→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    52→    review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    53→    if not has_completed_scan and not review_ids:
    54→        return set(plan_review_ids(plan))
    55→    return review_ids
    56→
    57→
    58→def active_triage_issue_ids(
    59→    plan: PlanModel,
    60→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    61→) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return the frozen review issue set for the current triage run."""
    63→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
    64→    active_ids = normalized_issue_id_list(meta.get(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY))
    65→    if active_ids:
    66→        return set(active_ids)
    67→    if state is None:
    68→        return set()
    69→    return coverage_open_ids(plan, state)
    70→
    71→
    72→def live_active_triage_issue_ids(
    73→    plan: PlanModel,
    74→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    75→) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return frozen triage IDs that are still open review issues in state."""
    77→    frozen_ids = active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    78→    if state is None or not frozen_ids:
    79→        return frozen_ids
    80→    return frozen_ids & open_review_ids(state)
    81→
    82→
    83→def ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    84→    """Freeze the current triage issue set for validation across stage reruns."""
    85→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
    86→    active_ids = sorted(coverage_open_ids(plan, state))
    87→    meta[_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY] = active_ids
    88→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
    89→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
    90→    return active_ids
    91→
    92→
    93→def clear_active_triage_issue_tracking(meta: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    94→    """Clear frozen triage coverage metadata after successful completion."""
    95→    meta.pop(_ACTIVE_TRIAGE_ISSUE_IDS_KEY, None)
    96→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
    97→    meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
    98→
    99→
   100→def undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(
   101→    plan: PlanModel,
   102→    state: StateModel | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Return frozen triage issues still lacking cluster/skip/dismiss coverage."""
   105→    target_ids = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   106→    if not target_ids:
   107→        return []
   108→    covered_ids: set[str] = set()
   109→    for cluster in ensure_cluster_map(plan).values():
   110→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   111→            continue
   112→        covered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   113→    covered_ids.update(
   114→        issue_id for issue_id in ensure_skipped_map(plan) if isinstance(issue_id, str)
   115→    )
   116→    covered_ids.update(
   117→        normalized_issue_id_list(ensure_triage_meta(plan).get("dismissed_ids"))
   118→    )
   119→    return sorted(issue_id for issue_id in target_ids if issue_id not in covered_ids)
   120→
   121→
   122→def sync_undispositioned_triage_meta(
   123→    plan: PlanModel,
   124→    state: StateModel | None = None,
   125→) -> list[str]:
   126→    """Persist the current undispositioned triage issue set for recovery UX."""
   127→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
   128→    missing = undispositioned_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   129→    if missing:
   130→        meta[_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY] = missing
   131→        meta[_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY] = len(missing)
   132→    else:
   133→        meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_ISSUES_KEY, None)
   134→        meta.pop(_UNDISPOSITIONED_TRIAGE_COUNT_KEY, None)
   135→    return missing
   136→
   137→
   138→def triage_coverage(
   139→    plan: PlanModel,
   140→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   141→) -> tuple[int, int, dict[str, Cluster]]:
   142→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage."""
   143→    clusters = ensure_cluster_map(plan)
   144→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
   145→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   146→        all_cluster_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   147→    review_ids = list(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else plan_review_ids(plan)
   148→    organized = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
   149→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   150→
   151→
   152→def manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
   153→    """Return manual clusters that currently own at least one issue."""
   154→    return [
   155→        name
   156→        for name, cluster in ensure_cluster_map(plan).items()
   157→        if cluster_issue_ids(cluster) and not cluster.get("auto")
   158→    ]
   159→
   160→
   161→def find_cluster_for(fid: str, clusters: dict[str, Cluster]) -> str | None:
   162→    """Return the owning cluster name for an issue ID, if any."""
   163→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   164→        if fid in cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   165→            return name
   166→    return None
   167→
   168→
   169→__all__ = [

   174→    "ensure_active_triage_issue_ids",
   175→    "find_cluster_for",

   177→    "live_active_triage_issue_ids",
```

```
1→"""Queue/stage mutation helpers for triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import purge_ids
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanModel
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_IDS, TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    12→
    13→from .plan_state_access import ensure_queue_order, ensure_skipped_map
    14→
    15→STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check"]
    16→
    17→
    18→def has_triage_in_queue(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    19→    """Return True when any triage stage IDs are currently queued."""
    20→    order = set(ensure_queue_order(plan))
    21→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    22→
    23→
    24→def clear_triage_stage_skips(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    25→    """Remove skipped markers for triage stages before reinjection."""
    26→    skipped = ensure_skipped_map(plan)
    27→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    28→        skipped.pop(sid, None)
    29→
    30→
    31→def inject_triage_stages(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    32→    """Inject the canonical triage stage IDs at the queue front."""
    33→    order = ensure_queue_order(plan)
    34→    clear_triage_stage_skips(plan)
    35→    remaining = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in TRIAGE_IDS]
    36→    order[:] = [*remaining, *TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS]
    37→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
    38→
    39→
    40→def purge_triage_stage(plan: PlanModel, stage_name: str) -> None:
    41→    """Remove one triage stage workflow item from the plan."""
    42→    purge_ids(plan, [f"triage::{stage_name}"])
    43→
    44→
    45→def cascade_clear_later_confirmations(
    46→    stages: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    47→    from_stage: str,
    48→) -> list[str]:
    49→    """Clear later-stage confirmations after mutating an earlier stage."""
    50→    try:
    51→        idx = STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    52→    except ValueError:
    53→        return []
    54→    cleared: list[str] = []
    55→    for later in STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    56→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    57→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    58→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    59→            cleared.append(later)
    60→    return cleared
    61→
    62→
    63→def print_cascade_clear_feedback(
    64→    cleared: list[str],
    65→    stages: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    66→) -> None:
    67→    """Render confirmation-clearing feedback after stage rewrites."""
    68→    if not cleared:
    69→        return
    70→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    71→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    72→        (
    73→            stage
    74→            for stage in STAGE_ORDER
    75→            if stage in stages and not stages[stage].get("confirmed_at")
    76→        ),
    77→        None,
    78→    )
    79→    if next_unconfirmed:
    80→        print(
    81→            colorize(
    82→                f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan triage --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
    83→                "dim",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→
    87→
    88→__all__ = [
    89→    "STAGE_ORDER",
    90→    "cascade_clear_later_confirmations",
    91→    "has_triage_in_queue",
    92→    "inject_triage_stages",
    93→    "print_cascade_clear_feedback",
    94→    "purge_triage_stage",
    95→]
    96→
```

```
1→"""Structured evidence parsing and validation for triage stage submissions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→
     9→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10→# Shared constants
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→
    13→VERDICT_KEYWORDS = frozenset({
    14→    "genuine",
    15→    "false positive",
    16→    "false-positive",
    17→    "exaggerated",
    18→    "over-engineering",
    19→    "over engineering",
    20→    "not-worth-it",
    21→    "not worth it",
    22→})
    23→
    24→# Permissive path regex — detects whether *any* file path was mentioned.
    25→# NOT used to validate existence (that's enrich-only in validation.enrich_checks).
    26→_EVIDENCE_PATH_RE = re.compile(r"[\w.@-]+/[\w./@-]+\.\w+")
    27→
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→# Data classes
    30→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31→
    32→@dataclass
    33→class EvidenceFailure:
    34→    """A single evidence validation failure."""
    35→
    36→    code: str
    37→    message: str
    38→    blocking: bool = True
    39→
    40→
    41→@dataclass
    42→class ObserveAssessment:
    43→    """A parsed per-issue assessment from an observe report (template format)."""
    44→
    45→    issue_hash: str
    46→    verdict: str  # normalised keyword
    47→    verdict_reasoning: str = ""
    48→    files_read: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    49→    recommendation: str = ""
    50→
    51→
    52→@dataclass
    53→class ObserveEvidence:
    54→    """Parsed observe-report evidence."""
    55→
    56→    entries: list[ObserveAssessment] = field(default_factory=list)
    57→    unparsed_citation_count: int = 0  # hashes cited without a verdict
    58→    has_parseable_ids: bool = True  # False if valid_ids had no hex-hash IDs
    59→
    60→
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→# OBSERVE evidence parsing — structured template format
    63→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    64→
    65→def _normalise_verdict(raw: str) -> str | None:
    66→    """Return a normalised verdict keyword, or None if not recognised."""
    67→    lower = raw.lower().strip()
    68→    for kw in VERDICT_KEYWORDS:
    69→        if kw in lower:
    70→            # Normalise to canonical form
    71→            canonical = kw.replace("-", " ")
    72→            return canonical
    73→    return None
    74→
    75→
    76→def _build_short_map(valid_ids: set[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    77→    """Build short-hash → full-id map from valid issue IDs."""
    78→    short_map: dict[str, str] = {}
    79→    for fid in valid_ids:
    80→        short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
    81→        if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{6,}", short):
    82→            short_map[short] = fid
    83→    return short_map
    84→
    85→
    86→def _is_valid_hash(raw_hash: str, short_map: dict[str, str], valid_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    87→    """Check if a hash is known in either the short map or valid IDs."""
    88→    return raw_hash in short_map or raw_hash in valid_ids
    89→
    90→
    91→# --- YAML-like template parser ---
    92→
    93→# Matches lines like:  - hash: abc12345  or  hash: abc12345
    94→_YAML_HASH_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*-?\s*hash\s*:\s*([0-9a-f]{6,})", re.IGNORECASE)
    95→# Matches lines like:  verdict: genuine  or  verdict: false-positive
    96→_YAML_VERDICT_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*verdict\s*:\s*(.+)", re.IGNORECASE)
    97→# Matches lines like:  verdict_reasoning: some reason
    98→_YAML_REASONING_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*verdict_reasoning\s*:\s*(.+)", re.IGNORECASE)
    99→# Matches lines like:  files_read: [src/foo.ts, src/bar.ts]  or  files_read: src/foo.ts
   100→_YAML_FILES_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*files_read\s*:\s*(.+)", re.IGNORECASE)
   101→# Matches lines like:  recommendation: do something
   102→_YAML_RECOMMENDATION_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*recommendation\s*:\s*(.+)", re.IGNORECASE)
   103→
   104→def _parse_yaml_files_field(raw: str) -> list[str]:
   105→    """Parse the files_read field — supports [a, b] list or single path."""
   106→    raw = raw.strip()
   107→    # Strip surrounding brackets
   108→    if raw.startswith("[") and raw.endswith("]"):
   109→        raw = raw[1:-1]
   110→    # Split on commas
   111→    paths = [p.strip().strip("'\"") for p in raw.split(",")]
   112→    return [p for p in paths if p]
   113→
   114→
   115→def _flush_yaml_entry(
   116→    current: dict,
   117→    short_map: dict[str, str],
   118→    valid_ids: set[str],
   119→) -> ObserveAssessment | None:
   120→    """Convert a collected YAML-like entry dict into an ObserveAssessment, or None if invalid."""
   121→    raw_hash = current.get("hash", "").lower()
   122→    if not raw_hash or not _is_valid_hash(raw_hash, short_map, valid_ids):
   123→        return None
   124→    raw_verdict = current.get("verdict", "")
   125→    verdict = _normalise_verdict(raw_verdict)
   126→    if not verdict:
   127→        return None
   128→    return ObserveAssessment(
   129→        issue_hash=raw_hash,
   130→        verdict=verdict,
   131→        verdict_reasoning=current.get("verdict_reasoning", ""),
   132→        files_read=current.get("files_read", []),
   133→        recommendation=current.get("recommendation", ""),
   134→    )
   135→
   136→
   137→def _parse_yaml_template(
   138→    report: str,
   139→    short_map: dict[str, str],
   140→    valid_ids: set[str],
   141→) -> list[ObserveAssessment]:
   142→    """Parse YAML-like template entries from report text."""
   143→    entries: list[ObserveAssessment] = []
   144→    current: dict | None = None
   145→
   146→    for line in report.splitlines():
   147→        m_hash = _YAML_HASH_RE.match(line)
   148→        if m_hash:
   149→            # Flush previous entry
   150→            if current is not None:
   151→                entry = _flush_yaml_entry(current, short_map, valid_ids)
   152→                if entry:
   153→                    entries.append(entry)
   154→            current = {"hash": m_hash.group(1).lower()}
   155→            continue
   156→
   157→        if current is None:
   158→            continue
   159→
   160→        m_verdict = _YAML_VERDICT_RE.match(line)
   161→        if m_verdict:
   162→            current["verdict"] = m_verdict.group(1).strip()
   163→            continue
   164→
   165→        m_reasoning = _YAML_REASONING_RE.match(line)
   166→        if m_reasoning:
   167→            current["verdict_reasoning"] = m_reasoning.group(1).strip()
   168→            continue
   169→
   170→        m_files = _YAML_FILES_RE.match(line)
   171→        if m_files:
   172→            current["files_read"] = _parse_yaml_files_field(m_files.group(1))
   173→            continue
   174→
   175→        m_rec = _YAML_RECOMMENDATION_RE.match(line)
   176→        if m_rec:
   177→            current["recommendation"] = m_rec.group(1).strip()
   178→            continue
   179→
   180→    # Flush final entry
   181→    if current is not None:
   182→        entry = _flush_yaml_entry(current, short_map, valid_ids)
   183→        if entry:
   184→            entries.append(entry)
   185→
   186→    return entries
   187→
   188→
   189→def parse_observe_evidence(report: str, valid_ids: set[str]) -> ObserveEvidence:
   190→    """Parse assessment entries from an observe-stage report.
   191→
   192→    Supports the YAML-like template format:
   193→    hash/verdict/verdict_reasoning/files_read/recommendation
   194→    """
   195→    short_map = _build_short_map(valid_ids)
   196→    entries = _parse_yaml_template(report, short_map, valid_ids)
   197→
   198→    # Count hashes that appear in report but weren't parsed as entries
   199→    all_hashes_in_report = set(re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", report.lower()))
   200→    valid_short_hashes = set(short_map.keys())
   201→    cited_hashes = all_hashes_in_report & valid_short_hashes
   202→    parsed_hashes = {e.issue_hash for e in entries}
   203→    unparsed = len(cited_hashes - parsed_hashes)
   204→
   205→    return ObserveEvidence(
   206→        entries=entries,
   207→        unparsed_citation_count=unparsed,
   208→        has_parseable_ids=bool(short_map),
   209→    )
   210→
   211→
   212→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   213→# OBSERVE evidence validation — field presence only
   214→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   215→
   216→_OBSERVE_TEMPLATE_HINT = (
   217→    "  Required template per issue:\n"
   218→    "    - hash: <hash>\n"
   219→    "      verdict: genuine | false-positive | exaggerated | over-engineering | not-worth-it\n"
   220→    "      verdict_reasoning: <why this verdict>\n"
   221→    "      files_read: [<file paths>]\n"
   222→    "      recommendation: <what to do>"
   223→)
   224→
   225→
   226→def validate_observe_evidence(
   227→    evidence: ObserveEvidence,
   228→    issue_count: int,
   229→) -> list[EvidenceFailure]:
   230→    """Validate parsed observe evidence — field presence only, no quality thresholds."""
   231→    if issue_count == 0:
   232→        return []
   233→
   234→    # If valid_ids contained no hex-hash IDs, verdict parsing is impossible — skip.
   235→    if not evidence.has_parseable_ids:
   236→        return []
   237→
   238→    failures: list[EvidenceFailure] = []
   239→
   240→    if not evidence.entries:
   241→        failures.append(EvidenceFailure(
   242→            code="no_verdicts",
   243→            message=(
   244→                "No per-issue assessment entries found in report.\n"
   245→                "\n"
   246→                f"{_OBSERVE_TEMPLATE_HINT}\n"
   247→                "\n"
   248→                "  Example:\n"
   249→                "    - hash: 34580232\n"
   250→                "      verdict: false-positive\n"
   251→                "      verdict_reasoning: Uses branded string union KnownTaskType\n"
   252→                "      files_read: [src/types/database.ts]\n"
   253→                "      recommendation: No action needed"
   254→            ),
   255→        ))
   256→        return failures
   257→
   258→    # Check field presence — no quality thresholds
   259→    missing_reasoning: list[ObserveAssessment] = []
   260→    missing_files: list[ObserveAssessment] = []
   261→    missing_recommendation: list[ObserveAssessment] = []
   262→
   263→    for entry in evidence.entries:
   264→        if not entry.verdict_reasoning.strip():
   265→            missing_reasoning.append(entry)
   266→        if not entry.files_read:
   267→            missing_files.append(entry)
   268→        if not entry.recommendation.strip():
   269→            missing_recommendation.append(entry)
   270→
   271→    if missing_reasoning:
   272→        examples = "\n".join(
   273→            f"    [{e.issue_hash}] {e.verdict}" for e in missing_reasoning[:5]
   274→        )
   275→        failures.append(EvidenceFailure(
   276→            code="missing_verdict_reasoning",
   277→            message=(
   278→                f"{len(missing_reasoning)} of {len(evidence.entries)} assessment(s) "
   279→                f"have no verdict_reasoning.\n"
   280→                "\n"
   281→                f"{_OBSERVE_TEMPLATE_HINT}\n"
   282→                "\n"
   283→                f"  Entries missing reasoning:\n{examples}"
   284→            ),
   285→        ))
   286→
   287→    if missing_files:
   288→        examples = "\n".join(
   289→            f"    [{e.issue_hash}] {e.verdict}" for e in missing_files[:5]
   290→        )
   291→        failures.append(EvidenceFailure(
   292→            code="missing_files_read",
   293→            message=(
   294→                f"{len(missing_files)} of {len(evidence.entries)} assessment(s) "
   295→                f"have no files_read.\n"
   296→                "\n"
   297→                f"{_OBSERVE_TEMPLATE_HINT}\n"
   298→                "\n"
   299→                f"  Entries missing files_read:\n{examples}"
   300→            ),
   301→        ))
   302→
   303→    if missing_recommendation:
   304→        examples = "\n".join(
   305→            f"    [{e.issue_hash}] {e.verdict}" for e in missing_recommendation[:5]
   306→        )
   307→        failures.append(EvidenceFailure(
   308→            code="missing_recommendation",
   309→            message=(
   310→                f"{len(missing_recommendation)} of {len(evidence.entries)} assessment(s) "
   311→                f"have no recommendation.\n"
   312→                "\n"
   313→                f"{_OBSERVE_TEMPLATE_HINT}\n"
   314→                "\n"
   315→                f"  Entries missing recommendation:\n{examples}"
   316→            ),
   317→        ))
   318→
   319→    return failures
   320→
   321→
   322→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   323→# REFLECT skip-reason validation
   324→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   325→
   326→_SKIP_LINE_RE = re.compile(
   327→    r"(?:skip|dismiss|defer|drop|remove)\s*:\s*\[?([0-9a-f]{6,})\]?\s*[(\-—–:]*\s*(.+)",
   328→    re.IGNORECASE,
   329→)
   330→
   331→
   332→def validate_reflect_skip_evidence(report: str) -> list[EvidenceFailure]:
   333→    """Validate that skip reasons in a reflect report are non-empty."""
   334→    failures: list[EvidenceFailure] = []
   335→    bad_skips: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   336→
   337→    for line in report.splitlines():
   338→        m = _SKIP_LINE_RE.search(line)
   339→        if not m:
   340→            continue
   341→        issue_hash = m.group(1)
   342→        reason = m.group(2).strip().rstrip(")")
   343→
   344→        # Just check non-empty reason
   345→        if not reason.strip():
   346→            bad_skips.append((issue_hash, reason))
   347→
   348→    if bad_skips:
   349→        failing_examples = "\n".join(
   350→            f'    [{h}] "{r}"' for h, r in bad_skips[:5]
   351→        )
   352→        failures.append(EvidenceFailure(
   353→            code="vague_skip_reason",
   354→            message=(
   355→                f"{len(bad_skips)} skip reason(s) are empty.\n"
   356→                "\n"
   357→                "  Required format:\n"
   358→                "    Skip: [<hash>] (<reason for skipping>)\n"
   359→                "\n"
   360→                f"  Failing skips:\n{failing_examples}"
   361→            ),
   362→        ))
   363→
   364→    return failures
   365→
   366→
   367→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   368→# Cluster-name mention check (organize + sense-check)
   369→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   370→
   371→def validate_report_references_clusters(
   372→    report: str,
   373→    cluster_names: list[str],
   374→) -> list[EvidenceFailure]:
   375→    """Check that at least one cluster name appears in the report."""
   376→    if not cluster_names:
   377→        return []
   378→
   379→    report_lower = report.lower()
   380→    for name in cluster_names:
   381→        if name.lower() in report_lower:
   382→            return []
   383→
   384→    failures: list[EvidenceFailure] = []
   385→    names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:5])
   386→    if len(cluster_names) > 5:
   387→        names_str += f" ... ({len(cluster_names)} total)"
   388→    failures.append(EvidenceFailure(
   389→        code="no_cluster_mention",
   390→        message=(
   391→            f"Report references none of the {len(cluster_names)} cluster name(s).\n"
   392→            "\n"
   393→            "  The report must mention at least one cluster by name to prove\n"
   394→            "  awareness of the plan structure.\n"
   395→            "\n"
   396→            f"  Cluster names: {names_str}"
   397→        ),
   398→    ))
   399→    return failures
   400→
   401→
   402→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   403→# File path mention check (sense-check)
   404→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   405→
   406→def validate_report_has_file_paths(report: str) -> list[EvidenceFailure]:
   407→    """Check that at least one file path appears in the report."""
   408→    if _EVIDENCE_PATH_RE.search(report):
   409→        return []
   410→    return [EvidenceFailure(
   411→        code="no_file_paths_in_report",
   412→        message=(
   413→            "Report references no file paths (need at least 1).\n"
   414→            "\n"
   415→            "  The sense-check report must prove code was read. Include specific\n"
   416→            "  file paths and line numbers for verified steps.\n"
   417→            "\n"
   418→            "  Example:\n"
   419→            "    Verified src/services/funds.ts lines 45-67: function signature\n"
   420→            "    matches step description. Effort tag 'small' is accurate."
   421→        ),
   422→    )]
   423→
   424→
   425→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   426→# Shared output helpers
   427→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   428→
   429→def format_evidence_failures(
   430→    failures: list[EvidenceFailure],
   431→    *,
   432→    stage_label: str,
   433→) -> str:
   434→    """Format evidence failures into a single rejection message."""
   435→    blocking = [f for f in failures if f.blocking]
   436→    advisory = [f for f in failures if not f.blocking]
   437→    parts: list[str] = []
   438→
   439→    for f in blocking:
   440→        parts.append(f"  REJECTED: {f.message}")
   441→
   442→    for f in advisory:
   443→        parts.append(f"  Warning: {f.message}")
   444→
   445→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   446→
   447→
   448→__all__ = [
   449→    "EvidenceFailure",
   450→    "ObserveAssessment",
   451→    "ObserveEvidence",
   452→    "VERDICT_KEYWORDS",
   453→    "format_evidence_failures",
   454→    "parse_observe_evidence",
   455→    "validate_observe_evidence",
   456→    "validate_reflect_skip_evidence",
   457→    "validate_report_has_file_paths",
   458→    "validate_report_references_clusters",
   459→]
   460→
```

```
     2→

    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class ActualDisposition:
    17→    """What actually happened to an issue in plan state."""
    18→
    19→    kind: Literal["clustered", "skipped", "unplaced"]
    20→    cluster_name: str = ""
    21→
    22→    def describe(self, intended: ReflectDisposition | None = None) -> str:
    23→        """Human-readable description for error messages."""
    24→        if self.kind == "skipped":
    25→            return "permanently skipped"
    26→        if self.kind == "clustered":
    27→            if intended and intended.decision == "cluster" and self.cluster_name != intended.target:
    28→                return f'in cluster "{self.cluster_name}" (expected "{intended.target}")'
    29→            return f'clustered in "{self.cluster_name}"'
    30→        if self.kind == "unplaced":
    31→            if intended and intended.decision == "cluster":
    32→                return "not in any cluster"
    33→            return "not skipped, not clustered"
    34→        return "unknown state"

    37→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    38→class LedgerMismatch:

    41→    intended: ReflectDisposition

    55→

    61→def _manual_clusters_or_error(
    62→    plan: dict,
    63→    *,
    64→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    65→) -> list[str] | None:
    66→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    67→    if manual_clusters:
    68→        return manual_clusters
    69→    if open_review_ids is not None and not open_review_ids:
    70→        return []
    71→    any_clusters = [

    73→        if cluster_issue_ids(cluster)

    76→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: only auto-clusters exist.", "red"))
    77→        print(colorize("  Create manual clusters that group issues by root cause:", "dim"))
    78→    else:
    79→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
    80→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
    81→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>", "dim"))

    90→    for name, missing in gaps:

    97→            "dim",
    98→        )
    99→    )
   100→    return False
   101→
   102→
   103→def _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   104→    """Block if open review issues aren't in any manual cluster. Return True if OK."""
   105→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   106→    if not unclustered:
   107→        return True
   108→    print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(unclustered)} review issue(s) have no cluster.", "red"))
   109→    for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   110→        short = fid.rsplit("::", 2)[-2] if "::" in fid else fid
   111→        print(colorize(f"    {short}", "yellow"))
   112→    if len(unclustered) > 10:
   113→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "yellow"))
   114→    print()
   115→    print(colorize("  Every review issue needs an action plan. Either:", "dim"))
   116→    print(colorize("    1. Add to a cluster: desloppify plan cluster add <name> <pattern>", "dim"))
   117→    print(
   118→        colorize(
   119→            '    2. Wontfix it: desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "reason" --attest "..."',
   120→            "dim",
   121→        )
   122→    )
   123→    return False
   124→
   125→
   126→def _organize_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   127→    if not report:
   128→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage organize.", "red"))
   129→        print(colorize("  Summarize your prioritized organization:", "dim"))
   130→        print(colorize("  - Did you defer contradictory issues before clustering?", "dim"))
   131→        print(colorize("  - What clusters did you create and why?", "dim"))
   132→        print(colorize("  - Explicit priority ordering: which cluster 1st, 2nd, 3rd and why?", "dim"))
   133→        print(colorize("  - What depends on what? What unblocks the most?", "dim"))
   134→        return None
   135→    if len(report) < 100:
   136→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   137→        print(colorize("  Explain what you organized, your priorities, and focus order.", "dim"))
   138→        return None
   139→    return report
   140→
   141→
   142→def _build_actual_disposition_index(plan: dict) -> dict[str, ActualDisposition]:
   143→    """Build a lookup from issue ID to its actual disposition in plan state."""
   144→    index: dict[str, ActualDisposition] = {}
   145→
   146→    for cluster_name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   147→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   148→            continue
   149→        for fid in cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   150→            index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="clustered", cluster_name=cluster_name)
   151→
   152→    for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}):
   153→        if isinstance(fid, str):
   154→            index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="skipped")
   155→
   156→    return index
   157→
   158→
   159→def _disposition_matches(intended: ReflectDisposition, actual: ActualDisposition) -> bool:
   160→    """True when the actual plan state satisfies the intended disposition."""
   161→    if intended.decision == "permanent_skip":
   162→        return actual.kind == "skipped"
   163→    if intended.decision == "cluster":
   164→        return actual.kind == "clustered" and actual.cluster_name == intended.target
   165→    return False
   166→
   167→
   168→def validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   169→    *,
   170→    plan: dict,
   171→    stages: dict,
   172→) -> list[LedgerMismatch]:
   173→    """Check that plan mutations match the reflect disposition ledger."""
   174→    raw_ledger = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("disposition_ledger")
   175→    if not raw_ledger:
   176→        return []
   177→
   178→    ledger = [ReflectDisposition.from_dict(d) for d in raw_ledger]
   179→    actuals = _build_actual_disposition_index(plan)
   180→    unplaced = ActualDisposition(kind="unplaced")
   181→
   182→    return [
   183→        LedgerMismatch(intended=d, actual=actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
   184→        for d in ledger
   185→        if not _disposition_matches(d, actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
   186→    ]
   187→
   188→
   189→def _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(
   190→    *,
   191→    plan: dict,
   192→    stages: dict,
   193→) -> bool:
   194→    """Block organize if plan state diverges from the reflect disposition ledger."""
   195→    mismatches = validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   196→        plan=plan,
   197→        stages=stages,
   198→    )
   199→    if not mismatches:
   200→        return True
   201→
   202→    print(
   203→        colorize(
   204→            f"  Cannot organize: {len(mismatches)} issue(s) diverge from the reflect plan.",
   205→            "red",
   206→        )
   207→    )
   208→    for mismatch in mismatches[:10]:
   209→        short_id = mismatch.issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
   210→        print(
   211→            colorize(
   212→                f"    {short_id}: reflect said {mismatch.expected_decision} "
   213→                f'"{mismatch.expected_target}", but {mismatch.actual_state}',
   214→                "yellow",
   215→            )
   216→        )
   217→    if len(mismatches) > 10:
   218→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(mismatches) - 10} more", "yellow"))
   219→    print()
   220→    print(
   221→        colorize(
   222→            "  Fix the plan to match the reflect ledger, or re-run reflect to update dispositions.",
   223→            "dim",
   224→        )
   225→    )
   226→    return False
   227→
   228→
   229→__all__ = [
   230→    "ActualDisposition",
   231→    "LedgerMismatch",
   232→    "_clusters_enriched_or_error",
   233→    "_manual_clusters_or_error",
```
