```
1→"""Reflect-stage coverage-ledger parsing and issue-accounting helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from collections import Counter
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→from typing import Literal
     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import extract_issue_citations
    12→
    13→DecisionKind = Literal["cluster", "permanent_skip"]
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class ReflectDisposition:
    18→    """One issue's intended disposition as declared by the reflect stage."""
    19→
    20→    issue_id: str
    21→    decision: DecisionKind
    22→    target: str
    23→
    24→    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
    25→        """Serialize for JSON persistence in ``plan.json``."""
    26→        return {"issue_id": self.issue_id, "decision": self.decision, "target": self.target}
    27→
    28→    @classmethod
    29→    def from_dict(cls, data: dict | ReflectDisposition) -> ReflectDisposition:
    30→        """Deserialize from persisted plan data, or pass through unchanged."""
    31→        if isinstance(data, cls):
    32→            return data
    33→        issue_id = data.get("issue_id", "")
    34→        target = data.get("target", "")
    35→        decision = _decision_kind_or_none(data.get("decision")) or "cluster"
    36→        return cls(
    37→            issue_id=issue_id if isinstance(issue_id, str) else "",
    38→            decision=decision,
    39→            target=target if isinstance(target, str) else "",
    40→        )
    41→
    42→
    43→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    44→class _IdResolutionMaps:
    45→    """Pre-built lookup structures for resolving ledger tokens to issue IDs."""
    46→
    47→    short_id_buckets: dict[str, list[str]]
    48→    short_hex_map: dict[str, str]
    49→    slug_prefix_map: dict[str, str]
    50→
    51→
    52→def _build_id_resolution_maps(valid_ids: set[str]) -> _IdResolutionMaps:
    53→    short_id_buckets: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    54→    short_hex_map: dict[str, str] = {}
    55→    slug_prefix_map: dict[str, str] = {}
    56→    ambiguous_slugs: set[str] = set()
    57→    for issue_id in sorted(valid_ids):
    58→        parts = issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)
    59→        short_id = parts[-1]
    60→        slug = parts[0] if len(parts) == 2 else ""
    61→        short_id_buckets.setdefault(short_id, []).append(issue_id)
    62→        if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", short_id):
    63→            existing = short_hex_map.get(short_id)
    64→            if existing is None:
    65→                short_hex_map[short_id] = issue_id
    66→            elif existing != issue_id:
    67→                short_hex_map.pop(short_id, None)
    68→        if not slug:
    69→            continue
    70→        if slug in ambiguous_slugs:
    71→            continue
    72→        if slug in slug_prefix_map:
    73→            slug_prefix_map.pop(slug)
    74→            ambiguous_slugs.add(slug)
    75→            continue
    76→        slug_prefix_map[slug] = issue_id
    77→    return _IdResolutionMaps(
    78→        short_id_buckets=short_id_buckets,
    79→        short_hex_map=short_hex_map,
    80→        slug_prefix_map=slug_prefix_map,
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def _clean_ledger_token(raw: str) -> str:
    85→    token = raw.strip().strip("`").strip()
    86→    if token.startswith("[") and token.endswith("]"):
    87→        token = token[1:-1].strip()
    88→    return token
    89→
    90→
    91→_CANONICAL_LEDGER_PATTERNS = (
    92→    re.compile(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*->\s*(\w+)\s+[\"']([^\"']+)[\"']"),
    93→    re.compile(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*->\s*(\w+)\s+(\S+.*)"),
    94→)
    95→_COMPAT_LEDGER_PATTERNS = (
    96→    re.compile(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*:\s*(\w+)\s+[\"']?([^\"']+?)[\"']?\s*$"),
    97→    re.compile(r"-\s*([^,]+),\s*(\w+),\s*[\"']?([^\"',]+?)[\"']?\s*$"),
    98→)
    99→_TOKEN_ONLY_PATTERNS = (
   100→    re.compile(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*->"),
   101→    re.compile(r"-\s+(\S+)\s*$"),
   102→)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _parse_ledger_line_with_patterns(
   106→    line: str,
   107→    patterns: tuple[re.Pattern[str], ...],
   108→) -> tuple[str, str | None, str | None]:
   109→    for pattern in patterns:
   110→        match = pattern.match(line)
   111→        if match:
   112→            [REDACTED](match.group(1))
   113→            return token, match.group(2).strip().lower(), match.group(3).strip().strip("\"'")
   114→    return "", None, None
   115→
   116→
   117→def _parse_token_only_ledger_line(line: str) -> tuple[str, str | None, str | None]:
   118→    for pattern in _TOKEN_ONLY_PATTERNS:
   119→        match = pattern.match(line)
   120→        if not match:
   121→            continue
   122→        [REDACTED](match.group(1))
   123→        if token:
   124→            return token, None, None
   125→    return "", None, None
   126→
   127→
   128→def _extract_ledger_entry(line: str) -> tuple[str, str | None, str | None]:
   129→    """Parse one ledger line into ``(token, decision, target)``."""
   130→    token, decision, target = _parse_ledger_line_with_patterns(line, _CANONICAL_LEDGER_PATTERNS)
   131→    if token:
   132→        return token, decision, target
   133→
   134→    token, decision, target = _parse_ledger_line_with_patterns(line, _COMPAT_LEDGER_PATTERNS)
   135→    if token:
   136→        return token, decision, target
   137→
   138→    return _parse_token_only_ledger_line(line)
   139→
   140→
   141→def _resolve_token_to_id(
   142→    token: str,
   143→    valid_ids: set[str],
   144→    maps: _IdResolutionMaps,
   145→    short_id_usage: Counter[str],
   146→) -> str | None:
   147→    if token in valid_ids:
   148→        return token
   149→    bucket = maps.short_id_buckets.get(token)
   150→    if bucket:
   151→        bucket_index = short_id_usage[token]
   152→        resolved = bucket[bucket_index] if bucket_index < len(bucket) else bucket[-1]
   153→        short_id_usage[token] += 1
   154→        return resolved
   155→    for hex_token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", token):
   156→        resolved = maps.short_hex_map.get(hex_token)
   157→        if resolved:
   158→            return resolved
   159→    return maps.slug_prefix_map.get(token.lower())
   160→
   161→
   162→_CLUSTER_DECISIONS = frozenset({"cluster"})
   163→_SKIP_DECISIONS = frozenset({"skip", "dismiss", "defer", "drop", "remove"})
   164→
   165→
   166→def _normalize_decision(raw: str) -> str:
   167→    lower = raw.lower()
   168→    if lower in _CLUSTER_DECISIONS:
   169→        return "cluster"
   170→    if lower in _SKIP_DECISIONS:
   171→        return "permanent_skip"
   172→    return lower
   173→
   174→
   175→def _decision_kind_or_none(raw: object) -> DecisionKind | None:
   176→    if not isinstance(raw, str):
   177→        return None
   178→    normalized = _normalize_decision(raw)
   179→    if normalized == "cluster":
   180→        return "cluster"
   181→    if normalized == "permanent_skip":
   182→        return "permanent_skip"
   183→    return None
   184→
   185→
   186→@dataclass
   187→class _LedgerParseResult:
   188→    """Combined output of a single pass over the Coverage Ledger section."""
   189→
   190→    hits: Counter[str]
   191→    dispositions: list[ReflectDisposition]
   192→    found_section: bool
   193→
   194→
   195→def _iter_coverage_ledger_lines(report: str) -> tuple[bool, list[str]]:
   196→    found_section = False
   197→    in_ledger = False
   198→    lines: list[str] = []
   199→    for raw_line in report.splitlines():
   200→        line = raw_line.strip()
   201→        if re.fullmatch(r"##\s+Coverage Ledger", line, re.IGNORECASE):
   202→            found_section = True
   203→            in_ledger = True
   204→            continue
   205→        if in_ledger and re.match(r"##\s+", line):
   206→            break
   207→        if in_ledger:
   208→            lines.append(line)
   209→    return found_section, lines
   210→
   211→
   212→def _resolve_ledger_issue_id(
   213→    *,
   214→    token: str,
   215→    line: str,
   216→    valid_ids: set[str],
   217→    maps: _IdResolutionMaps,
   218→    short_id_usage: Counter[str],
   219→) -> str | None:
   220→    issue_id = _resolve_token_to_id(token, valid_ids, maps, short_id_usage)
   221→    if issue_id:
   222→        return issue_id
   223→    for hex_token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", line):
   224→        resolved = maps.short_hex_map.get(hex_token)
   225→        if resolved:
   226→            return resolved
   227→    return None
   228→
   229→
   230→def _append_disposition_if_supported(
   231→    dispositions: list[ReflectDisposition],
   232→    *,
   233→    issue_id: str,
   234→    decision: str | None,
   235→    target: str | None,
   236→) -> None:
   237→    parsed_decision = _decision_kind_or_none(decision)
   238→    if parsed_decision is None or not target:
   239→        return
   240→    dispositions.append(
   241→        ReflectDisposition(
   242→            issue_id=issue_id,
   243→            decision=parsed_decision,
   244→            target=target,
   245→        )
   246→    )
   247→
   248→
   249→def _walk_coverage_ledger(
   250→    report: str,
   251→    valid_ids: set[str],
   252→) -> _LedgerParseResult:
   253→    maps = _build_id_resolution_maps(valid_ids)
   254→    hits: Counter[str] = Counter()
   255→    dispositions: list[ReflectDisposition] = []
   256→    short_id_usage: Counter[str] = Counter()
   257→    found_section, ledger_lines = _iter_coverage_ledger_lines(report)
   258→
   259→    for line in ledger_lines:
   260→        token, decision, target = _extract_ledger_entry(line)
   261→        if not token:
   262→            continue
   263→
   264→        issue_id = _resolve_ledger_issue_id(
   265→            token=token,
   266→            line=line,
   267→            valid_ids=valid_ids,
   268→            maps=maps,
   269→            short_id_usage=short_id_usage,
   270→        )
   271→        if not issue_id:
   272→            continue
   273→        hits[issue_id] += 1
   274→        _append_disposition_if_supported(
   275→            dispositions,
   276→            issue_id=issue_id,
   277→            decision=decision,
   278→            target=target,
   279→        )
   280→
   281→    return _LedgerParseResult(
   282→        hits=hits,
   283→        dispositions=dispositions,
   284→        found_section=found_section,
   285→    )
   286→
   287→
   288→def parse_reflect_dispositions(
   289→    report: str,
   290→    valid_ids: set[str],
   291→) -> list[ReflectDisposition]:
   292→    """Parse structured dispositions from the Coverage Ledger section."""
   293→    return _walk_coverage_ledger(report, valid_ids).dispositions
   294→
   295→
   296→def analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   297→    *,
   298→    report: str,
   299→    valid_ids: set[str],
   300→) -> tuple[set[str], list[str], list[str]]:
   301→    """Return cited, missing, and duplicate issue IDs referenced by reflect."""
   302→    result = _walk_coverage_ledger(report, valid_ids)
   303→    if result.found_section and result.hits:
   304→        cited = set(result.hits)
   305→        duplicates = sorted(issue_id for issue_id, count in result.hits.items() if count > 1)
   306→        missing = sorted(valid_ids - cited)
   307→        return cited, missing, duplicates
   308→
   309→    maps = _build_id_resolution_maps(valid_ids)
   310→    cited = extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
   311→    for issue_id in valid_ids:
   312→        if issue_id in report:
   313→            cited.add(issue_id)
   314→
   315→    short_hits = _collect_legacy_short_hex_hits(report, maps)
   316→    cited.update(short_hits)
   317→
   318→    duplicates = sorted(issue_id for issue_id, count in short_hits.items() if count > 1)
   319→    missing = sorted(valid_ids - cited)
   320→    return cited, missing, duplicates
   321→
   322→
   323→def _collect_legacy_short_hex_hits(
   324→    report: str,
   325→    maps: _IdResolutionMaps,
   326→) -> Counter[str]:
   327→    short_hits: Counter[str] = Counter()
   328→    for token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", report):
   329→        resolved = maps.short_hex_map.get(token)
   330→        if resolved:
   331→            short_hits[resolved] += 1
   332→    return short_hits
   333→
   334→
   335→def validate_reflect_accounting(
   336→    *,
   337→    report: str,
   338→    valid_ids: set[str],
   339→) -> tuple[bool, set[str], list[str], list[str]]:
   340→    """Require the reflect report to account for each open issue exactly once."""
   341→    cited, missing, duplicates = analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   342→        report=report,
   343→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
   344→    )
   345→    if not missing and not duplicates:
   346→        return True, cited, missing, duplicates
   347→
   348→    print(
   349→        colorize(
   350→            "  Reflect report must account for every open review issue exactly once.",
   351→            "red",
   352→        )
   353→    )
   354→    if missing:
   355→        missing_short = ", ".join(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in missing[:10])
   356→        print(colorize(f"    Missing: {missing_short}", "yellow"))
   357→    if duplicates:
   358→        duplicate_short = ", ".join(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in duplicates[:10])
   359→        print(colorize(f"    Duplicated: {duplicate_short}", "yellow"))
   360→    print(colorize("  Fix the reflect blueprint before running organize.", "dim"))
   361→    if missing:
   362→        print(colorize("  Expected format — include a ## Coverage Ledger section:", "dim"))
   363→        print(colorize('    - <hash> -> cluster "cluster-name"', "dim"))
   364→        print(colorize('    - <hash> -> skip "reason"', "dim"))
   365→        print(colorize("  Also accepted: bare hashes, colon-separated, comma-separated.", "dim"))
   366→    return False, cited, missing, duplicates
   367→
   368→
   369→__all__ = [
   370→    "ReflectDisposition",
   371→    "analyze_reflect_issue_accounting",
   372→    "parse_reflect_dispositions",
   373→    "validate_reflect_accounting",
   374→]
   375→
```

```
    34→def _required_issue_hashes(triage_input: TriageInput) -> list[str]:

    38→
    39→def _compact_issue_summary(triage_input: TriageInput) -> str:
    40→    """Return a compact issue summary for later triage stages."""
    41→    by_dim: Counter[str] = Counter()
    42→    for issue in triage_input.open_issues.values():
    43→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    44→        by_dim[str(detail.get("dimension", "unknown"))] += 1
    45→    dims = ", ".join(f"{name} ({count})" for name, count in sorted(by_dim.items()))
    46→    parts = [

    49→    ]
    50→    if dims:
    51→        parts.append(f"Open dimensions: {dims}")
    52→    if triage_input.new_since_last:
    53→        parts.append(f"New since last triage: {len(triage_input.new_since_last)}")
    54→    if triage_input.resolved_since_last:
    55→        parts.append(f"Resolved since last triage: {len(triage_input.resolved_since_last)}")
    56→
    57→    # Cluster summary if clusters exist
    58→    existing = triage_input.existing_clusters or {}
    59→    if existing:
    60→        parts.append("")
    61→        parts.append("## Clusters")
    62→        for cname, cdata in existing.items():
    63→            desc = cdata.get("description") or ""
    64→            count = len(cdata.get("issue_ids", []))
    65→            desc_str = f" ({desc})" if desc else ""
    66→            parts.append(f"- {cname}: {count} issues{desc_str}")
    67→
    68→    return "\n".join(parts)

    84→
    85→def _issue_context_for_stage(
    86→    stage: str,
    87→    triage_input: TriageInput,

    90→    """Return the amount of issue context appropriate for a stage."""

    93→        if stage == "reflect":

   100→                + ", ".join(short_ids)

   102→            parts.append(

   105→                "Allowed forms:\n"

   108→                "Do not mention hashes outside the `## Coverage Ledger` section.\n"
   109→                + "\n".join(f"- {short_id} -> TODO" for short_id in short_ids)
   110→            )
   111→        return "\n\n".join(parts)
   112→    summary = _compact_issue_summary(triage_input)
   113→    if mode == "self_record":

   117→
   118→def _format_assessments_table(assessments: list[dict]) -> str:
   119→    """Format structured observe assessments as a readable table for downstream stages."""
   120→    if not assessments:
   121→        return ""
   122→    lines = ["### Structured Observe Assessments\n"]
   123→    lines.append("| Hash | Verdict | Recommendation |")
   124→    lines.append("|------|---------|----------------|")
   125→    for a in assessments:
   126→        h = a.get("hash", "?")
   127→        v = a.get("verdict", "?")
   128→        r = a.get("recommendation", "")
   129→        # Truncate recommendation for table readability
   130→        if len(r) > 80:
   131→            r = r[:77] + "..."
   132→        lines.append(f"| {h} | {v} | {r} |")
   133→    return "\n".join(lines)
   134→
   135→
   136→def _format_assessment_file_evidence(assessments: list[dict]) -> str:
   137→    """Format file-level observe evidence for clustering-heavy downstream stages."""
   138→    if not assessments:
   139→        return ""
   140→    lines = ["### Observe File Evidence\n"]
   141→    for assessment in assessments:
   142→        issue_hash = str(assessment.get("hash", "?"))
   143→        files_read = [
   144→            str(path).strip()
   145→            for path in assessment.get("files_read", [])
   146→            if str(path).strip()
   147→        ]
   148→        verdict = str(assessment.get("verdict", "?")).strip() or "?"
   149→        reasoning = str(assessment.get("verdict_reasoning", "")).strip()
   150→        recommendation = str(assessment.get("recommendation", "")).strip()
   151→        lines.append(f"- {issue_hash}: {verdict}")
   152→        if files_read:
   153→            lines.append(f"  files_read: {', '.join(files_read)}")
   154→        if reasoning:
   155→            lines.append(f"  verdict_reasoning: {reasoning}")
   156→        if recommendation:
   157→            lines.append(f"  recommendation: {recommendation}")
   158→    return "\n".join(lines)
   159→
   160→
   161→def _relevant_prior_reports(
   162→    stage: str,
   163→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   164→    stages_data: dict | None = None,
   165→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   166→    """Return the stage reports that matter for the current stage."""
   167→    wanted = {
   168→        "reflect": ("observe",),
   169→        "organize": ("reflect",),
   170→        "enrich": ("organize",),
   171→        "sense-check": ("organize", "enrich"),
   172→    }.get(stage, tuple(prior_reports))
   173→    result = [(name, prior_reports[name]) for name in wanted if name in prior_reports]
   174→
   175→    # Append structured observe assessments for stages that need verdict data
   176→    if stage in {"reflect", "organize", "sense-check"} and stages_data:
   177→        observe_data = stages_data.get("observe", {})
   178→        assessments = observe_data.get("assessments", [])
   179→        if assessments:
   180→            table = _format_assessments_table(assessments)
   181→            if table:
   182→                result.append(("observe-assessments", table))
   183→        if stage == "organize":
   184→            evidence = _format_assessment_file_evidence(assessments)
   185→            if evidence:
   186→                result.append(("observe-evidence", evidence))
   187→
   188→    return result
   189→
   190→
   191→def build_stage_prompt(
   192→    stage: str,
   193→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   194→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   195→    *,
   196→    repo_root: Path,
   197→    mode: PromptMode = "self_record",
   198→    cli_command: str = "desloppify",
   199→    stages_data: dict | None = None,
   200→) -> str:
   201→    """Build a complete subagent prompt for a triage stage."""
   202→    parts: list[str] = []
   203→
   204→    # Preamble
   205→    parts.append(
   206→        triage_prompt_preamble(mode).format(
   207→            stage=stage.upper(),
   208→            repo_root=repo_root,
   209→            cli_command=cli_command,
   210→        )
   211→    )
   212→
   213→    # Prior stage reports
   214→    relevant_prior_reports = _relevant_prior_reports(stage, prior_reports, stages_data)
   215→    if relevant_prior_reports:
   216→        parts.append("## Prior Stage Reports\n")
   217→        for prior_stage, report in relevant_prior_reports:
   218→            parts.append(f"### {prior_stage.upper()} Report\n{report}\n")
   219→
   220→    # Issue data / summary
   221→    parts.append(_issue_context_for_stage(stage, triage_input, mode))
   222→
   223→    # Stage-specific instructions
   224→    instruction_fn = _STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS.get(stage)
   225→    if instruction_fn:
   226→        parts.append(instruction_fn(mode))
   227→
   228→    # CLI reference
   229→    if mode == "self_record":
   230→        parts.append(render_cli_reference(cli_command))
   231→
   232→    # Validation requirements
   233→    parts.append(_validation_requirements(stage))
   234→
   235→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   236→
   237→
   238→def cmd_stage_prompt(
   239→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   240→    *,
   241→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   242→) -> None:
   243→    """Print the current prompt for a triage stage, built from live plan data."""
   244→    stage = args.stage_prompt
   245→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   246→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   247→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   248→    state = runtime.state
   249→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   250→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   251→
   252→    # Extract real prior reports from plan.json
   253→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   254→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   255→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
   256→    for prior_stage in _STAGES:
   257→        if prior_stage == stage:
   258→            break
   259→        report = stages.get(prior_stage, {}).get("report", "")
   260→        if report:
   261→            prior_reports[prior_stage] = report
   262→
   263→    prompt = build_stage_prompt(stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root, stages_data=stages)
   264→    print(prompt)
   265→
   266→
```

```
    16→class SkipOptions:

    19→    kind: str = "temporary"

    26→
    27→def _skip_options(
    28→    *,
    29→    options: SkipOptions | None,
    30→    legacy_kwargs: dict[str, object],
    31→) -> SkipOptions:
    32→    """Resolve skip options from a config object or legacy kwargs."""

    35→        "reason",
    36→        "note",
    37→        "attestation",
    38→        "review_after",
    39→        "scan_count",
    40→    }
    41→    unknown = sorted(set(legacy_kwargs) - allowed_keys)
    42→    if unknown:
    43→        joined = ", ".join(unknown)
    44→        raise TypeError(f"Unexpected keyword argument(s): {joined}")
    45→    if options is not None and legacy_kwargs:
    46→        raise TypeError("Pass either options=... or legacy keyword args, not both")
    47→    if options is not None:
    48→        return options
    49→    kind = legacy_kwargs.get("kind")
    50→    reason = legacy_kwargs.get("reason")
    51→    note = legacy_kwargs.get("note")
    52→    attestation = legacy_kwargs.get("attestation")
    53→    review_after = legacy_kwargs.get("review_after")
    54→    scan_count = legacy_kwargs.get("scan_count")
    55→    return SkipOptions(
    56→        kind=str(kind) if kind is not None else "temporary",
    57→        reason=None if reason is None else str(reason),
    58→        note=None if note is None else str(note),
    59→        attestation=None if attestation is None else str(attestation),
    60→        review_after=int(review_after) if isinstance(review_after, int) else None,
    61→        scan_count=int(scan_count) if isinstance(scan_count, int) else 0,
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def skip_items(
    66→    plan: PlanModel,
    67→    issue_ids: list[str],
    68→    *,
    69→    options: SkipOptions | None = None,
    70→    **legacy_kwargs,
    71→) -> int:
    72→    """Move issue IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
    73→    skip_options = _skip_options(
    74→        options=options,
    75→        legacy_kwargs=legacy_kwargs,
    76→    )
    77→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    78→    now = utc_now()
    79→    count = 0
    80→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    81→    skip_set = set(issue_ids)
    82→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, skip_set)
    83→    for fid in issue_ids:
    84→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
    85→        skipped[fid] = {
    86→            "issue_id": fid,
    87→            "kind": skip_options.kind,
    88→            "reason": skip_options.reason,
    89→            "note": skip_options.note,
    90→            "attestation": skip_options.attestation,
    91→            "created_at": now,
    92→            "review_after": skip_options.review_after,
    93→            "skipped_at_scan": skip_options.scan_count,
    94→        }
    95→        count += 1
    96→    clear_focus_if_cluster_empty(plan)
    97→    return count
    98→
    99→
   100→def _is_protected_skip(entry: SkipEntry) -> bool:
   101→    """A skip is protected if it was permanent/false_positive AND has a note.
   102→
   103→    Protected skips represent deliberate human judgment and should not be
   104→    silently undone by bulk unskip operations.
   105→    """
   106→    kind = str(entry.get("kind", ""))
   107→    has_note = bool(entry.get("note", ""))
   108→    return kind in ("permanent", "false_positive") and has_note
   109→
   110→
   111→def unskip_items(
   112→    plan: PlanModel,
   113→    issue_ids: list[str],
   114→    *,
   115→    include_protected: bool = False,
   116→) -> tuple[int, list[str], list[str]]:
   117→    """Bring issue IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   118→
   119→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen, protected_ids_kept)``
   120→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   121→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller, and the third
   122→    list contains protected IDs that were NOT unskipped (unless
   123→    ``include_protected=True``).
   124→    """
   125→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   126→    count = 0
   127→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   128→    protected_kept: list[str] = []
   129→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   130→    for fid in issue_ids:
   131→        entry = skipped.get(fid)
   132→        if entry is None:
   133→            continue
   134→        if not include_protected and _is_protected_skip(entry):
   135→            protected_kept.append(fid)
   136→            continue
   137→        skipped.pop(fid)
   138→        if skip_kind_needs_state_reopen(str(entry.get("kind", ""))):
   139→            need_reopen.append(fid)
   140→        if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   141→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   142→        count += 1
   143→    return count, need_reopen, protected_kept
   144→
   145→
   146→def resurface_stale_skips(
   147→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   148→) -> list[str]:
   149→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   150→
   151→    Returns list of resurfaced issue IDs.
   152→    """
   153→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   154→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   155→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   156→    for fid in list(skipped):
   157→        entry = skipped[fid]
   158→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   159→            continue
   160→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   161→        if review_after is None:
   162→            continue
   163→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   164→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   165→            skipped.pop(fid)
   166→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   167→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   168→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   169→    return resurfaced
   170→
   171→
   172→def backlog_items(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   173→    """Remove from plan.skipped without adding to queue_order.
   174→
   175→    Issues stay open in state but are no longer plan-tracked.
   176→    Returns the IDs that were actually removed.
   177→    """
   178→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   179→    removed: list[str] = []
   180→    for fid in issue_ids:
   181→        if fid in plan["skipped"]:
   182→            plan["skipped"].pop(fid)
   183→            removed.append(fid)
   184→    return removed
   185→
   186→
   187→__all__ = ["backlog_items", "resurface_stale_skips", "skip_items", "unskip_items"]
   188→
```

```
1→"""Dismissal helpers for cluster triage plan mutations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from .prompt import TriageResult
     6→
     7→
     8→def _triaged_out_payload(
     9→    *,
    10→    issue_id: str,
    11→    reason: str,
    12→    note: str | None,
    13→    now: str,
    14→    scan_count: int,
    15→) -> dict:
    16→    return {
    17→        "issue_id": issue_id,
    18→        "kind": "triaged_out",
    19→        "reason": reason,
    20→        "note": note,
    21→        "attestation": None,
    22→        "created_at": now,
    23→        "review_after": None,
    24→        "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def dismiss_triage_issues(
    29→    *,
    30→    triage: TriageResult,
    31→    order: list[str],
    32→    skipped: dict,
    33→    now: str,
    34→    version: int,
    35→    scan_count: int,
    36→) -> tuple[list[str], int]:
    37→    """Move triage-dismissed issues out of queue and into skipped metadata."""
    38→    dismissed_ids: list[str] = []
    39→    dismiss_count = 0
    40→    for dismissed in triage.dismissed_issues:
    41→        issue_id = dismissed.issue_id
    42→        dismissed_ids.append(issue_id)
    43→        if issue_id in order:
    44→            order.remove(issue_id)
    45→        skipped[issue_id] = _triaged_out_payload(
    46→            issue_id=issue_id,
    47→            reason=dismissed.reason,
    48→            note=f"Dismissed by epic triage v{version}",
    49→            now=now,
    50→            scan_count=scan_count,
    51→        )
    52→        dismiss_count += 1
    53→
    54→    for epic_data in triage.clusters:
    55→        for issue_id in epic_data.get("dismissed", []):
    56→            if issue_id in dismissed_ids or issue_id not in order:
    57→                continue
    58→            order.remove(issue_id)
    59→            dismissed_ids.append(issue_id)
    60→            skipped[issue_id] = _triaged_out_payload(
    61→                issue_id=issue_id,
    62→                reason=f"Dismissed by epic triage v{version}",
    63→                note=None,
    64→                now=now,
    65→                scan_count=scan_count,
    66→            )
    67→            dismiss_count += 1
    68→
    69→    return dismissed_ids, dismiss_count
    70→
    71→
    72→__all__ = ["dismiss_triage_issues"]
    73→
```

```
1→"""Completion helpers for finishing triage planning."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→

    29→
    30→def _normalize_summary_text(text: str | None) -> str:
    31→    return " ".join(str(text or "").split()).strip()
    32→
    33→
    34→def _truncate_summary_text(text: str, limit: int = 360) -> str:
    35→    if len(text) <= limit:
    36→        return text
    37→    return text[: limit - 3].rstrip() + "..."
    38→
    39→
    40→def _effective_completion_strategy_summary(
    41→    *,
    42→    completion_mode: str,
    43→    strategy: str,
    44→    existing_strategy: str,
    45→    completion_note: str,
    46→) -> str:
    47→    if strategy.lower() != "same":
    48→        return strategy
    49→    if completion_mode == "confirm_existing":
    50→        summary = (
    51→            "Reused the existing enriched cluster plan after re-review instead "
    52→            "of materializing a new reflect blueprint."
    53→        )
    54→        if completion_note:
    55→            summary += f" Reason: {completion_note}"
    56→        elif existing_strategy:
    57→            summary += " Prior execution sequencing remains in force."
    58→        return _truncate_summary_text(summary)
    59→    return existing_strategy
    60→
    61→
    62→def _sync_completion_meta(
    63→    *,
    64→    plan: PlanModel,
    65→    state: StateModel,
    66→    strategy: str,
    67→    completion_mode: str,
    68→    completion_note: str,
    69→    coverage_ids: set[str],
    70→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str]:
    71→    meta = ensure_triage_meta(plan)
    72→    if state.get("last_scan"):
    73→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
    74→    elif not meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash"):
    75→        meta.pop("issue_snapshot_hash", None)
    76→
    77→    normalized_strategy = _normalize_summary_text(strategy)
    78→    existing_strategy = _normalize_summary_text(meta.get("strategy_summary", ""))
    79→    normalized_note = _normalize_summary_text(completion_note)
    80→    effective_strategy_summary = _effective_completion_strategy_summary(
    81→        completion_mode=completion_mode,
    82→        strategy=normalized_strategy,
    83→        existing_strategy=existing_strategy,
    84→        completion_note=normalized_note,
    85→    )
    86→    meta["triaged_ids"] = sorted(coverage_ids)
    87→    if effective_strategy_summary:
    88→        meta["strategy_summary"] = effective_strategy_summary
    89→    meta["trigger"] = (
    90→        "confirm_existing" if completion_mode == "confirm_existing" else "manual_triage"
    91→    )
    92→    meta["last_completion_mode"] = completion_mode
    93→    if normalized_note:
    94→        meta["last_completion_note"] = normalized_note
    95→    else:
    96→        meta.pop("last_completion_note", None)
    97→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
    98→    return meta, effective_strategy_summary
    99→
   100→
   101→def _archive_and_clear_triage_stages(
   102→    meta: dict[str, Any],
   103→    *,
   104→    effective_strategy_summary: str,
   105→    existing_strategy: str,
   106→    completion_mode: str,
   107→    completion_note: str,
   108→) -> None:
   109→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   110→    normalized_note = _normalize_summary_text(completion_note)
   111→    if stages:
   112→        last_triage = {
   113→            "completed_at": utc_now(),
   114→            "stages": {key: dict(value) for key, value in stages.items()},
   115→            "strategy": effective_strategy_summary,
   116→            "completion_mode": completion_mode,
   117→        }
   118→        if completion_mode == "confirm_existing":
   119→            last_triage["reused_existing_plan"] = True
   120→            if normalized_note:
   121→                last_triage["completion_note"] = normalized_note
   122→            if existing_strategy and existing_strategy != effective_strategy_summary:
   123→                last_triage["previous_strategy_summary"] = existing_strategy
   124→        meta["last_triage"] = last_triage
   125→    meta["triage_stages"] = {}
   126→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   127→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   128→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   129→    clear_active_triage_issue_tracking(meta)
   130→
   131→
   132→def _print_completion_summary(
   133→    *,
   134→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster],
   135→    organized: int,
   136→    total: int,
   137→    completion_mode: str,
   138→    effective_strategy_summary: str,
   139→) -> None:
   140→    cluster_count = len([cluster for cluster in clusters.values() if cluster_issue_ids(cluster)])
   141→    print(colorize(f"  Triage complete: {organized}/{total} issues in {cluster_count} cluster(s).", "green"))
   142→    if completion_mode == "confirm_existing":
   143→        print(
   144→            colorize(
   145→                "  Completion mode: reused the current enriched cluster plan; "
   146→                "did not materialize a new reflect blueprint.",
   147→                "cyan",
   148→            )
   149→        )
   150→    if effective_strategy_summary:
   151→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {effective_strategy_summary}", "cyan"))
   152→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))
   153→
   154→
   155→def apply_completion(
   156→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   157→    plan: PlanModel,
   158→    strategy: str,
   159→    *,
   160→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   161→    completion_mode: str = "manual_triage",
   162→    completion_note: str = "",
   163→) -> None:
   164→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove triage stage IDs, save."""
   165→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   166→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   167→    state = runtime.state
   168→
   169→    coverage_ids = coverage_open_ids(plan, state)
   170→    organized, total, clusters = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=coverage_ids)
   171→
   172→    purge_ids(
   173→        plan,
   174→        [*TRIAGE_IDS, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID],
   175→    )
   176→    existing_strategy = _normalize_summary_text(
   177→        ensure_triage_meta(plan).get("strategy_summary", "")
   178→    )
   179→    meta, effective_strategy_summary = _sync_completion_meta(
   180→        plan=plan,
   181→        state=state,
   182→        strategy=strategy,
   183→        completion_mode=completion_mode,
   184→        completion_note=completion_note,
   185→        coverage_ids=coverage_ids,
   186→    )
   187→    _archive_and_clear_triage_stages(
   188→        meta,
   189→        effective_strategy_summary=effective_strategy_summary,
   190→        existing_strategy=existing_strategy,
   191→        completion_mode=completion_mode,
   192→        completion_note=completion_note,
   193→    )
   194→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   195→    _print_completion_summary(
   196→        clusters=clusters,
   197→        organized=organized,
   198→        total=total,
   199→        completion_mode=completion_mode,
   200→        effective_strategy_summary=effective_strategy_summary,
   201→    )
   202→
   203→
   204→def count_log_activity_since(plan: PlanModel, since: str) -> dict[str, int]:
   205→    """Count execution-log activity by action since a timestamp string."""
   206→    counts: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   207→    for raw_entry in ensure_execution_log(plan):
   208→        if "timestamp" not in raw_entry or "action" not in raw_entry:
   209→            continue
   210→        timestamp = raw_entry["timestamp"]
   211→        action = raw_entry["action"]
   212→        if not isinstance(timestamp, str) or not isinstance(action, str):
   213→            continue
   214→        if timestamp >= since:
   215→            counts[action] += 1
   216→    return dict(counts)
   217→
   218→
   219→__all__ = [
   220→    "apply_completion",
   221→    "count_log_activity_since",
   222→]
   223→
```

```
   100→def _require_cluster_update_activity(
   101→    *,
   102→    plan: dict,
   103→    state: dict,
   104→    stages: dict,
   105→    attestation: str | None,
   106→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
   107→) -> bool:
   108→    organize_ts = stages.get("organize", {}).get("timestamp", "")
   109→    if not organize_ts:
   110→        return True
   111→    activity = deps.count_log_activity_since(plan, organize_ts)
   112→    update_ops = activity.get("cluster_update", 0)
   113→    if update_ops != 0 or not open_review_ids_from_state(state):
   114→        return True
   115→    if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 40:
   116→        print(
   117→            deps.colorize(
   118→                "  Note: 0 cluster_update ops logged since organize. "
   119→                "Proceeding with attestation override.",
   120→                "yellow",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→        return True
   124→    print(
   125→        deps.colorize(
   126→            "  Cannot enrich: no cluster_update operations logged since organize.",
   127→            "red",
   128→        )
   129→    )
   130→    print(
   131→        deps.colorize(
   132→            "  Enriching steps requires running cluster update commands.\n"
   133→            '  e.g. desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "..."',
   134→            "dim",
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→    print(
   138→        deps.colorize(
   139→            '  Override: pass --attestation "reason why no update ops" (40+ chars).',
   140→            "dim",
   141→        )
   142→    )
   143→    return False
   144→
   145→
   146→def _print_underspecified_step_error(
   147→    underspec: list[tuple[str, int, int]],
   148→    *,
   149→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
   150→) -> None:
   151→    total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)
   152→    print(
   153→        deps.colorize(
   154→            f"  Cannot enrich: {total_bare} step(s) across {len(underspec)} cluster(s) lack detail or issue_refs:",
   155→            "red",
   156→        )
   157→    )
   158→    for name, bare, total in underspec:
   159→        print(deps.colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
   160→    print()
   161→    print(
   162→        deps.colorize(
   163→            "  Every step needs --detail (sub-points) or --issue-refs (for auto-completion).",
   164→            "dim",
   165→        )
   166→    )
   167→    print(deps.colorize("  Fix:", "dim"))
   168→    print(
   169→        deps.colorize(
   170→            '    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"',
   171→            "dim",
   172→        )
   173→    )
   174→    print(
   175→        deps.colorize(
   176→            "  You can also still reorganize: add/remove clusters, reorder, etc.",
   177→            "dim",
   178→        )
   179→    )
   180→
   181→
   182→def _print_enrich_warnings(
   183→    *,
   184→    report,
   185→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
   186→) -> None:
   187→    bad_paths = report.warning("bad_paths")
   188→    if bad_paths:
   189→        print(
   190→            deps.colorize(
   191→                f"  Warning: {bad_paths.total} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk:",
   192→                "yellow",
   193→            )
   194→        )
   195→        for name, step_num, paths in bad_paths.rows[:5]:
   196→            print(deps.colorize(f"    {name} step {step_num}: {', '.join(paths[:3])}", "yellow"))
   197→        print(
   198→            deps.colorize(
   199→                "  Fix paths before confirming enrich (confirmation will block on bad paths).",
   200→                "dim",
   201→            )
   202→        )
   203→
   204→    untagged = report.warning("missing_effort")
   205→    if untagged:
   206→        print(deps.colorize(f"  Note: {untagged.total} step(s) have no effort tag.", "yellow"))
   207→        print(
   208→            deps.colorize(
   209→                "  Consider: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --effort small",
   210→                "dim",
   211→            )
   212→        )
   213→
   214→
   215→def run_stage_enrich(
   216→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   217→    *,
   218→    services: TriageServices | None,
   219→    deps: EnrichStageDeps | None = None,
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Record the ENRICH stage with validation and optional auto-confirm."""
   222→    resolved_deps = deps or EnrichStageDeps()
   223→    resolved_services, plan, state, stages, report, attestation = _resolve_enrich_stage_context(
   224→        args=args,
   225→        services=services,
   226→        deps=resolved_deps,
   227→    )
   228→
   229→    if not resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   230→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to enrich.", "yellow"))
   231→        return
   232→
   233→    existing_stage = stages.get("enrich")
   234→    report, is_reuse = resolved_deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   235→
   236→    if not resolved_deps.require_organize_stage_for_enrich(stages):
   237→        return
   238→
   239→    if not _require_confirmed_organize_stage(
   240→        plan=plan,
   241→        stages=stages,
   242→        attestation=attestation,
   243→        services=resolved_services,
   244→        deps=resolved_deps,
   245→    ):
   246→        return
   247→
   248→    if not is_reuse and not _require_cluster_update_activity(
   249→        plan=plan,
   250→        state=state,
   251→        stages=stages,
   252→        attestation=attestation,
   253→        deps=resolved_deps,
   254→    ):
   255→        return
   256→
   257→    get_project_root = resolved_deps.get_project_root
   258→    if get_project_root is None:
   259→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
   260→
   261→    triage_ids = active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state) or None
   262→    quality_report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
   263→        plan,
   264→        get_project_root(),
   265→        phase_label="enrich",
   266→        bad_paths_severity="warning",
   267→        missing_effort_severity="warning",
   268→        include_missing_issue_refs=False,
   269→        include_vague_detail=False,
   270→        stale_issue_refs_severity=None,
   271→        triage_issue_ids=triage_ids,
   272→    )
   273→    underspec = quality_report.failure("underspecified")
   274→    total_bare = underspec.total if underspec else 0
   275→
   276→    if underspec:
   277→        _print_underspecified_step_error(underspec.rows, deps=resolved_deps)
   278→        return
   279→
   280→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  All steps have detail or issue_refs.", "green"))
   281→    _print_enrich_warnings(report=quality_report, deps=resolved_deps)
   282→
   283→    report = resolved_deps.enrich_report_or_error(report)
   284→    if report is None:
   285→        return
   286→
   287→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
   288→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   289→    cleared = resolved_deps.record_enrich_stage(
   290→        stages,
   291→        report=report,
   292→        shallow_count=total_bare,
   293→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   294→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   295→    )
   296→
   297→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   298→
   299→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   300→        plan,
   301→        "triage_enrich",
   302→        actor="user",
   303→        detail={"shallow_count": total_bare, "reuse": is_reuse},
   304→    )
   305→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   306→
   307→    print(
   308→        resolved_deps.colorize(
   309→            f"  Enrich stage recorded: {total_bare} step(s) still without detail.",
   310→            "green",
   311→        )
   312→    )
   313→    if is_reuse:
   314→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Enrich data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   315→        if cleared:
   316→            resolved_deps.print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   317→    else:
   318→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Now confirm the enrichment.", "yellow"))
   319→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   320→
   321→    resolved_deps.print_user_message(
   322→        "Enrich recorded. Before confirming — check the subagent's"
   323→        " work. Could a developer who has never seen this code"
   324→        " execute every step without asking a question? Every step"
   325→        " needs: file path, specific location, specific action."
   326→        " 'Refactor X' fails. 'Extract lines 45-89 into Y' passes."
   327→    )
   328→
   329→
   330→def cmd_stage_enrich(
   331→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   332→    *,
   333→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   334→) -> None:
   335→    """Public entrypoint for enrich stage recording."""
   336→    run_stage_enrich(args, services=services)
   337→
   338→
   339→__all__ = ["ColorizeFn", "EnrichStageDeps", "cmd_stage_enrich", "run_stage_enrich"]
   340→
```

```
1→"""Historical plan schema upgrades applied during load."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema.helpers import (
     8→    _cleanup_synthesis_meta,
     9→    _drop_legacy_plan_keys,
    10→    _has_synthesis_artifacts,
    11→    _migrate_action_steps_to_v8,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema.normalize import (
    14→    ensure_container_types,
    15→    normalize_cluster_defaults,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    18→
    19→V7_SCHEMA_VERSION = 7
    20→V8_SCHEMA_VERSION = 8
    21→
    22→
    23→def _rename_key(d: dict, old: str, new: str) -> bool:
    24→    if old not in d:
    25→        return False
    26→    d.setdefault(new, d.pop(old))
    27→    return True
    28→
    29→
    30→def migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    31→    """Migrate legacy deferred IDs into structured skip entries."""
    32→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
    33→    skipped: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = plan["skipped"]
    34→    if not deferred:
    35→        return
    36→
    37→    now = utc_now()
    38→    for issue_id in list(deferred):
    39→        if issue_id in skipped:
    40→            continue
    41→        skipped[issue_id] = {
    42→            "issue_id": issue_id,
    43→            "kind": "temporary",
    44→            "reason": None,
    45→            "note": None,
    46→            "attestation": None,
    47→            "created_at": now,
    48→            "review_after": None,
    49→            "skipped_at_scan": 0,
    50→        }
    51→    deferred.clear()
    52→
    53→
    54→def migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    55→    """Migrate v3 top-level ``epics`` dict into ``clusters`` (v4 unification)."""
    56→    epics = plan.pop("epics", None)
    57→    if not isinstance(epics, dict) or not epics:
    58→        return
    59→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
    60→    now = utc_now()
```

```
1→"""Sense-check stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→
    12→from .records import record_sense_check_stage, resolve_reusable_report
    13→from ..validation.enrich_quality import evaluate_enrich_quality
    14→from ..validation.enrich_checks import (
    15→    _steps_missing_issue_refs,
    16→    _steps_with_bad_paths,
    17→    _steps_with_vague_detail,
    18→    _steps_without_effort,
    19→    _underspecified_steps,
    20→)

    23→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    24→from .enrich import ColorizeFn
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    28→class SenseCheckStageDeps:
    29→    has_triage_in_queue: Callable[[dict], bool] = has_triage_in_queue
    30→    resolve_reusable_report: Callable[[str | None, dict | None], tuple[str | None, bool]] = (
    31→        resolve_reusable_report
    32→    )
    33→    record_sense_check_stage: Callable[..., list[str]] = record_sense_check_stage
    34→    colorize: ColorizeFn = colorize
    35→    default_triage_services: Callable[[], TriageServices] = default_triage_services
    36→    print_cascade_clear_feedback: Callable[[list[str], dict], None] = print_cascade_clear_feedback
    37→    get_project_root: Callable[[], Path] | None = None
    38→    underspecified_steps: Callable[[dict], list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = _underspecified_steps
    39→    steps_missing_issue_refs: Callable[[dict], list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = _steps_missing_issue_refs
    40→    steps_with_bad_paths: Callable[[dict, Path], list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]] = _steps_with_bad_paths
    41→    steps_with_vague_detail: Callable[[dict, Path], list[tuple[str, int, str]]] = _steps_with_vague_detail
    42→    steps_without_effort: Callable[[dict], list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = _steps_without_effort
    43→
    44→
    45→def _sense_check_report(
    46→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    47→    stages: dict,
    48→    *,
    49→    deps: SenseCheckStageDeps,
    50→) -> tuple[str | None, bool, dict | None]:
    51→    existing_stage = stages.get("sense-check")
    52→    report = getattr(args, "report", None)
    53→    report, is_reuse = deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    54→    return report, is_reuse, existing_stage
    55→
    56→
    57→def _sense_check_quality_problems(
    58→    plan: dict,
    59→    state: dict,
    60→    *,
    61→    deps: SenseCheckStageDeps,
    62→) -> list[str]:
    63→    get_project_root = deps.get_project_root
    64→    if get_project_root is None:
    65→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    66→
    67→    repo_root = get_project_root()
    68→    triage_ids = active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state) or None
    69→    quality_report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
    70→        plan,
    71→        repo_root,
    72→        phase_label="sense-check",
    73→        bad_paths_severity="warning",
    74→        missing_effort_severity="failure",
    75→        include_missing_issue_refs=True,
    76→        include_vague_detail=True,
    77→        stale_issue_refs_severity=None,
    78→        triage_issue_ids=triage_ids,
    79→    )
    80→    return [issue.message for issue in quality_report.failures]
    81→
    82→
    83→def _print_sense_check_problems(
    84→    problems: list[str],
    85→    *,
    86→    colorize_fn: ColorizeFn,
    87→) -> None:
    88→    print(colorize_fn("  Cannot record sense-check — plan still has issues:", "red"))
    89→    for problem in problems:
    90→        print(colorize_fn(f"    • {problem}", "yellow"))
    91→    print(colorize_fn("  Fix these before recording the sense-check stage.", "dim"))
    92→
    93→
    94→def _report_length_ok(report: str | None, *, colorize_fn: ColorizeFn) -> bool:
    95→    if not report:
    96→        print(colorize_fn("  --report is required for --stage sense-check.", "red"))
    97→        print(
    98→            colorize_fn(
    99→                "  Describe what the content and structure subagents found and fixed.",
   100→                "dim",
   101→            )
   102→        )
   103→        return False
   104→    if len(report) < 100:
   105→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   106→        return False
   107→    return True
   108→
   109→
   110→def _sense_check_evidence_failures(
   111→    report: str,
   112→    *,
   113→    plan: dict,
   114→    state: dict,
   115→) -> tuple[list[object], list[object]]:
   116→    from .evidence_parsing import (
   117→        validate_report_has_file_paths,
   118→        validate_report_references_clusters,
   119→    )
   120→    from ..review_coverage import manual_clusters_with_issues
   121→
   122→    failures: list[object] = []
   123→    if open_review_ids_from_state(state):
   124→        failures.extend(validate_report_has_file_paths(report) or [])
   125→
   126→    cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   127→    if cluster_names:
   128→        failures.extend(validate_report_references_clusters(report, cluster_names) or [])
   129→
   130→    blocking = [failure for failure in failures if failure.blocking]
   131→    advisory = [failure for failure in failures if not failure.blocking]
   132→    return blocking, advisory
   133→
   134→
   135→def _print_evidence_failures(
   136→    failures: list[object],
   137→    *,
   138→    stage_label: str,
   139→    colorize_fn: ColorizeFn,
   140→    style: str,
   141→) -> None:
   142→    from .evidence_parsing import format_evidence_failures
   143→
   144→    if not failures:
   145→        return
   146→    print(colorize_fn(format_evidence_failures(failures, stage_label=stage_label), style))
   147→
   148→
   149→def run_stage_sense_check(
   150→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   151→    *,
   152→    services: TriageServices | None,
   153→    deps: SenseCheckStageDeps | None = None,
   154→) -> None:
   155→    """Record the SENSE-CHECK stage after rerunning enrich-level validations."""
   156→    resolved_deps = deps or SenseCheckStageDeps()
   157→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   158→
   159→    resolved_services = services or resolved_deps.default_triage_services()
   160→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   161→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
   162→
   163→    if not resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   164→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to sense-check.", "yellow"))
   165→        return
   166→
   167→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   168→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   169→    report, is_reuse, existing_stage = _sense_check_report(
   170→        args,
   171→        stages,
   172→        deps=resolved_deps,
   173→    )
   174→
   175→    if not stages.get("enrich", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
   176→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Cannot sense-check: enrich stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   177→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   178→        return
   179→
   180→    problems = _sense_check_quality_problems(plan, state, deps=resolved_deps)
   181→    if problems:
   182→        _print_sense_check_problems(problems, colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize)
   183→        return
   184→
   185→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  All enrich-level checks pass after sense-check.", "green"))
   186→
   187→    if not _report_length_ok(report, colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize):
   188→        return
   189→
   190→    blocking_ev, advisory_ev = _sense_check_evidence_failures(report, plan=plan, state=state)
   191→    if blocking_ev:
   192→        _print_evidence_failures(
   193→            blocking_ev,
   194→            stage_label="sense-check",
   195→            colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize,
   196→            style="red",
   197→        )
   198→        return
   199→    if advisory_ev:
   200→        _print_evidence_failures(
   201→            advisory_ev,
   202→            stage_label="sense-check",
   203→            colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize,
   204→            style="yellow",
   205→        )
   206→
   207→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   208→    cleared = resolved_deps.record_sense_check_stage(
   209→        stages,
   210→        report=report,
   211→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   212→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   213→    )
   214→
   215→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   216→
   217→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   218→        plan,
   219→        "triage_sense_check",
   220→        actor="user",
   221→        detail={"reuse": is_reuse},
   222→    )
   223→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   224→
   225→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Sense-check stage recorded.", "green"))
   226→    if is_reuse:
   227→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Sense-check data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   228→        if cleared:
   229→            resolved_deps.print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   230→    else:
   231→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Now confirm the sense-check.", "yellow"))
   232→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm sense-check", "dim"))
   233→
   234→
   235→def cmd_stage_sense_check(
   236→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   237→    *,
   238→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   239→) -> None:
   240→    """Public entrypoint for sense-check stage recording."""
   241→    run_stage_sense_check(args, services=services)
   242→
   243→
```

```
    71→def ensure_triage_started(
    72→    plan: PlanModel,
    73→    *,
    74→    services: TriageServices,
    75→    state: StateModel | None = None,
    76→    attestation: str | None = None,
    77→    log_action: str | None = None,
    78→    log_actor: str = "system",
    79→    log_detail: Mapping[str, object] | None = None,
    80→    start_message: str | None = None,
    81→    start_message_style: str = "cyan",
    82→    deps: TriageLifecycleDeps | None = None,
    83→) -> TriageStartOutcome:
    84→    """Ensure triage stages are injected or return a blocked/already-active outcome."""
    85→    resolved_deps = deps or TriageLifecycleDeps()
    86→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    87→
    88→    if resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    89→        if state is not None:
    90→            ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
    91→            services.save_plan(plan)
    92→        meta.pop("triage_start_blocked", None)
    93→        return TriageStartOutcome(status="already_active", reason="already_active")
    94→
    95→    decision = resolved_deps.decide_triage_start(
    96→        plan,
    97→        state,
    98→        explicit_start=True,
    99→        attested_override=bool(attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 30),
   100→    )
   101→    if decision.action == "defer":
   102→        meta["triage_start_blocked"] = decision.reason
   103→        services.save_plan(plan)
   104→        _print_triage_start_block(decision.reason, deps=resolved_deps)
   105→        return TriageStartOutcome(status="blocked", reason=decision.reason)
   106→
   107→    resolved_deps.inject_triage_stages(plan)
   108→    meta.pop("triage_start_blocked", None)
   109→    meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   110→    if state is not None:
   111→        ensure_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   112→
   113→    if log_action:
   114→        detail = dict(log_detail or {})
   115→        detail.setdefault("injected_stage_ids", list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS))
   116→        services.append_log_entry(
   117→            plan,
   118→            log_action,
   119→            actor=log_actor,
   120→            detail=detail,
   121→        )
   122→
   123→    services.save_plan(plan)
   124→    if start_message:
   125→        print(
   126→            resolved_deps.colorize(
   127→                start_message,
   128→                start_message_style,
   129→            )
   130→        )
   131→    return TriageStartOutcome(status="started", reason=decision.reason)
   132→
   133→
   134→__all__ = [
   135→    "TriageLifecycleDeps",
   136→    "TriageStartOutcome",
```

```
1→"""Stage-gate and coverage helpers for triage command handlers."""

     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize

    10→
    11→def _require_triage_pending(plan: dict, *, action: str) -> bool:
    12→    """Require at least one triage stage ID to be present in queue for an action."""
    13→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    14→    if order & TRIAGE_IDS:
    15→        return True
    16→    print(colorize(f"  No triage stage in the queue — nothing to {action}.", "yellow"))
    17→    return False
    18→
    19→
    20→def _validate_stage_report(
    21→    report: str | None,
    22→    *,
    23→    stage: str,
    24→    min_chars: int,
    25→    missing_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    26→    short_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    27→) -> str | None:
    28→    """Validate staged report presence/length and print consistent guidance."""
    29→    if not report:
    30→        print(colorize(f"  --report is required for --stage {stage}.", "red"))
    31→        for line in missing_guidance or []:
    32→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    33→        return None
    34→    cleaned = report.strip()
    35→    if len(cleaned) < min_chars:
    36→        print(
    37→            colorize(
    38→                f"  Report too short: {len(cleaned)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).",
    39→                "red",
    40→            )
    41→        )
    42→        for line in short_guidance or []:
    43→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    44→        return None
    45→    return cleaned
    46→
    47→
    48→def unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    49→    """Return clusters with issues that are missing required enrichment.
    50→
    51→    Requirements:
    52→    - Every cluster needs a description and at least one action_step.
    53→    - Small clusters (< 5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue,
    54→      so each item has a concrete plan. Large clusters (>= 5) just need
    55→      steps overall (cluster-level plan is sufficient).
    56→    """
    57→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    58→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    59→        issue_ids = cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    60→        if not issue_ids:
    61→            continue
    62→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    63→            continue
    64→        missing: list[str] = []
    65→        if not cluster.get("description"):
    66→            missing.append("description")
    67→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
    68→        issue_count = len(issue_ids)
    69→        if not steps:
    70→            missing.append("action_steps")
    71→        elif issue_count < 5 and len(steps) < issue_count:
    72→            missing.append(
    73→                f"action_steps (have {len(steps)}, need >= {issue_count} for small cluster)"
    74→            )
    75→        if missing:
    76→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    77→    return gaps
    78→
    79→
    80→def unclustered_review_issues(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> list[str]:
    81→    """Return review issue IDs that aren't in any manual cluster.
    82→
    83→    When *state* is provided, uses open review/concerns issues from state
    84→    (the canonical source). Falls back to scanning queue_order for backwards
    85→    compatibility.
    86→    """
    87→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    88→    clustered_ids: set[str] = set()
    89→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    90→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    91→            clustered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    92→    skipped_ids = {
    93→        fid for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}).keys() if isinstance(fid, str)
    94→    }
    95→
    96→    if state is not None:
    97→        review_ids = [
    98→            fid for fid, finding in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    99→            if finding.get("status") == "open"
   100→            and finding.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   101→        ]
   102→        frozen_ids = (plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}) or {}).get("active_triage_issue_ids")
   103→        if isinstance(frozen_ids, list) and frozen_ids:
   104→            frozen_id_set = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   105→            review_ids = [fid for fid in review_ids if fid in frozen_id_set]
   106→    else:
   107→        review_ids = [
   108→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   109→            if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
   110→            and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   111→        ]
   112→
   113→    return [
   114→        fid for fid in review_ids
   115→        if fid not in clustered_ids and fid not in skipped_ids
   116→    ]
   117→
```
